===== backend/app/ai/detector.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict, List

import numpy as np

from app.config import settings
from app.services.yolo_service import Detection, YOLOService


class VehicleDetector:
    """Compatibility wrapper for code that still imports VehicleDetector."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._service = YOLOService(
            model_path=settings.YOLO_MODEL_PATH,
            confidence=settings.YOLO_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD,
            iou=settings.YOLO_IOU_THRESHOLD,
        )

    @property
    def model(self) -> YOLOService:
        return self._service

    def load_model(self, path: str) -> None:
        self._service = YOLOService(
            model_path=path,
            confidence=settings.YOLO_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD,
            iou=settings.YOLO_IOU_THRESHOLD,
        )
        self._service.load_model()

    def detect_vehicles(self, frame: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        return [item.to_dict() for item in self._service.detect(frame)]

    def draw_detections(
        self,
        frame: np.ndarray,
        detections: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        typed_detections = [
            Detection(
                bbox=[float(value) for value in item["bbox"]],
                class_name=str(item["class_name"]),
                confidence=float(item["confidence"]),
                track_id=int(item.get("track_id", -1)),
            )
            for item in detections
        ]
        return self._service.draw_detections(frame, typed_detections)


===== backend/app/ai/slot_mapper.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Sequence

import cv2
import numpy as np


class SlotMapper:
    """Maps vehicle bounding boxes to fixed parking-space polygons."""

    def __init__(self, overlap_threshold: float = 0.20) -> None:
        self.slots: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.reference_width = 1280
        self.reference_height = 720
        self.overlap_threshold = overlap_threshold

    @classmethod
    def from_layout_file(
        cls,
        path: str | Path,
        overlap_threshold: float = 0.20,
    ) -> "SlotMapper":
        with Path(path).open("r", encoding="utf-8") as layout_file:
            layout = json.load(layout_file)

        mapper = cls(overlap_threshold=overlap_threshold)
        mapper.define_slots(
            layout["slots"],
            reference_width=int(layout.get("reference_width", 1280)),
            reference_height=int(layout.get("reference_height", 720)),
        )
        return mapper

    def define_slots(
        self,
        slot_regions: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        reference_width: int = 1280,
        reference_height: int = 720,
    ) -> None:
        validated: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        for slot in slot_regions:
            slot_id = str(slot.get("id", "")).strip()
            points = slot.get("points")

            if not slot_id or not isinstance(points, list) or len(points) < 3:
                raise ValueError("Every slot requires an id and at least 3 points")

            validated.append({"id": slot_id, "points": points})

        self.slots = validated
        self.reference_width = reference_width
        self.reference_height = reference_height

    def slot_ids(self) -> List[str]:
        return [str(slot["id"]) for slot in self.slots]

    def map_detections_to_slots(
        self,
        detections: Iterable[Any],
        frame_shape: Sequence[int] | None = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, bool]:
        height, width = self._frame_size(frame_shape)
        result = {str(slot["id"]): False for slot in self.slots}
        slot_geometry = [
            (
                str(slot["id"]),
                self._scaled_polygon(slot["points"], width, height),
            )
            for slot in self.slots
        ]

        # Assign each detected vehicle to only its strongest matching space.
        # This prevents one car that crosses a back-to-back boundary from
        # marking both neighbouring spaces occupied.
        for detection in detections:
            bbox = self._detection_bbox(detection)
            if bbox is None:
                continue

            x1, y1, x2, y2 = bbox
            center = ((x1 + x2) / 2.0, (y1 + y2) / 2.0)
            box_polygon = np.array(
                [[x1, y1], [x2, y1], [x2, y2], [x1, y2]],
                dtype=np.float32,
            )
            box_area = max((x2 - x1) * (y2 - y1), 1.0)
            best_slot_id: str | None = None
            best_score = -1.0

            for slot_id, slot_polygon in slot_geometry:
                slot_area = max(float(cv2.contourArea(slot_polygon)), 1.0)
                intersection_area, _ = cv2.intersectConvexConvex(
                    slot_polygon.astype(np.float32),
                    box_polygon,
                )
                overlap = float(intersection_area) / min(slot_area, box_area)
                center_inside = (
                    cv2.pointPolygonTest(slot_polygon, center, False) >= 0
                )

                if not center_inside and overlap < self.overlap_threshold:
                    continue

                # Prefer a polygon containing the vehicle centre, then use
                # overlap to resolve boundary cases.
                score = overlap + (1.0 if center_inside else 0.0)
                if score > best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best_slot_id = slot_id

            if best_slot_id is not None:
                result[best_slot_id] = True

        return result

    def draw_slots(
        self,
        frame: np.ndarray,
        occupancy: Dict[str, bool],
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        output = frame.copy()
        overlay = output.copy()
        height, width = output.shape[:2]

        for slot in self.slots:
            slot_id = str(slot["id"])
            polygon = self._scaled_polygon(slot["points"], width, height)
            occupied = occupancy.get(slot_id, False)
            color = (0, 0, 255) if occupied else (0, 190, 0)

            cv2.fillPoly(overlay, [polygon], color)
            cv2.polylines(output, [polygon], True, color, 2, cv2.LINE_AA)

            center = polygon.mean(axis=0).astype(int)
            label = f"{slot_id} {'OCCUPIED' if occupied else 'VACANT'}"
            cv2.putText(
                output,
                label,
                (max(0, center[0] - 40), center[1]),
                cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
                0.38,
                (255, 255, 255),
                1,
                cv2.LINE_AA,
            )

        cv2.addWeighted(overlay, 0.14, output, 0.86, 0, output)
        return output

    def _frame_size(
        self,
        frame_shape: Sequence[int] | None,
    ) -> tuple[int, int]:
        if frame_shape is None:
            return self.reference_height, self.reference_width
        return int(frame_shape[0]), int(frame_shape[1])

    def _scaled_polygon(
        self,
        points: List[List[float]],
        width: int,
        height: int,
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        scale_x = width / self.reference_width
        scale_y = height / self.reference_height
        return np.array(
            [[round(x * scale_x), round(y * scale_y)] for x, y in points],
            dtype=np.int32,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _detection_bbox(detection: Any) -> tuple[float, float, float, float] | None:
        bbox = (
            detection.get("bbox")
            if isinstance(detection, dict)
            else getattr(detection, "bbox", None)
        )
        if bbox is None or len(bbox) != 4:
            return None

        x1, y1, x2, y2 = (float(value) for value in bbox)
        if x2 <= x1 or y2 <= y1:
            return None
        return x1, y1, x2, y2


===== backend/app/config.py =====
from typing import List

from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    """Application settings."""

    DATABASE_URL: str = (
        "postgresql+asyncpg://parking_admin:"
        "parking_secret_2024@localhost:5432/smart_parking"
    )

    REDIS_URL: str = "redis://localhost:6379"

    SECRET_KEY: str = "supersecretkey"
    ALGORITHM: str = "HS256"
    ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES: int = 100
    REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS: int = 50

    YOLO_MODEL_PATH: str = "yolo11n.pt"
    YOLO_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD: float = 0.5
    YOLO_IOU_THRESHOLD: float = 0.45

    # Recorded-video detection settings. The model is loaded lazily so a YOLO
    # problem never prevents authentication, reservations, or payments from
    # starting.
    YOLO_VIDEO_SAMPLE_FPS: float = 2.0
    YOLO_VIDEO_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD: float = 0.30
    YOLO_VIDEO_INFERENCE_SIZE: int = 960
    YOLO_OCCUPANCY_CONFIRMATIONS: int = 3
    YOLO_SLOT_OVERLAP_THRESHOLD: float = 0.20
    YOLO_MAX_VIDEO_UPLOAD_MB: int = 1024

    # Payment verification remains responsible for releasing an occupied slot.
    # Keep this false unless the reservation/payment rules are changed later.
    YOLO_ALLOW_AUTOMATIC_VACATE: bool = False

    RESERVATION_TIMEOUT_MINUTES: int = 30

    CORS_ORIGINS: List[str] = ["*"]

    FIREBASE_CREDENTIALS_PATH: str | None = None

    # Razorpay Test/Live credentials.
    # Real values must only be stored in backend/.env.
    RAZORPAY_KEY_ID: str = ""
    RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET: str = ""
    RAZORPAY_CURRENCY: str = "INR"

    # Razorpay requires the order amount in paise.
    RAZORPAY_MINIMUM_AMOUNT_PAISE: int = 100

    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
        env_file=".env",
        env_file_encoding="utf-8",
        extra="ignore",
    )


settings = Settings()

===== backend/app/database.py =====
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import create_async_engine, AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.orm import declarative_base
from app.config import settings

engine = create_async_engine(settings.DATABASE_URL, echo=False)
AsyncSessionLocal = async_sessionmaker(engine, class_=AsyncSession, expire_on_commit=False)

Base = declarative_base()

async def get_db():
    """Dependency for getting async database session."""
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
        yield session

async def init_db():
    """Initialize database tables."""
    async with engine.begin() as conn:
        await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)


===== backend/app/main.py =====
from fastapi import FastAPI
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
import logging
import asyncio

from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal, init_db
from app.utils.redis_client import redis_client
from app.config import settings
from app.middleware.cors import setup_cors
from app.routers import auth_router, parking_router, reservation_router, admin_router, notification_router, payment_router, websocket_router, detection_router
from app.services.reservation_service import expire_pending_reservations

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


async def reservation_expiry_worker():
    while True:
        try:
            async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
                await expire_pending_reservations(db)
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            raise
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("Failed to expire pending reservations")
        await asyncio.sleep(30)

@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    logger.info("Initializing database...")
    await init_db()
    
    logger.info("Connecting to Redis...")
    await redis_client.connect(settings.REDIS_URL)

    expiry_task = asyncio.create_task(reservation_expiry_worker())
    
    logger.info("Application startup complete.")
    yield

    expiry_task.cancel()
    try:
        await expiry_task
    except asyncio.CancelledError:
        pass

    logger.info("Application shutdown.")

app = FastAPI(title="AI Smart Parking System API", lifespan=lifespan)

setup_cors(app)

app.include_router(auth_router.router)
app.include_router(parking_router.router)
app.include_router(reservation_router.router)
app.include_router(admin_router.router)
app.include_router(notification_router.router)
app.include_router(payment_router.router)
app.include_router(websocket_router.router)
app.include_router(detection_router.router)

@app.get("/")
async def root():
    return {"message": "Welcome to AI Smart Parking System API"}

@app.get("/health")
async def health_check():
    return {"status": "ok"}

===== backend/app/middleware/cors.py =====
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from app.config import settings

def setup_cors(app: FastAPI):
    allow_credentials = "*" not in settings.CORS_ORIGINS
    app.add_middleware(
        CORSMiddleware,
        allow_origins=settings.CORS_ORIGINS,
        allow_credentials=allow_credentials,
        allow_methods=["*"],
        allow_headers=["*"],
    )

===== backend/app/models/camera.py =====
import uuid
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Integer, Boolean, DateTime, Text, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.database import Base

class Camera(Base):
    """Camera model."""
    __tablename__ = "cameras"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    name = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    stream_url = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    camera_type = Column(String(20), default="IP")
    parking_area_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("parking_areas.id"))
    floor = Column(Integer, nullable=True)
    status = Column(String(20), default="OFFLINE")
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    parking_area = relationship("ParkingArea", back_populates="cameras")


===== backend/app/models/notification.py =====
import uuid
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Boolean, DateTime, Text, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID, JSONB
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.database import Base

class Notification(Base):
    """Notification model."""
    __tablename__ = "notifications"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"))
    title = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    body = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    type = Column(String(30), nullable=True)
    is_read = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    data = Column(JSONB, nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    user = relationship("User", back_populates="notifications")


===== backend/app/models/parking_area.py =====
import uuid
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Integer, Float, Boolean, DateTime, Time, Numeric, Text
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.database import Base
from datetime import time

class ParkingArea(Base):
    """ParkingArea model."""
    __tablename__ = "parking_areas"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    name = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    address = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    latitude = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    longitude = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    total_floors = Column(Integer, default=1)
    total_slots = Column(Integer, default=0)
    hourly_rate = Column(Numeric(10, 2), nullable=False)
    currency = Column(String(3), default="INR")
    image_url = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    opening_time = Column(Time, default=time(6, 0))
    closing_time = Column(Time, default=time(23, 0))
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())

    slots = relationship("ParkingSlot", back_populates="parking_area")
    cameras = relationship("Camera", back_populates="parking_area")
    reservations = relationship("Reservation", back_populates="parking_area")


===== backend/app/models/parking_settlement.py =====
import uuid

from sqlalchemy import Column, DateTime, ForeignKey, Numeric, String
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func

from app.database import Base


class ParkingSettlement(Base):
    """Final parking charge after the vehicle checks out."""

    __tablename__ = "parking_settlements"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    reservation_id = Column(
        UUID(as_uuid=True),
        ForeignKey("reservations.id"),
        nullable=False,
        unique=True,
        index=True,
    )
    user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False)
    total_amount = Column(Numeric(10, 2), nullable=False)
    advance_amount = Column(Numeric(10, 2), nullable=False)
    amount_due = Column(Numeric(10, 2), nullable=False)
    currency = Column(String(3), default="INR", nullable=False)
    status = Column(String(20), default="PENDING", nullable=False)
    razorpay_order_id = Column(String(255), nullable=True, unique=True)
    razorpay_payment_id = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        onupdate=func.now(),
    )


===== backend/app/models/parking_slot.py =====
import uuid
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Integer, DateTime, UniqueConstraint, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.database import Base

class ParkingSlot(Base):
    """ParkingSlot model."""
    __tablename__ = "parking_slots"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    slot_id = Column(String(10), nullable=False)
    parking_area_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("parking_areas.id"))
    floor = Column(Integer, default=1)
    slot_type = Column(String(20), default="STANDARD")
    status = Column(String(20), default="VACANT")
    position_x = Column(Integer, nullable=True)
    position_y = Column(Integer, nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())

    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("parking_area_id", "slot_id", name="_area_slot_uc"),)

    parking_area = relationship("ParkingArea", back_populates="slots")
    reservations = relationship("Reservation", back_populates="slot")


===== backend/app/models/payment.py =====
import uuid
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Numeric, DateTime, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.database import Base

class Payment(Base):
    """Payment model."""
    __tablename__ = "payments"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    reservation_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("reservations.id"))
    user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"))
    amount = Column(Numeric(10, 2), nullable=False)
    currency = Column(String(3), default="INR")
    method = Column(String(20), nullable=True)
    status = Column(String(20), default="PENDING")
    transaction_id = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    reservation = relationship("Reservation", back_populates="payment")
    user = relationship("User", back_populates="payments")


===== backend/app/models/reservation.py =====
import uuid
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, DateTime, Numeric, Text, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.database import Base

class Reservation(Base):
    """Reservation model."""
    __tablename__ = "reservations"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"))
    slot_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("parking_slots.id"))
    parking_area_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("parking_areas.id"))
    start_time = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    end_time = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    actual_check_in = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    actual_check_out = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    status = Column(String(20), default="PENDING")
    qr_code = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    total_amount = Column(Numeric(10, 2), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())

    user = relationship("User", back_populates="reservations")
    slot = relationship("ParkingSlot", back_populates="reservations")
    parking_area = relationship("ParkingArea", back_populates="reservations")
    payment = relationship("Payment", back_populates="reservation", uselist=False)


===== backend/app/models/user.py =====
import uuid
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Boolean, DateTime, Text
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.database import Base

class User(Base):
    """User model."""
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, server_default=func.gen_random_uuid())
    email = Column(String(255), unique=True, nullable=False)
    hashed_password = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    full_name = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    phone = Column(String(20), nullable=True)
    avatar_url = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    firebase_uid = Column(String(128), unique=True, nullable=True)
    role = Column(String(20), default="USER")
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    is_verified = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())

    reservations = relationship("Reservation", back_populates="user")
    payments = relationship("Payment", back_populates="user")
    notifications = relationship("Notification", back_populates="user")

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/admin", tags=["Admin"])
admin_dependency = Depends(require_role("ADMIN", "MANAGER"))


@router.get(
    "/areas",
    response_model=List[ParkingAreaResponse],
    dependencies=[admin_dependency],
)
async def list_parking_areas(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    areas = await get_all_areas(db, is_active=None)
    responses = []
    for area in areas:
        slots = await get_slots_by_area(db, area.id)
        area_data = {
            column.name: getattr(area, column.name)
            for column in area.__table__.columns
        }
        area_data["available_slots"] = sum(
            slot.status == "VACANT" for slot in slots
        )
        area_data["occupied_slots"] = sum(
            slot.status == "OCCUPIED" for slot in slots
        )
        responses.append(area_data)
    return responses

async def create_parking_area(

@router.put(
    "/slots/{slot_id}",
    response_model=ParkingSlotResponse,
    dependencies=[admin_dependency],
)
async def update_parking_slot(
    slot_id: UUID,
    data: ParkingSlotUpdate,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    result = await db.execute(
        select(ParkingSlot).where(ParkingSlot.id == slot_id)
    )
    slot = result.scalars().first()
    if not slot:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Slot not found")
    if data.status is not None:
        slot.status = data.status
    if data.slot_type is not None:
        slot.slot_type = data.slot_type
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(slot)
    return slot


@router.delete("/slots/{slot_id}", dependencies=[admin_dependency])
async def delete_parking_slot(
    slot_id: UUID,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    result = await db.execute(
        select(ParkingSlot).where(ParkingSlot.id == slot_id)
    )
    slot = result.scalars().first()
    if not slot:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Slot not found")
    await db.delete(slot)
    await db.commit()
    return {"message": "Slot deleted"}


@router.get(
    "/users",
    response_model=List[UserResponse],
    dependencies=[admin_dependency],
)
async def list_users(
    search: Optional[str] = Query(default=None, max_length=100),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    query = select(User).order_by(User.created_at.desc())
    if search and search.strip():
        term = f"%{search.strip()}%"
        query = query.where(
            or_(User.full_name.ilike(term), User.email.ilike(term))
        )
    result = await db.execute(query)
    return result.scalars().all()


@router.put(
    "/users/{user_id}/role",
    response_model=UserResponse,
    dependencies=[admin_dependency],
)
async def change_role(
    user_id: UUID,
    role: str,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    normalized_role = role.upper()
    allowed_roles = {"USER", "ADMIN", "MANAGER", "OPERATOR", "SECURITY"}
    if normalized_role not in allowed_roles:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid role")
    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.id == user_id))
    user = result.scalars().first()
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")
    user.role = normalized_role
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(user)
    return user


@router.put(
    "/users/{user_id}/suspend",
    response_model=UserResponse,
    dependencies=[admin_dependency],
)
async def toggle_suspend(
    user_id: UUID,
    suspend: bool,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.id == user_id))
    user = result.scalars().first()
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")
    user.is_active = not suspend
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(user)
    return user


@router.get(
    "/stats",
    response_model=DashboardStatsResponse,
    dependencies=[admin_dependency],
)
async def get_dashboard_stats(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    total_users = int(await db.scalar(select(func.count(User.id))) or 0)
    active_users = int(
        await db.scalar(
            select(func.count(User.id)).where(User.is_active.is_(True))
        )
        or 0
    )
    total_areas = int(await db.scalar(select(func.count(ParkingArea.id))) or 0)
    total_slots = int(await db.scalar(select(func.count(ParkingSlot.id))) or 0)
    available_slots = int(
        await db.scalar(
            select(func.count(ParkingSlot.id)).where(
                ParkingSlot.status == "VACANT"
            )
        )
        or 0
    )
    occupied_slots = int(
        await db.scalar(
            select(func.count(ParkingSlot.id)).where(
                ParkingSlot.status == "OCCUPIED"
            )
        )
        or 0
    )
    reserved_slots = int(
        await db.scalar(
            select(func.count(ParkingSlot.id)).where(
                ParkingSlot.status == "RESERVED"
            )
        )
        or 0
    )
    total_reservations = int(
        await db.scalar(select(func.count(Reservation.id))) or 0
    )
    total_revenue = Decimal(
        await db.scalar(
            select(func.coalesce(func.sum(Reservation.total_amount), 0)).where(
                Reservation.status == "COMPLETED"
            )
        )
        or 0
    )

    today = datetime.now(timezone.utc).date()
    first_day = today - timedelta(days=6)
    revenue_result = await db.execute(
        select(
            func.date(Reservation.created_at).label("day"),
            func.coalesce(func.sum(Reservation.total_amount), 0).label(
                "amount"
            ),
        )
        .where(
            Reservation.status == "COMPLETED",
            Reservation.created_at
            >= datetime.combine(
                first_day,
                datetime.min.time(),
                tzinfo=timezone.utc,
            ),
        )
        .group_by(func.date(Reservation.created_at))
    )
    amounts_by_day = {
        row.day: Decimal(row.amount or 0) for row in revenue_result
    }
    revenue_last_7_days = [
        {
            "date": first_day + timedelta(days=index),
            "amount": amounts_by_day.get(
                first_day + timedelta(days=index), Decimal("0")
            ),
        }
        for index in range(7)
    ]

    occupancy_rate = (
        round((occupied_slots / total_slots) * 100, 1)
        if total_slots
        else 0.0
    )
    return {
        "total_users": total_users,
        "active_users": active_users,
        "total_areas": total_areas,
        "total_slots": total_slots,
        "available_slots": available_slots,
        "occupied_slots": occupied_slots,
        "reserved_slots": reserved_slots,
        "total_reservations": total_reservations,
        "total_revenue": total_revenue,
        "occupancy_rate": occupancy_rate,
        "revenue_last_7_days": revenue_last_7_days,
    }


@router.get(
    "/reservations",
    response_model=List[AdminReservationResponse],
    dependencies=[admin_dependency],
)
async def all_reservations(
    status: Optional[str] = None,
    limit: int = Query(default=100, ge=1, le=500),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    query = (
        select(
            Reservation,
            User.full_name.label("user_name"),
            ParkingSlot.slot_id.label("slot_number"),
            ParkingArea.name.label("area_name"),
        )
        .join(User, Reservation.user_id == User.id)
        .join(ParkingSlot, Reservation.slot_id == ParkingSlot.id)
        .join(ParkingArea, Reservation.parking_area_id == ParkingArea.id)
        .order_by(Reservation.created_at.desc())
        .limit(limit)
    )
    if status and status.strip():
        query = query.where(Reservation.status == status.strip().upper())

    result = await db.execute(query)
    return [
        {
            "id": reservation.id,
            "user_id": reservation.user_id,
            "user_name": user_name,
            "slot_id": slot_number,
            "area_name": area_name,
            "start_time": reservation.start_time,
            "end_time": reservation.end_time,
            "status": reservation.status,
            "amount": reservation.total_amount or Decimal("0"),
            "created_at": reservation.created_at,
        }
        for reservation, user_name, slot_number, area_name in result.all()
    ]


@router.put(
    "/reservations/{reservation_id}/force-cancel",
    response_model=AdminReservationResponse,
    dependencies=[admin_dependency],
)
async def force_cancel(
    reservation_id: UUID,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    result = await db.execute(
        select(Reservation).where(Reservation.id == reservation_id)
    )
    reservation = result.scalars().first()
    if not reservation:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Reservation not found")
    if reservation.status not in {"PENDING", "ACTIVE"}:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail=f"Cannot cancel a {reservation.status.lower()} reservation",
        )

    reservation.status = "CANCELLED"
    slot_result = await db.execute(
        select(ParkingSlot).where(ParkingSlot.id == reservation.slot_id)
    )
    slot = slot_result.scalars().first()
    if slot:
        slot.status = "VACANT"
    await db.commit()

    joined = await db.execute(
        select(
            Reservation,
            User.full_name,
            ParkingSlot.slot_id,
            ParkingArea.name,
        )
        .join(User, Reservation.user_id == User.id)
        .join(ParkingSlot, Reservation.slot_id == ParkingSlot.id)
        .join(ParkingArea, Reservation.parking_area_id == ParkingArea.id)
        .where(Reservation.id == reservation_id)
    )
    reservation, user_name, slot_number, area_name = joined.one()
    return {
        "id": reservation.id,
        "user_id": reservation.user_id,
        "user_name": user_name,
        "slot_id": slot_number,
        "area_name": area_name,
        "start_time": reservation.start_time,
        "end_time": reservation.end_time,
        "status": reservation.status,
        "amount": reservation.total_amount or Decimal("0"),
        "created_at": reservation.created_at,
    }


@router.get(
    "/cameras",
    response_model=List[AdminCameraResponse],
    dependencies=[admin_dependency],
)
async def list_cameras(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    result = await db.execute(
        select(Camera, ParkingArea.name)
        .join(ParkingArea, Camera.parking_area_id == ParkingArea.id)
        .order_by(Camera.name)
    )
    return [
        {
            "id": camera.id,
            "name": camera.name,
            "parking_area_id": camera.parking_area_id,
            "area_name": area_name,
            "floor": camera.floor,
            "status": camera.status,
            "is_active": camera.is_active,
        }
        for camera, area_name in result.all()
    ]


===== backend/app/routers/auth_router.py =====
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from app.database import get_db
from app.models.user import User
from app.schemas.user_schema import (
    UserCreate,
    UserLogin,
    UserResponse,
    TokenResponse,
    UserUpdate,
    PasswordResetRequest,
)
from app.services.auth_service import (
    register_user,
    authenticate_user,
    create_access_token,
    create_refresh_token,
    get_current_user,
)
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordRequestForm
import logging

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/auth", tags=["Authentication"])
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@router.post("/register", response_model=TokenResponse)
async def register(
    user_data: UserCreate,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    user = await register_user(db, user_data)

    access_token = create_access_token(
        data={"sub": str(user.id), "role": user.role}
    )

    refresh_token = create_refresh_token(
        data={"sub": str(user.id)}
    )

    return {
        "access_token": access_token,
        "refresh_token": refresh_token,
        "token_type": "bearer",
    }


@router.post("/login", response_model=TokenResponse)
async def login(
    form_data: OAuth2PasswordRequestForm = Depends(),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    user = await authenticate_user(
        db,
        form_data.username,
        form_data.password,
    )

    access_token = create_access_token(
        data={"sub": str(user.id), "role": user.role}
    )

    refresh_token = create_refresh_token(
        data={"sub": str(user.id)}
    )

    return {
        "access_token": access_token,
        "refresh_token": refresh_token,
        "token_type": "bearer",
    }


@router.post("/refresh", response_model=TokenResponse)
async def refresh(refresh_token: str):
    pass


@router.get("/me", response_model=UserResponse)
async def read_users_me(
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    return current_user


@router.put("/me", response_model=UserResponse)
async def update_users_me(
    update_data: UserUpdate,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    if update_data.full_name is not None:
        current_user.full_name = update_data.full_name

    if update_data.phone is not None:
        current_user.phone = update_data.phone

    if update_data.avatar_url is not None:
        current_user.avatar_url = update_data.avatar_url

    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(current_user)

    return current_user


@router.post("/forgot-password")
async def forgot_password(req: PasswordResetRequest):
    logger.info(f"Password reset requested for {req.email}")

    return {
        "message": "If the email is registered, a reset link has been sent."
    }


===== backend/app/routers/detection_router.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import (
    APIRouter,
    BackgroundTasks,
    Depends,
    File,
    Form,
    HTTPException,
    UploadFile,
    status,
)
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.config import settings
from app.database import get_db
from app.services.parking_service import get_slots_by_area
from app.services.video_detection_service import (
    DEFAULT_LAYOUT,
    video_detection_service,
)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/detection", tags=["YOLO Detection"])

ALLOWED_VIDEO_EXTENSIONS = {".mp4", ".avi", ".mov", ".mkv"}


def _job_response(job: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    data = job.public_data()
    data["status_url"] = f"/api/v1/detection/jobs/{job.id}"
    data["result_video_url"] = (
        f"/api/v1/detection/jobs/{job.id}/video"
        if data["result_available"]
        else None
    )
    return data


@router.get("/model")
async def model_information(load: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if load:
        try:
            await asyncio.to_thread(video_detection_service.yolo.load_model)
        except RuntimeError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    return video_detection_service.yolo.get_model_info()


@router.get("/layouts/default")
async def default_layout() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        return video_detection_service.layout_info(DEFAULT_LAYOUT)
    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc)) from exc


@router.post("/videos", status_code=status.HTTP_202_ACCEPTED)
async def upload_recorded_video(
    background_tasks: BackgroundTasks,
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    area_id: UUID = Form(...),
    floor: int = Form(1, ge=1),
    layout_name: str = Form(DEFAULT_LAYOUT),
    repeat_count: int = Form(1, ge=1, le=360),
    update_database: bool = Form(False),
    save_annotated_video: bool = Form(True),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    original_filename = Path(file.filename or "recorded_video.mp4").name
    suffix = Path(original_filename).suffix.lower()

    if suffix not in ALLOWED_VIDEO_EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Upload an MP4, AVI, MOV, or MKV video",
        )

    try:
        mapper = video_detection_service.load_mapper(layout_name)
    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    slots = await get_slots_by_area(db, area_id, floor)
    if not slots:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="No parking slots exist for this area and floor",
        )

    database_slot_ids = {str(slot.slot_id) for slot in slots}
    mapped_slot_ids = set(mapper.slot_ids())
    matching_slot_ids = database_slot_ids & mapped_slot_ids
    if not matching_slot_ids:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail=(
                "The selected area does not contain any slot IDs from the "
                f"layout. Expected examples: {', '.join(mapper.slot_ids()[:5])}"
            ),
        )

    missing_slots = sorted(mapped_slot_ids - database_slot_ids)
    warning = None
    if missing_slots:
        warning = (
            "These layout slots are not present in the selected floor and will "
            f"not update the database: {', '.join(missing_slots)}"
        )

    upload_path = video_detection_service.allocate_upload_path(suffix)
    maximum_bytes = settings.YOLO_MAX_VIDEO_UPLOAD_MB * 1024 * 1024
    uploaded_bytes = 0

    try:
        with upload_path.open("wb") as output_file:
            while chunk := await file.read(1024 * 1024):
                uploaded_bytes += len(chunk)
                if uploaded_bytes > maximum_bytes:
                    raise HTTPException(
                        status_code=413,
                        detail=(
                            "Video exceeds the configured "
                            f"{settings.YOLO_MAX_VIDEO_UPLOAD_MB} MB limit"
                        ),
                    )
                output_file.write(chunk)

        if uploaded_bytes == 0:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="The video file is empty")

        try:
            video_detection_service.probe_video(upload_path)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc

        job = video_detection_service.create_job(
            source_path=upload_path,
            original_filename=original_filename,
            area_id=area_id,
            floor=floor,
            layout_name=layout_name,
            repeat_count=repeat_count,
            update_database=update_database,
            save_annotated_video=save_annotated_video,
            warning=warning,
        )
        background_tasks.add_task(
            video_detection_service.process_job,
            job.id,
        )
        return _job_response(job)
    except Exception:
        if upload_path.exists() and not any(
            job.source_path == upload_path
            for job in video_detection_service.jobs.values()
        ):
            upload_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise
    finally:
        await file.close()


@router.get("/jobs")
async def list_video_jobs() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    return [
        _job_response(job)
        for job in sorted(
            video_detection_service.jobs.values(),
            key=lambda item: item.created_at,
            reverse=True,
        )
    ]


@router.get("/jobs/{job_id}")
async def video_job_status(job_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    job = video_detection_service.get_job(job_id)
    if job is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Detection job not found")
    return _job_response(job)


@router.post("/jobs/{job_id}/stop")
async def stop_video_job(job_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    job = video_detection_service.request_stop(job_id)
    if job is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Detection job not found")
    return _job_response(job)


@router.get("/jobs/{job_id}/video")
async def download_annotated_video(job_id: str) -> FileResponse:
    job = video_detection_service.get_job(job_id)
    if job is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Detection job not found")
    if job.status not in {"completed", "stopped"}:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Detection is not finished")
    if job.result_path is None or not job.result_path.is_file():
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="This job has no annotated result video",
        )

    return FileResponse(
        path=job.result_path,
        media_type="video/mp4",
        filename=f"yolo_{Path(job.original_filename).stem}.mp4",
    )


@router.delete("/jobs/{job_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)
async def delete_video_job(job_id: str) -> None:
    job = video_detection_service.get_job(job_id)
    if job is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Detection job not found")
    if job.status in {"queued", "processing"}:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=409,
            detail="Stop the job before deleting it",
        )
    video_detection_service.delete_job(job_id)


===== backend/app/routers/notification_router.py =====
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from uuid import UUID
from app.database import get_db
from app.models.user import User
from app.services.auth_service import get_current_user, require_role
from app.services.notification_service import get_user_notifications, mark_as_read, mark_all_as_read, create_notification

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/notifications", tags=["Notifications"])

@router.get("/")
async def list_notifications(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return await get_user_notifications(db, current_user.id)

@router.put("/{id}/read")
async def read_notification(id: UUID, current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    await mark_as_read(db, id)
    return {"message": "Marked as read"}

@router.put("/read-all")
async def read_all(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    await mark_all_as_read(db, current_user.id)
    return {"message": "All marked as read"}

@router.post("/broadcast", dependencies=[Depends(require_role("ADMIN"))])
async def broadcast(title: str, body: str, type: str = "ANNOUNCEMENT", db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    from sqlalchemy import select
    result = await db.execute(select(User))
    users = result.scalars().all()
    for user in users:
        await create_notification(db, user.id, title, body, type)
    return {"message": f"Broadcasted to {len(users)} users"}


===== backend/app/routers/parking_router.py =====
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from typing import List, Optional
from uuid import UUID
from app.database import get_db
from app.schemas.parking_schema import ParkingAreaResponse, ParkingSlotResponse, FloorSummary
from app.services.parking_service import get_all_areas, get_area_by_id, get_slots_by_area, get_slot_by_id, get_area_summary

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/parking", tags=["Parking"])

@router.get("/areas", response_model=List[ParkingAreaResponse])
async def list_areas(
    lat: Optional[float] = None,
    lng: Optional[float] = None,
    radius: Optional[float] = None,
    is_active: bool = True,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)
):
    areas = await get_all_areas(db, is_active)

    responses = []

    for area in areas:
        slots = await get_slots_by_area(db, area.id)

        available_slots = sum(
            1 for slot in slots if slot.status == "VACANT"
        )

        occupied_slots = sum(
            1 for slot in slots if slot.status == "OCCUPIED"
        )

        area_data = {
            "id": area.id,
            "name": area.name,
            "address": area.address,
            "latitude": area.latitude,
            "longitude": area.longitude,
            "total_floors": area.total_floors,
            "total_slots": area.total_slots,
            "hourly_rate": area.hourly_rate,
            "currency": area.currency,
            "image_url": area.image_url,
            "is_active": area.is_active,
            "opening_time": area.opening_time,
            "closing_time": area.closing_time,
            "created_at": area.created_at,
            "updated_at": area.updated_at,
            "available_slots": available_slots,
            "occupied_slots": occupied_slots,
        }

        responses.append(area_data)

    return responses

@router.get("/areas/{area_id}", response_model=ParkingAreaResponse)
async def get_area(area_id: UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    area = await get_area_by_id(db, area_id)
    if not area:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Area not found")
    # You would compute available/occupied here
    return area

@router.get("/areas/{area_id}/slots", response_model=List[ParkingSlotResponse])
async def get_slots(area_id: UUID, floor: Optional[int] = None, status: Optional[str] = None, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    slots = await get_slots_by_area(db, area_id, floor)
    if status:
        slots = [s for s in slots if s.status == status]
    return slots

@router.get("/areas/{area_id}/summary", response_model=List[FloorSummary])
async def get_summary(area_id: UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    summary = await get_area_summary(db, area_id)
    return summary

@router.get("/slots/{slot_id}", response_model=ParkingSlotResponse)
async def get_slot(slot_id: UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    slot = await get_slot_by_id(db, slot_id)
    if not slot:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Slot not found")
    return slot

===== backend/app/routers/reservation_router.py =====
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import select
from typing import List, Optional
from uuid import UUID
from app.database import get_db
from app.models.user import User
from sqlalchemy.orm import selectinload
from app.models.reservation import Reservation
from app.schemas.reservation_schema import ReservationCreate, ReservationResponse
from app.services.auth_service import get_current_user
from app.services.reservation_service import create_reservation, cancel_reservation, check_in, check_out, get_user_reservations, get_active_reservation

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/reservations", tags=["Reservations"])

@router.post("/", response_model=ReservationResponse)
async def create_new_reservation(data: ReservationCreate, current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return await create_reservation(db, current_user.id, data)

@router.get("/", response_model=List[ReservationResponse])
async def list_reservations(status: Optional[str] = None, current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return await get_user_reservations(db, current_user.id, status)

@router.get("/active", response_model=Optional[ReservationResponse])
async def get_active(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return await get_active_reservation(db, current_user.id)
@router.get("/{reservation_id}", response_model=ReservationResponse)
async def get_reservation(
    reservation_id: UUID,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)
):
    result = await db.execute(
        select(Reservation)
        .options(selectinload(Reservation.slot))
        .filter(
            Reservation.id == reservation_id,
            Reservation.user_id == current_user.id
        )
    )

    res = result.scalars().first()

    if not res:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Reservation not found"
        )

    return res

@router.put("/{reservation_id}/cancel", response_model=ReservationResponse)
async def cancel(reservation_id: UUID, current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return await cancel_reservation(db, reservation_id, current_user.id)

@router.put("/{reservation_id}/check-in", response_model=ReservationResponse)
async def perform_check_in(reservation_id: UUID, current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    # In reality, this might be triggered by hardware, not directly by user, but let's allow it for simulation
    return await check_in(db, reservation_id, current_user.id)

@router.put("/{reservation_id}/check-out", response_model=ReservationResponse)
async def perform_check_out(reservation_id: UUID, current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return await check_out(db, reservation_id, current_user.id)


===== backend/app/routers/websocket_router.py =====
from fastapi import APIRouter, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from typing import Dict, List
import logging
import asyncio

router = APIRouter(tags=["WebSocket"])
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class ConnectionManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.active_connections: Dict[str, List[WebSocket]] = {}

    async def connect(self, area_id: str, websocket: WebSocket):
        await websocket.accept()
        if area_id not in self.active_connections:
            self.active_connections[area_id] = []
        self.active_connections[area_id].append(websocket)
        logger.info(f"Client connected to area {area_id}. Total: {len(self.active_connections[area_id])}")

    def disconnect(self, area_id: str, websocket: WebSocket):
        if area_id in self.active_connections:
            self.active_connections[area_id].remove(websocket)
            logger.info(f"Client disconnected from area {area_id}.")

    async def broadcast_slot_update(self, area_id: str, slot_data: dict):
        if area_id in self.active_connections:
            for connection in self.active_connections[area_id]:
                try:
                    await connection.send_json(slot_data)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"Failed to send to websocket: {e}")

manager = ConnectionManager()

@router.websocket("/ws/parking/{area_id}")
async def websocket_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket, area_id: str):
    await manager.connect(area_id, websocket)
    try:
        while True:
            data = await websocket.receive_text()
            if data == "ping":
                await websocket.send_text("pong")
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        manager.disconnect(area_id, websocket)

===== backend/app/seed.py =====
import asyncio
import logging
from sqlalchemy import select
from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal, init_db
from app.models.user import User
from app.models.parking_area import ParkingArea
from app.models.parking_slot import ParkingSlot
from app.services.auth_service import hash_password
from datetime import time
from decimal import Decimal

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

async def seed_data():
    await init_db()
    
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        # Create admin user
        result = await db.execute(select(User).filter(User.email == "admin@smartparking.com"))
        admin = result.scalars().first()
        if not admin:
            admin = User(
                email="admin@smartparking.com",
                hashed_password=hash_password("Admin@123"),
                full_name="System Admin",
                role="ADMIN",
                is_active=True,
                is_verified=True
            )
            db.add(admin)
            await db.commit()
            await db.refresh(admin)
            logger.info("Admin user created")
        else:
            admin.hashed_password = hash_password("Admin@123")
            admin.full_name = "System Admin"
            admin.role = "ADMIN"
            admin.is_active = True
            admin.is_verified = True
            await db.commit()
            logger.info("Admin user reset and verified")
        
        # Create Parking Area
        result = await db.execute(select(ParkingArea).filter(ParkingArea.name == "Smart Park Central"))
        area = result.scalars().first()
        if not area:
            area = ParkingArea(
                name="Smart Park Central",
                address="123 Smart City Blvd",
                latitude=28.6139,
                longitude=77.2090,
                total_floors=1,
                total_slots=20,
                hourly_rate=Decimal("20.00"),
                currency="INR",
                opening_time=time(6, 0),
                closing_time=time(23, 0)
            )
            db.add(area)
            await db.commit()
            await db.refresh(area)
            logger.info("Parking area created")
            
            # Create Slots
            slots_data = []
            
            # Row A (A01-A10): y=0, x=0-9
            for i in range(1, 11):
                slots_data.append(ParkingSlot(slot_id=f"A{i:02d}", parking_area_id=area.id, floor=1, slot_type="STANDARD", position_x=i-1, position_y=0, status="VACANT"))
            
            # Row B (B01-B05): y=1, x=0-4
            for i in range(1, 6):
                slots_data.append(ParkingSlot(slot_id=f"B{i:02d}", parking_area_id=area.id, floor=1, slot_type="STANDARD", position_x=i-1, position_y=1, status="VACANT"))
            
            # Row C (C01-C03): y=2, x=0-2, type=VIP
            for i in range(1, 4):
                slots_data.append(ParkingSlot(slot_id=f"C{i:02d}", parking_area_id=area.id, floor=1, slot_type="VIP", position_x=i-1, position_y=2, status="VACANT"))
                
            # Row D (D01): y=3, x=0, type=EV
            slots_data.append(ParkingSlot(slot_id="D01", parking_area_id=area.id, floor=1, slot_type="EV", position_x=0, position_y=3, status="VACANT"))
            
            # Row E (E01): y=3, x=1, type=DISABLED
            slots_data.append(ParkingSlot(slot_id="E01", parking_area_id=area.id, floor=1, slot_type="DISABLED", position_x=1, position_y=3, status="VACANT"))

            for slot in slots_data:
                if slot.slot_id in ["A03", "A07", "B02"]:
                    slot.status = "OCCUPIED"
                elif slot.slot_id == "A05":
                    slot.status = "RESERVED"
                db.add(slot)
                
            await db.commit()
            logger.info("Parking slots created and initialized")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(seed_data())


===== backend/app/services/__init__.py =====
"""Services package."""


===== backend/app/services/auth_service.py =====
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Optional, List
import bcrypt
from jose import JWTError, jwt
from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from app.config import settings
from app.database import get_db
from app.models.user import User
from app.schemas.user_schema import UserCreate

oauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl="/api/v1/auth/login")

def hash_password(password: str) -> str:
    password_bytes = password.encode("utf-8")
    if len(password_bytes) > 72:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Password must be at most 72 UTF-8 bytes",
        )
    return bcrypt.hashpw(password_bytes, bcrypt.gensalt()).decode("utf-8")

def verify_password(plain_password: str, hashed_password: str) -> bool:
    try:
        return bcrypt.checkpw(
            plain_password.encode("utf-8"),
            hashed_password.encode("utf-8"),
        )
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return False

def create_access_token(data: dict, expires_delta: Optional[timedelta] = None) -> str:
    to_encode = data.copy()
    if expires_delta:
        expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + expires_delta
    else:
        expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES)
    to_encode.update({"exp": expire})
    encoded_jwt = jwt.encode(to_encode, settings.SECRET_KEY, algorithm=settings.ALGORITHM)
    return encoded_jwt

def create_refresh_token(data: dict) -> str:
    to_encode = data.copy()
    expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(days=settings.REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS)
    to_encode.update({"exp": expire})
    encoded_jwt = jwt.encode(to_encode, settings.SECRET_KEY, algorithm=settings.ALGORITHM)
    return encoded_jwt

def verify_token(token: str) -> dict:
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[settings.ALGORITHM])
        return payload
    except JWTError:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Could not validate credentials",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )

async def get_current_user(token: str = Depends(oauth2_scheme), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)) -> User:
    payload = verify_token(token)
    user_id: str = payload.get("sub")
    if user_id is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid authentication credentials")
    
    result = await db.execute(select(User).filter(User.id == user_id))
    user = result.scalars().first()
    
    if user is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="User not found")
    return user

def require_role(*roles: str):
    async def role_checker(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)) -> User:
        if current_user.role not in roles:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not enough privileges")
        return current_user
    return role_checker

async def register_user(db: AsyncSession, user_data: UserCreate) -> User:
    result = await db.execute(select(User).filter(User.email == user_data.email))
    if result.scalars().first():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Email already registered")
        
    hashed_pwd = hash_password(user_data.password)
    new_user = User(
        email=user_data.email,
        hashed_password=hashed_pwd,
        full_name=user_data.full_name,
        phone=user_data.phone
    )
    db.add(new_user)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(new_user)
    return new_user

async def authenticate_user(db: AsyncSession, email: str, password: str) -> User:
    result = await db.execute(select(User).filter(func.lower(User.email) == email.strip().lower()))
    user = result.scalars().first()
    if not user or not verify_password(password, user.hashed_password):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Incorrect email or password")
    if not user.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="This account is suspended")
    return user
