===== backend/app/services/parking_pricing.py =====
from decimal import Decimal
from math import ceil


ADVANCE_FEE = Decimal("10.00")
FIRST_HOUR_FEE = Decimal("20.00")
EXTRA_STARTED_HOUR_FEE = Decimal("5.00")
SECONDS_PER_HOUR = 3600


def calculate_parking_total(duration_seconds: float) -> Decimal:
    """Return ₹20 for the first hour and ₹5 per started extra hour."""
    safe_duration = max(1.0, duration_seconds)
    if safe_duration <= SECONDS_PER_HOUR:
        return FIRST_HOUR_FEE

    extra_started_hours = ceil(
        (safe_duration - SECONDS_PER_HOUR) / SECONDS_PER_HOUR
    )
    return (
        FIRST_HOUR_FEE
        + (EXTRA_STARTED_HOUR_FEE * Decimal(extra_started_hours))
    ).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def calculate_checkout_balance(total_amount: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    return max(Decimal("0.00"), total_amount - ADVANCE_FEE).quantize(
        Decimal("0.01")
    )


===== backend/app/services/parking_service.py =====
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from uuid import UUID
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import select, func, update, delete, case
from app.models.parking_area import ParkingArea
from app.models.parking_slot import ParkingSlot
from app.schemas.parking_schema import ParkingAreaCreate, ParkingAreaUpdate, ParkingSlotCreate

async def get_all_areas(db: AsyncSession, is_active: Optional[bool] = True) -> List[ParkingArea]:
    query = select(ParkingArea)
    if is_active is not None:
        query = query.filter(ParkingArea.is_active == is_active)
    query = query.order_by(ParkingArea.name)
    result = await db.execute(query)
    return list(result.scalars().all())

async def get_area_by_id(db: AsyncSession, area_id: UUID) -> Optional[ParkingArea]:
    result = await db.execute(select(ParkingArea).filter(ParkingArea.id == area_id))
    return result.scalars().first()

async def create_area(db: AsyncSession, area_data: ParkingAreaCreate) -> ParkingArea:
    new_area = ParkingArea(**area_data.model_dump())
    db.add(new_area)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(new_area)
    return new_area

async def update_area(db: AsyncSession, area_id: UUID, data: ParkingAreaUpdate) -> Optional[ParkingArea]:
    area = await get_area_by_id(db, area_id)
    if not area:
        return None
    for key, value in data.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items():
        setattr(area, key, value)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(area)
    return area

async def delete_area(db: AsyncSession, area_id: UUID):
    area = await get_area_by_id(db, area_id)
    if area:
        await db.delete(area)
        await db.commit()

async def get_slots_by_area(db: AsyncSession, area_id: UUID, floor: Optional[int] = None) -> List[ParkingSlot]:
    query = select(ParkingSlot).filter(ParkingSlot.parking_area_id == area_id)
    if floor is not None:
        query = query.filter(ParkingSlot.floor == floor)
    result = await db.execute(query)
    return list(result.scalars().all())

async def get_slot_by_id(db: AsyncSession, slot_id: UUID) -> Optional[ParkingSlot]:
    result = await db.execute(select(ParkingSlot).filter(ParkingSlot.id == slot_id))
    return result.scalars().first()

async def create_slot(db: AsyncSession, slot_data: ParkingSlotCreate) -> ParkingSlot:
    new_slot = ParkingSlot(**slot_data.model_dump())
    db.add(new_slot)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(new_slot)
    return new_slot

async def update_slot_status(db: AsyncSession, slot_id: UUID, status: str) -> Optional[ParkingSlot]:
    slot = await get_slot_by_id(db, slot_id)
    if not slot:
        return None
    slot.status = status
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(slot)
    return slot

async def get_area_summary(db: AsyncSession, area_id: UUID) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    query = (
        select(
            ParkingSlot.floor,
            func.count(ParkingSlot.id).label("total"),
            func.sum(
                case(
                    (ParkingSlot.status == "VACANT", 1),
                    else_=0
                )
            ).label("vacant"),
            func.sum(
                case(
                    (ParkingSlot.status == "OCCUPIED", 1),
                    else_=0
                )
            ).label("occupied"),
            func.sum(
                case(
                    (ParkingSlot.status == "RESERVED", 1),
                    else_=0
                )
            ).label("reserved"),
        )
        .where(ParkingSlot.parking_area_id == area_id)
        .group_by(ParkingSlot.floor)
        .order_by(ParkingSlot.floor)
    )

    result = await db.execute(query)

    summary = []

    for row in result:
        summary.append({
            "floor": row.floor,
            "total": row.total,
            "vacant": row.vacant or 0,
            "occupied": row.occupied or 0,
            "reserved": row.reserved or 0,
        })

    return summary
async def bulk_create_slots(db: AsyncSession, area_id: UUID, floor: int, prefix: str, count: int) -> List[ParkingSlot]:
    slots = []
    for i in range(1, count + 1):
        slot_id = f"{prefix}{i:02d}"
        new_slot = ParkingSlot(
            slot_id=slot_id,
            parking_area_id=area_id,
            floor=floor,
            slot_type="STANDARD"
        )
        db.add(new_slot)
        slots.append(new_slot)
    await db.commit()
    for slot in slots:
        await db.refresh(slot)
    return slots


===== backend/app/services/payment_service.py =====
import asyncio
import hashlib
import hmac
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

import razorpay
from fastapi import HTTPException
from razorpay import errors as razorpay_errors
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy.orm import selectinload

from app.config import settings
from app.models.parking_settlement import ParkingSettlement
from app.models.parking_slot import ParkingSlot
from app.models.payment import Payment
from app.models.reservation import Reservation
from app.schemas.payment_schema import RazorpayOrderCreate, RazorpayPaymentVerify
from app.services.parking_pricing import ADVANCE_FEE
from app.utils.helpers import generate_qr_code


def _credentials() -> tuple[str, str]:
    key_id = settings.RAZORPAY_KEY_ID.strip()
    secret_setting = settings.RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET
    key_secret = (
        secret_setting.get_secret_value()
        if hasattr(secret_setting, "get_secret_value")
        else str(secret_setting)
    ).strip()
    if not key_id or not key_secret:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=503,
            detail="Razorpay is not configured on the backend",
        )
    return key_id, key_secret


def _razorpay_error_status(error: Exception) -> int:
    message = str(error).lower()
    if (
        "authentication" in message
        or "invalid key" in message
        or "unauthorized" in message
    ):
        return 401
    return 500


async def _create_gateway_order(
    key_id: str,
    key_secret: str,
    amount: int,
    currency: str,
    receipt: str,
    reservation_id: UUID,
    user_id: UUID,
    purpose: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    client = razorpay.Client(auth=(key_id, key_secret))
    order_data = {
        "amount": amount,
        "currency": currency,
        "receipt": receipt,
        "notes": {
            "reservation_id": str(reservation_id),
            "user_id": str(user_id),
            "purpose": purpose,
        },
    }

    try:
        return await asyncio.to_thread(client.order.create, data=order_data)
    except razorpay_errors.BadRequestError as error:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=_razorpay_error_status(error),
            detail="Razorpay rejected the order request",
        ) from error
    except (razorpay_errors.ServerError, razorpay_errors.GatewayError) as error:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=500,
            detail="Razorpay order creation failed",
        ) from error
    except Exception as error:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=500,
            detail="Razorpay order creation failed",
        ) from error


async def create_razorpay_order(
    db: AsyncSession,
    user_id: UUID,
    data: RazorpayOrderCreate,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    key_id, key_secret = _credentials()

    result = await db.execute(
        select(Reservation)
        .options(selectinload(Reservation.parking_area))
        .filter(
            Reservation.id == data.reservation_id,
            Reservation.user_id == user_id,
        )
    )
    reservation = result.scalars().first()
    if not reservation:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Reservation not found")

    if data.purpose == "SETTLEMENT":
        return await _create_settlement_order(
            db,
            reservation,
            user_id,
            data,
            key_id,
            key_secret,
        )

    return await _create_advance_order(
        db,
        reservation,
        user_id,
        data,
        key_id,
        key_secret,
    )


async def _create_advance_order(
    db: AsyncSession,
    reservation: Reservation,
    user_id: UUID,
    data: RazorpayOrderCreate,
    key_id: str,
    key_secret: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if reservation.status != "PAYMENT_PENDING":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Advance payment is not pending")

    expected_amount = int(ADVANCE_FEE * 100)
    if data.amount != expected_amount:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Advance payment must be ₹10")

    expected_currency = (reservation.parking_area.currency or "INR").upper()
    if data.currency.upper() != expected_currency:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Payment currency does not match the reservation",
        )

    payment_result = await db.execute(
        select(Payment).filter(
            Payment.reservation_id == reservation.id,
            Payment.user_id == user_id,
        )
    )
    payment = payment_result.scalars().first()
    if payment and payment.status == "COMPLETED":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Advance is already paid")
    if (
        payment
        and payment.transaction_id
        and payment.transaction_id.startswith("order_")
        and payment.status in ("CREATED", "PENDING")
    ):
        return {
            "order_id": payment.transaction_id,
            "amount": int(Decimal(payment.amount) * 100),
            "currency": payment.currency,
            "key_id": key_id,
        }

    receipt = data.receipt or f"advance_{reservation.id.hex[:22]}"
    order = await _create_gateway_order(
        key_id,
        key_secret,
        expected_amount,
        expected_currency,
        receipt,
        reservation.id,
        user_id,
        "ADVANCE",
    )

    if not payment:
        payment = Payment(
            reservation_id=reservation.id,
            user_id=user_id,
            amount=ADVANCE_FEE,
            currency=expected_currency,
            method="RAZORPAY_ADVANCE",
        )
        db.add(payment)

    reservation.total_amount = ADVANCE_FEE
    payment.amount = ADVANCE_FEE
    payment.status = "CREATED"
    payment.transaction_id = order["id"]
    await db.commit()

    return {
        "order_id": order["id"],
        "amount": order["amount"],
        "currency": order["currency"],
        "key_id": key_id,
    }


async def _create_settlement_order(
    db: AsyncSession,
    reservation: Reservation,
    user_id: UUID,
    data: RazorpayOrderCreate,
    key_id: str,
    key_secret: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if reservation.status != "CHECKOUT_PAYMENT_PENDING":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Checkout payment is not pending")

    result = await db.execute(
        select(ParkingSettlement).filter(
            ParkingSettlement.reservation_id == reservation.id,
            ParkingSettlement.user_id == user_id,
        )
    )
    settlement = result.scalars().first()
    if not settlement:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Checkout charge not found")
    if settlement.status == "COMPLETED":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Checkout is already paid")

    expected_amount = int(Decimal(settlement.amount_due) * 100)
    if expected_amount < 100:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No checkout balance is due")
    if data.amount != expected_amount:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Payment amount does not match the checkout balance",
        )
    if data.currency.upper() != settlement.currency.upper():
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Payment currency does not match the checkout balance",
        )

    if settlement.razorpay_order_id and settlement.status == "CREATED":
        return {
            "order_id": settlement.razorpay_order_id,
            "amount": expected_amount,
            "currency": settlement.currency,
            "key_id": key_id,
        }

    receipt = data.receipt or f"checkout_{reservation.id.hex[:21]}"
    order = await _create_gateway_order(
        key_id,
        key_secret,
        expected_amount,
        settlement.currency,
        receipt,
        reservation.id,
        user_id,
        "SETTLEMENT",
    )

    settlement.status = "CREATED"
    settlement.razorpay_order_id = order["id"]
    await db.commit()

    return {
        "order_id": order["id"],
        "amount": order["amount"],
        "currency": order["currency"],
        "key_id": key_id,
    }


async def verify_razorpay_payment(
    db: AsyncSession,
    user_id: UUID,
    data: RazorpayPaymentVerify,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    _, key_secret = _credentials()

    message = f"{data.razorpay_order_id}|{data.razorpay_payment_id}".encode()
    generated_signature = hmac.new(
        key_secret.encode(),
        message,
        hashlib.sha256,
    ).hexdigest()
    if not hmac.compare_digest(generated_signature, data.razorpay_signature):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Payment signature verification failed",
        )

    payment_result = await db.execute(
        select(Payment).filter(
            Payment.user_id == user_id,
            Payment.transaction_id == data.razorpay_order_id,
        )
    )
    advance_payment = payment_result.scalars().first()
    if advance_payment:
        return await _complete_advance_payment(
            db,
            user_id,
            advance_payment,
            data.razorpay_payment_id,
        )

    settlement_result = await db.execute(
        select(ParkingSettlement).filter(
            ParkingSettlement.user_id == user_id,
            ParkingSettlement.razorpay_order_id == data.razorpay_order_id,
        )
    )
    settlement = settlement_result.scalars().first()
    if settlement:
        return await _complete_settlement_payment(
            db,
            user_id,
            settlement,
            data.razorpay_payment_id,
        )

    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Payment order not found")


async def _complete_advance_payment(
    db: AsyncSession,
    user_id: UUID,
    payment: Payment,
    payment_id: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    result = await db.execute(
        select(Reservation).filter(
            Reservation.id == payment.reservation_id,
            Reservation.user_id == user_id,
        )
    )
    reservation = result.scalars().first()
    if not reservation:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Reservation not found")

    if payment.status != "COMPLETED":
        payment.status = "COMPLETED"
        if reservation.status == "PAYMENT_PENDING":
            reservation.status = "PENDING"
        if not reservation.qr_code:
            reservation.qr_code = generate_qr_code(
                f"RES-{reservation.id}-{payment_id}"
            )
        await db.commit()

    return {
        "success": True,
        "payment_id": payment_id,
        "reservation_id": reservation.id,
    }


async def _complete_settlement_payment(
    db: AsyncSession,
    user_id: UUID,
    settlement: ParkingSettlement,
    payment_id: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    result = await db.execute(
        select(Reservation).filter(
            Reservation.id == settlement.reservation_id,
            Reservation.user_id == user_id,
        )
    )
    reservation = result.scalars().first()
    if not reservation:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Reservation not found")

    if settlement.status != "COMPLETED":
        settlement.status = "COMPLETED"
        settlement.razorpay_payment_id = payment_id
        reservation.status = "COMPLETED"

        slot_result = await db.execute(
            select(ParkingSlot).filter(ParkingSlot.id == reservation.slot_id)
        )
        slot = slot_result.scalars().first()
        if slot:
            slot.status = "VACANT"
        await db.commit()

    return {
        "success": True,
        "payment_id": payment_id,
        "reservation_id": reservation.id,
    }


===== backend/app/services/reservation_service.py =====
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import List, Optional
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import HTTPException
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy.orm import selectinload

from app.config import settings
from app.models.parking_area import ParkingArea
from app.models.parking_settlement import ParkingSettlement
from app.models.parking_slot import ParkingSlot
from app.models.reservation import Reservation
from app.schemas.reservation_schema import ReservationCreate
from app.services.parking_pricing import (
    ADVANCE_FEE,
    calculate_checkout_balance,
    calculate_parking_total,
)


def _as_utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    if value.tzinfo is None:
        return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc)


async def _release_slot(db: AsyncSession, slot_id: UUID) -> None:
    slot_result = await db.execute(
        select(ParkingSlot).filter(ParkingSlot.id == slot_id)
    )
    slot = slot_result.scalars().first()
    if slot:
        slot.status = "VACANT"


async def create_reservation(
    db: AsyncSession,
    user_id: UUID,
    data: ReservationCreate,
) -> Reservation:
    result = await db.execute(
        select(ParkingSlot).filter(ParkingSlot.id == data.slot_id)
    )
    slot = result.scalars().first()

    if not slot or slot.status != "VACANT":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Slot is not available")

    area_result = await db.execute(
        select(ParkingArea).filter(ParkingArea.id == data.parking_area_id)
    )
    area = area_result.scalars().first()
    if not area or slot.parking_area_id != area.id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid parking area")

    end_time = data.end_time or (data.start_time + timedelta(hours=1))
    new_reservation = Reservation(
        user_id=user_id,
        slot_id=data.slot_id,
        parking_area_id=data.parking_area_id,
        start_time=data.start_time,
        end_time=end_time,
        status="PAYMENT_PENDING",
        qr_code=None,
        total_amount=ADVANCE_FEE,
    )

    slot.status = "RESERVED"
    db.add(new_reservation)
    await db.commit()

    result = await db.execute(
        select(Reservation)
        .options(selectinload(Reservation.slot))
        .filter(Reservation.id == new_reservation.id)
    )
    return result.scalars().first()


async def cancel_reservation(
    db: AsyncSession,
    reservation_id: UUID,
    user_id: UUID,
) -> Reservation:
    result = await db.execute(
        select(Reservation)
        .options(selectinload(Reservation.slot))
        .filter(
            Reservation.id == reservation_id,
            Reservation.user_id == user_id,
        )
    )
    reservation = result.scalars().first()

    if not reservation:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Reservation not found")
    if reservation.status not in ("PAYMENT_PENDING", "PENDING"):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail=(
                "Only a reservation that has not checked in can be cancelled. "
                f"Current status: {reservation.status}"
            ),
        )

    # The ₹10 advance is intentionally non-refundable.
    reservation.status = "CANCELLED"
    await _release_slot(db, reservation.slot_id)
    await db.commit()

    result = await db.execute(
        select(Reservation)
        .options(selectinload(Reservation.slot))
        .filter(Reservation.id == reservation_id)
    )
    return result.scalars().first()


async def check_in(
    db: AsyncSession,
    reservation_id: UUID,
    user_id: UUID,
) -> Reservation:
    result = await db.execute(
        select(Reservation)
        .options(selectinload(Reservation.slot))
        .filter(
            Reservation.id == reservation_id,
            Reservation.user_id == user_id,
        )
    )
    reservation = result.scalars().first()

    if not reservation or reservation.status != "PENDING":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid reservation for check-in")

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    created_at = _as_utc(reservation.created_at)
    entry_deadline = created_at + timedelta(
        minutes=settings.RESERVATION_TIMEOUT_MINUTES
    )
    if now >= entry_deadline:
        reservation.status = "EXPIRED"
        await _release_slot(db, reservation.slot_id)
        await db.commit()
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="The 30-minute entry window has expired",
        )

    reservation.status = "ACTIVE"
    reservation.actual_check_in = now

    slot_result = await db.execute(
        select(ParkingSlot).filter(ParkingSlot.id == reservation.slot_id)
    )
    slot = slot_result.scalars().first()
    if slot:
        slot.status = "OCCUPIED"

    await db.commit()

    result = await db.execute(
        select(Reservation)
        .options(selectinload(Reservation.slot))
        .filter(Reservation.id == reservation_id)
    )
    return result.scalars().first()


async def check_out(
    db: AsyncSession,
    reservation_id: UUID,
    user_id: UUID,
) -> Reservation:
    result = await db.execute(
        select(Reservation)
        .options(
            selectinload(Reservation.slot),
            selectinload(Reservation.parking_area),
        )
        .filter(
            Reservation.id == reservation_id,
            Reservation.user_id == user_id,
        )
    )
    reservation = result.scalars().first()

    if not reservation:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Reservation not found")
    if reservation.status == "CHECKOUT_PAYMENT_PENDING":
        return reservation
    if reservation.status != "ACTIVE" or not reservation.actual_check_in:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid reservation for check-out")

    checkout_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    duration = checkout_time - _as_utc(reservation.actual_check_in)
    total_amount = calculate_parking_total(duration.total_seconds())
    amount_due = calculate_checkout_balance(total_amount)

    reservation.actual_check_out = checkout_time
    reservation.total_amount = total_amount
    reservation.status = "CHECKOUT_PAYMENT_PENDING"

    settlement_result = await db.execute(
        select(ParkingSettlement).filter(
            ParkingSettlement.reservation_id == reservation.id,
            ParkingSettlement.user_id == user_id,
        )
    )
    settlement = settlement_result.scalars().first()
    if not settlement:
        settlement = ParkingSettlement(
            reservation_id=reservation.id,
            user_id=user_id,
            total_amount=total_amount,
            advance_amount=ADVANCE_FEE,
            amount_due=amount_due,
            currency=(reservation.parking_area.currency or "INR").upper(),
            status="PENDING",
        )
        db.add(settlement)
    else:
        settlement.total_amount = total_amount
        settlement.advance_amount = ADVANCE_FEE
        settlement.amount_due = amount_due
        settlement.status = "PENDING"
        settlement.razorpay_order_id = None
        settlement.razorpay_payment_id = None

    # Keep the slot occupied until the final Razorpay signature is verified.
    await db.commit()

    result = await db.execute(
        select(Reservation)
        .options(selectinload(Reservation.slot))
        .filter(Reservation.id == reservation_id)
    )
    return result.scalars().first()


async def get_user_reservations(
    db: AsyncSession,
    user_id: UUID,
    status: Optional[str] = None,
) -> List[Reservation]:
    query = select(Reservation).filter(Reservation.user_id == user_id)
    if status:
        query = query.filter(Reservation.status == status)

    query = query.options(selectinload(Reservation.slot))
    result = await db.execute(query)
    return list(result.scalars().all())


async def get_active_reservation(
    db: AsyncSession,
    user_id: UUID,
) -> Optional[Reservation]:
    query = (
        select(Reservation)
        .options(selectinload(Reservation.slot))
        .filter(
            Reservation.user_id == user_id,
            Reservation.status.in_(
                [
                    "PAYMENT_PENDING",
                    "PENDING",
                    "ACTIVE",
                    "CHECKOUT_PAYMENT_PENDING",
                ]
            ),
        )
        .order_by(Reservation.created_at.desc())
    )
    result = await db.execute(query)
    return result.scalars().first()


async def expire_pending_reservations(db: AsyncSession) -> None:
    timeout_threshold = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(
        minutes=settings.RESERVATION_TIMEOUT_MINUTES
    )
    query = select(Reservation).filter(
        Reservation.status.in_(["PAYMENT_PENDING", "PENDING"]),
        Reservation.created_at < timeout_threshold,
    )
    result = await db.execute(query)

    for reservation in result.scalars().all():
        reservation.status = "EXPIRED"
        await _release_slot(db, reservation.slot_id)

    await db.commit()


===== backend/app/services/video_detection_service.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import logging
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

import cv2
from fastapi.encoders import jsonable_encoder

from app.ai.slot_mapper import SlotMapper
from app.config import settings
from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.routers.websocket_router import manager
from app.services.parking_service import get_slots_by_area
from app.services.yolo_service import YOLOService

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

APP_DIRECTORY = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
LAYOUT_DIRECTORY = APP_DIRECTORY / "data" / "slot_layouts"
WORK_DIRECTORY = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / "smart_parking_detection"
DEFAULT_LAYOUT = "generated_parking_1280x720"


def _utc_now() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


@dataclass
class VideoDetectionJob:
    id: str
    original_filename: str
    source_path: Path
    area_id: str
    floor: int
    layout_name: str
    repeat_count: int
    update_database: bool
    save_annotated_video: bool
    status: str = "queued"
    progress: float = 0.0
    created_at: str = field(default_factory=_utc_now)
    started_at: str | None = None
    completed_at: str | None = None
    error: str | None = None
    warning: str | None = None
    source_duration_seconds: float = 0.0
    effective_duration_seconds: float = 0.0
    input_frames_read: int = 0
    sampled_frames_processed: int = 0
    peak_vehicle_count: int = 0
    final_occupancy: Dict[str, bool] = field(default_factory=dict)
    database_updates: int = 0
    protected_status_skips: int = 0
    result_path: Path | None = None
    stop_requested: bool = False

    def public_data(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "job_id": self.id,
            "filename": self.original_filename,
            "area_id": self.area_id,
            "floor": self.floor,
            "layout": self.layout_name,
            "repeat_count": self.repeat_count,
            "update_database": self.update_database,
            "status": self.status,
            "progress_percent": round(self.progress, 2),
            "created_at": self.created_at,
            "started_at": self.started_at,
            "completed_at": self.completed_at,
            "error": self.error,
            "warning": self.warning,
            "source_duration_seconds": round(self.source_duration_seconds, 2),
            "effective_duration_seconds": round(
                self.effective_duration_seconds,
                2,
            ),
            "input_frames_read": self.input_frames_read,
            "sampled_frames_processed": self.sampled_frames_processed,
            "peak_vehicle_count": self.peak_vehicle_count,
            "final_occupancy": self.final_occupancy,
            "database_updates": self.database_updates,
            "protected_status_skips": self.protected_status_skips,
            "result_available": bool(
                self.result_path
                and self.result_path.exists()
                and self.status in {"completed", "stopped"}
            ),
        }


class _OccupancyStabilizer:
    """Requires repeated observations before confirming a status change."""

    def __init__(self, confirmations: int) -> None:
        self.confirmations = max(1, confirmations)
        self._candidate: Dict[str, bool] = {}
        self._candidate_count: Dict[str, int] = {}
        self._stable: Dict[str, bool] = {}

    def update(
        self,
        observed: Dict[str, bool],
    ) -> tuple[Dict[str, bool], Dict[str, bool]]:
        confirmed_changes: Dict[str, bool] = {}

        for slot_id, occupied in observed.items():
            if self._candidate.get(slot_id) == occupied:
                self._candidate_count[slot_id] = (
                    self._candidate_count.get(slot_id, 0) + 1
                )
            else:
                self._candidate[slot_id] = occupied
                self._candidate_count[slot_id] = 1

            if (
                self._candidate_count[slot_id] >= self.confirmations
                and self._stable.get(slot_id) != occupied
            ):
                self._stable[slot_id] = occupied
                confirmed_changes[slot_id] = occupied

        display_state = {
            slot_id: self._stable.get(slot_id, occupied)
            for slot_id, occupied in observed.items()
        }
        return display_state, confirmed_changes


class VideoDetectionService:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.jobs: Dict[str, VideoDetectionJob] = {}
        self.yolo = YOLOService(
            model_path=settings.YOLO_MODEL_PATH,
            confidence=settings.YOLO_VIDEO_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD,
            iou=settings.YOLO_IOU_THRESHOLD,
            image_size=settings.YOLO_VIDEO_INFERENCE_SIZE,
        )

    def allocate_upload_path(self, suffix: str) -> Path:
        WORK_DIRECTORY.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return WORK_DIRECTORY / f"upload_{uuid4().hex}{suffix}"

    def probe_video(self, path: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        capture = cv2.VideoCapture(str(path))
        try:
            if not capture.isOpened():
                raise ValueError("OpenCV could not open this video")

            fps = float(capture.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS))
            frame_count = int(capture.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT))
            width = int(capture.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH))
            height = int(capture.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT))
            duration = frame_count / fps if fps > 0 else 0.0

            if width <= 0 or height <= 0:
                raise ValueError("The uploaded file has no readable video frames")

            return {
                "fps": fps if fps > 0 else 24.0,
                "frame_count": frame_count,
                "width": width,
                "height": height,
                "duration_seconds": duration,
            }
        finally:
            capture.release()

    def layout_path(self, layout_name: str) -> Path:
        safe_name = Path(layout_name).name
        if safe_name != layout_name:
            raise ValueError("Invalid layout name")
        if not safe_name.endswith(".json"):
            safe_name = f"{safe_name}.json"

        path = LAYOUT_DIRECTORY / safe_name
        if not path.is_file():
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown slot layout: {layout_name}")
        return path

    def layout_info(self, layout_name: str = DEFAULT_LAYOUT) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        path = self.layout_path(layout_name)
        with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as layout_file:
            return json.load(layout_file)

    def load_mapper(self, layout_name: str) -> SlotMapper:
        return SlotMapper.from_layout_file(
            self.layout_path(layout_name),
            overlap_threshold=settings.YOLO_SLOT_OVERLAP_THRESHOLD,
        )

    def create_job(
        self,
        *,
        source_path: Path,
        original_filename: str,
        area_id: UUID,
        floor: int,
        layout_name: str,
        repeat_count: int,
        update_database: bool,
        save_annotated_video: bool,
        warning: str | None = None,
    ) -> VideoDetectionJob:
        metadata = self.probe_video(source_path)
        job_id = uuid4().hex
        result_path = (
            WORK_DIRECTORY / f"annotated_{job_id}.mp4"
            if save_annotated_video
            else None
        )
        job = VideoDetectionJob(
            id=job_id,
            original_filename=original_filename,
            source_path=source_path,
            area_id=str(area_id),
            floor=floor,
            layout_name=layout_name,
            repeat_count=repeat_count,
            update_database=update_database,
            save_annotated_video=save_annotated_video,
            source_duration_seconds=metadata["duration_seconds"],
            effective_duration_seconds=(
                metadata["duration_seconds"] * repeat_count
            ),
            result_path=result_path,
            warning=warning,
        )
        self.jobs[job_id] = job
        return job

    def get_job(self, job_id: str) -> VideoDetectionJob | None:
        return self.jobs.get(job_id)

    def list_jobs(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        jobs = sorted(
            self.jobs.values(),
            key=lambda item: item.created_at,
            reverse=True,
        )
        return [job.public_data() for job in jobs]

    def request_stop(self, job_id: str) -> VideoDetectionJob | None:
        job = self.get_job(job_id)
        if job and job.status in {"queued", "processing"}:
            job.stop_requested = True
        return job

    def delete_job(self, job_id: str) -> bool:
        job = self.get_job(job_id)
        if job is None or job.status in {"queued", "processing"}:
            return False

        job.source_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        if job.result_path:
            job.result_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        self.jobs.pop(job_id, None)
        return True

    async def process_job(self, job_id: str) -> None:
        job = self.get_job(job_id)
        if job is None:
            return

        writer: cv2.VideoWriter | None = None
        job.status = "processing"
        job.started_at = _utc_now()

        try:
            metadata = self.probe_video(job.source_path)
            mapper = self.load_mapper(job.layout_name)
            stabilizer = _OccupancyStabilizer(
                settings.YOLO_OCCUPANCY_CONFIRMATIONS
            )

            source_fps = metadata["fps"]
            sample_interval = max(
                1,
                round(source_fps / max(settings.YOLO_VIDEO_SAMPLE_FPS, 0.1)),
            )
            output_fps = max(0.1, source_fps / sample_interval)
            source_frame_count = max(metadata["frame_count"], 1)
            expected_input_frames = source_frame_count * job.repeat_count

            if job.result_path:
                codec = cv2.VideoWriter_fourcc(*"mp4v")
                writer = cv2.VideoWriter(
                    str(job.result_path),
                    codec,
                    output_fps,
                    (metadata["width"], metadata["height"]),
                )
                if not writer.isOpened():
                    raise RuntimeError("Unable to create the annotated MP4")

            for _ in range(job.repeat_count):
                if job.stop_requested:
                    break

                capture = cv2.VideoCapture(str(job.source_path))
                if not capture.isOpened():
                    raise RuntimeError("Unable to reopen the uploaded video")

                try:
                    source_frame_index = 0

                    while not job.stop_requested:
                        ok, frame = capture.read()
                        if not ok:
                            break

                        job.input_frames_read += 1

                        if source_frame_index % sample_interval != 0:
                            source_frame_index += 1
                            continue

                        detections = await asyncio.to_thread(
                            self.yolo.detect,
                            frame,
                        )
                        observed = mapper.map_detections_to_slots(
                            detections,
                            frame.shape,
                        )
                        stable_occupancy, confirmed_changes = (
                            stabilizer.update(observed)
                        )
                        job.final_occupancy = stable_occupancy
                        job.sampled_frames_processed += 1
                        job.peak_vehicle_count = max(
                            job.peak_vehicle_count,
                            len(detections),
                        )

                        if job.update_database and confirmed_changes:
                            update_count, protected_count = (
                                await self._apply_confirmed_occupancy(
                                    area_id=UUID(job.area_id),
                                    floor=job.floor,
                                    changes=confirmed_changes,
                                )
                            )
                            job.database_updates += update_count
                            job.protected_status_skips += protected_count

                        if writer:
                            annotated = self.yolo.draw_detections(
                                frame,
                                detections,
                            )
                            annotated = mapper.draw_slots(
                                annotated,
                                stable_occupancy,
                            )
                            self._draw_summary(annotated, stable_occupancy)
                            writer.write(annotated)

                        job.progress = min(
                            99.9,
                            job.input_frames_read
                            / expected_input_frames
                            * 100.0,
                        )
                        source_frame_index += 1
                finally:
                    capture.release()

            job.status = "stopped" if job.stop_requested else "completed"
            if not job.stop_requested:
                job.progress = 100.0
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Video detection job %s failed", job.id)
            job.status = "failed"
            job.error = str(exc)
            if job.result_path:
                job.result_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        finally:
            if writer:
                writer.release()
            job.source_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            job.completed_at = _utc_now()

    async def _apply_confirmed_occupancy(
        self,
        *,
        area_id: UUID,
        floor: int,
        changes: Dict[str, bool],
    ) -> tuple[int, int]:
        updates: List[Any] = []
        protected_count = 0

        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
            slots = await get_slots_by_area(db, area_id, floor)
            slots_by_code = {str(slot.slot_id): slot for slot in slots}

            for slot_code, occupied in changes.items():
                slot = slots_by_code.get(slot_code)
                if slot is None:
                    continue

                current_status = str(slot.status).upper()

                if occupied and current_status == "VACANT":
                    slot.status = "OCCUPIED"
                    updates.append(slot)
                elif (
                    not occupied
                    and current_status == "OCCUPIED"
                    and settings.YOLO_ALLOW_AUTOMATIC_VACATE
                ):
                    slot.status = "VACANT"
                    updates.append(slot)
                elif (
                    (occupied and current_status not in {"OCCUPIED"})
                    or (not occupied and current_status == "OCCUPIED")
                ):
                    protected_count += 1

            if updates:
                await db.commit()

                for slot in updates:
                    await self._broadcast_slot(slot)

        return len(updates), protected_count

    async def _broadcast_slot(self, slot: Any) -> None:
        database_values = {
            column.name: getattr(slot, column.name)
            for column in slot.__table__.columns
        }
        payload = jsonable_encoder(database_values)
        payload.update(
            {
                self._to_camel_case(key): value
                for key, value in payload.items()
                if "_" in key
            }
        )
        payload["type"] = "slot_status_update"

        await manager.broadcast_slot_update(
            str(slot.parking_area_id),
            payload,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _to_camel_case(value: str) -> str:
        first, *remaining = value.split("_")
        return first + "".join(item.capitalize() for item in remaining)

    @staticmethod
    def _draw_summary(frame: Any, occupancy: Dict[str, bool]) -> None:
        occupied = sum(1 for value in occupancy.values() if value)
        total = len(occupancy)
        text = f"YOLOv11: {occupied} occupied / {total - occupied} vacant"
        cv2.rectangle(frame, (12, 12), (390, 48), (15, 15, 15), -1)
        cv2.putText(
            frame,
            text,
            (24, 38),
            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
            0.65,
            (255, 255, 255),
            2,
            cv2.LINE_AA,
        )


video_detection_service = VideoDetectionService()


===== backend/app/services/yolo_service.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import threading
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from typing import Any, Dict, List

import cv2
import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

VEHICLE_CLASS_NAMES = frozenset({"car", "motorcycle", "bus", "truck"})


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Detection:
    """One vehicle detected by YOLO."""

    bbox: List[float]  # [x1, y1, x2, y2]
    class_name: str
    confidence: float
    track_id: int = -1

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)


class YOLOService:
    """Lazy, thread-safe YOLOv11 vehicle detector.

    Loading is deliberately delayed until the first detection request. This
    keeps the rest of the backend available even when the model file is absent
    or Ultralytics cannot download it.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model_path: str,
        confidence: float = 0.5,
        iou: float = 0.45,
        image_size: int = 640,
    ) -> None:
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.confidence = confidence
        self.iou = iou
        self.image_size = image_size
        self._model: Any = None
        self._load_error: str | None = None
        self._load_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._inference_lock = threading.Lock()

    def load_model(self) -> None:
        if self._model is not None:
            return

        with self._load_lock:
            if self._model is not None:
                return

            try:
                from ultralytics import YOLO

                logger.info("Loading YOLO model from %s", self.model_path)
                self._model = YOLO(self.model_path)
                self._load_error = None
                logger.info("YOLO model loaded")
            except Exception as exc:
                self._load_error = str(exc)
                logger.exception("Unable to load YOLO model")
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "YOLO model could not be loaded. Confirm that ultralytics "
                    "is installed and YOLO_MODEL_PATH is valid."
                ) from exc

    def detect(self, frame: np.ndarray) -> List[Detection]:
        if frame is None or frame.size == 0:
            return []

        self.load_model()

        with self._inference_lock:
            results = self._model.predict(
                source=frame,
                conf=self.confidence,
                iou=self.iou,
                imgsz=self.image_size,
                verbose=False,
            )

        detections: List[Detection] = []

        for result in results:
            boxes = result.boxes
            if boxes is None or len(boxes) == 0:
                continue

            coordinates = boxes.xyxy.cpu().tolist()
            confidences = boxes.conf.cpu().tolist()
            class_ids = boxes.cls.int().cpu().tolist()
            track_ids = (
                boxes.id.int().cpu().tolist()
                if boxes.id is not None
                else [-1] * len(coordinates)
            )
            names = result.names

            for bbox, confidence, class_id, track_id in zip(
                coordinates,
                confidences,
                class_ids,
                track_ids,
            ):
                class_name = str(names.get(class_id, class_id)).lower()
                if class_name not in VEHICLE_CLASS_NAMES:
                    continue

                detections.append(
                    Detection(
                        bbox=[float(value) for value in bbox],
                        class_name=class_name,
                        confidence=float(confidence),
                        track_id=int(track_id),
                    )
                )

        return detections

    def draw_detections(
        self,
        frame: np.ndarray,
        detections: List[Detection],
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        output = frame.copy()

        for detection in detections:
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = (int(value) for value in detection.bbox)
            label = f"{detection.class_name} {detection.confidence:.2f}"

            cv2.rectangle(output, (x1, y1), (x2, y2), (255, 170, 0), 2)
            cv2.putText(
                output,
                label,
                (x1, max(18, y1 - 7)),
                cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
                0.5,
                (255, 255, 255),
                2,
                cv2.LINE_AA,
            )

        return output

    def get_model_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "model_path": self.model_path,
            "confidence_threshold": self.confidence,
            "iou_threshold": self.iou,
            "inference_image_size": self.image_size,
            "vehicle_classes": sorted(VEHICLE_CLASS_NAMES),
            "loaded": self.is_loaded,
            "load_error": self._load_error,
        }

    @property
    def is_loaded(self) -> bool:
        return self._model is not None

===== backend/app/utils/helpers.py =====
import qrcode
import base64
from io import BytesIO
from decimal import Decimal

def generate_qr_code(data: str) -> str:
    """Generate QR code and return as base64 PNG string."""
    qr = qrcode.QRCode(
        version=1,
        error_correction=qrcode.constants.ERROR_CORRECT_L,
        box_size=10,
        border=4,
    )
    qr.add_data(data)
    qr.make(fit=True)
    
    img = qr.make_image(fill_color="black", back_color="white")
    buffered = BytesIO()
    img.save(buffered, format="PNG")
    img_str = base64.b64encode(buffered.getvalue()).decode("utf-8")
    return img_str

def generate_slot_ids(prefix: str, count: int) -> list[str]:
    """Generate slot IDs like A01, A02."""
    return [f"{prefix}{i:02d}" for i in range(1, count + 1)]

def format_currency(amount: Decimal, currency: str = 'INR') -> str:
    if currency == 'INR':
        return f"₹{amount}"
    return f"{amount} {currency}"


===== backend/app/utils/redis_client.py =====
import json
import logging
from typing import Any, Optional
# Placeholder for redis client
import asyncio

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class RedisClient:

        self.redis = None # In real app, initialize aioredis here

===== user_app/lib/app.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/router/app_router.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/theme_provider.dart';

class SmartParkingApp extends ConsumerWidget {
  const SmartParkingApp({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final themeMode = ref.watch(themeProvider);
    final router = ref.watch(appRouterProvider);

    return MaterialApp.router(
      title: 'Smart Parking',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      themeMode: themeMode,
      theme: AppTheme.lightTheme(),
      darkTheme: AppTheme.darkTheme(),
      routerConfig: router,
    );
  }
}
