===== user_app/lib/core/constants/app_constants.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

class AppConstants {
  static const String _apiOverride = String.fromEnvironment(
    'API_BASE_URL',
  );

  static const String _webApiUrl = 'http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/v1';

  static const String _physicalDeviceApiUrl = 'http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/v1';

  static String get apiBaseUrl {
    final url = _apiOverride.isNotEmpty
        ? _apiOverride
        : kIsWeb
            ? _webApiUrl
            : _physicalDeviceApiUrl;

    // Prevent accidental double slashes in API endpoints.
    return url.endsWith('/') ? url.substring(0, url.length - 1) : url;
  }

  static String get wsBaseUrl {
    return apiBaseUrl
        .replaceFirst('http://', 'ws://')
        .replaceFirst('https://', 'wss://')
        .replaceFirst('/api/v1', '/ws');
  }

  static const int reservationTimeoutMinutes = 30;

  static const Duration animationDuration = Duration(
    milliseconds: 300,
  );
}

enum SlotStatus {
  VACANT,
  OCCUPIED,
  RESERVED,
  MAINTENANCE,
}

enum SlotType {
  STANDARD,
  VIP,
  EV,
  DISABLED,
}


===== user_app/lib/core/router/app_router.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/auth/forgot_password_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/auth/login_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/auth/register_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/dashboard/dashboard_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/notifications/notifications_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/reservation_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/navigation/indoor_navigation_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/parking_map/parking_map_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/payment/payment_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/profile/history_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/profile/profile_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/profile/settings_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/reservation/reservation_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/search/search_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/shared/main_scaffold.dart';

final appRouterProvider = Provider<GoRouter>((ref) {
  late final GoRouter router;

  router = GoRouter(
    initialLocation: '/login',
    redirect: (context, state) {
      final authState = ref.read(authStateProvider);
      final location = state.uri.path;

      final isAuthenticated = authState is AuthAuthenticated;
      final isChecking = authState is AuthInitial || authState is AuthLoading;

      final isPublicAuthPage = location == '/login' ||
          location == '/register' ||
          location == '/forgot-password';

      // Keep the user on login/register while an authentication
      // operation is running.
      if (isChecking) {
        if (isPublicAuthPage) {
          return null;
        }

        return '/login';
      }

      // Unauthenticated users can only open authentication pages.
      if (!isAuthenticated) {
        if (isPublicAuthPage) {
          return null;
        }

        return '/login';
      }

      // Authenticated users should not return to authentication pages.
      if (isAuthenticated && (isPublicAuthPage || location == '/')) {
        return '/dashboard';
      }

      return null;
    },
    routes: [
      GoRoute(
        path: '/',
        redirect: (context, state) => '/login',
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/login',
        builder: (context, state) => const LoginScreen(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/register',
        builder: (context, state) => const RegisterScreen(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/forgot-password',
        builder: (context, state) => const ForgotPasswordScreen(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/notifications',
        builder: (context, state) => const NotificationsScreen(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/payment',
        builder: (context, state) {
          final args = state.extra;
          if (args is! PaymentScreenArgs) {
            return const Scaffold(
              body: Center(
                child: Text('Payment details are missing.'),
              ),
            );
          }

          return PaymentScreen(args: args);
        },
      ),
      StatefulShellRoute.indexedStack(
        builder: (context, state, navigationShell) {
          return MainScaffold(
            navigationShell: navigationShell,
          );
        },
        branches: [
          StatefulShellBranch(
            routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: '/dashboard',
                builder: (context, state) => const DashboardScreen(),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          StatefulShellBranch(
            routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: '/parking-map',
                builder: (context, state) => const ParkingMapScreen(),
                routes: [
                  GoRoute(
                    path: ':areaId',
                    builder: (context, state) {
                      return ParkingMapScreen(
                        areaId: state.pathParameters['areaId'],
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
          StatefulShellBranch(
            routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: '/search',
                builder: (context, state) => const SearchScreen(),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          StatefulShellBranch(
            routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: '/reservations',
                builder: (context, state) => const ReservationScreen(),
                routes: [
                  GoRoute(
                    path: 'navigate',
                    builder: (context, state) {
                      final reservation = state.extra;
                      if (reservation is! ReservationModel) {
                        return const Scaffold(
                          body: Center(
                            child: Text('Navigation details are unavailable.'),
                          ),
                        );
                      }

                      return IndoorNavigationScreen(
                        reservation: reservation,
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
          StatefulShellBranch(
            routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: '/profile',
                builder: (context, state) => const ProfileScreen(),
                routes: [
                  GoRoute(
                    path: 'settings',
                    builder: (context, state) => const SettingsScreen(),
                  ),
                  GoRoute(
                    path: 'history',
                    builder: (context, state) => const HistoryScreen(),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    ],
  );

  // Refresh redirects without rebuilding and resetting the router.
  ref.listen(authStateProvider, (previous, next) {
    router.refresh();
  });

  ref.onDispose(router.dispose);

  return router;
});


===== user_app/lib/core/theme/app_theme.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/theme/typography.dart';

class AppTheme {
  static ThemeData lightTheme() {
    return ThemeData(
      useMaterial3: true,
      brightness: Brightness.light,
      colorScheme: ColorScheme.fromSeed(
        seedColor: AppColors.primary,
        brightness: Brightness.light,
        primary: AppColors.primary,
        secondary: AppColors.secondary,
        tertiary: AppColors.accent,
        surface: AppColors.cardLight,
        error: AppColors.error,
      ),
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceLight,
      textTheme: AppTypography.textTheme(Brightness.light),
      appBarTheme: AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        elevation: 0,
        centerTitle: true,
        iconTheme: const IconThemeData(color: AppColors.primaryDark),
        titleTextStyle:
            AppTypography.textTheme(Brightness.light).titleLarge?.copyWith(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  color: AppColors.primaryDark,
                ),
      ),
      cardTheme: CardThemeData(
        color: AppColors.cardLight,
        elevation: 0,
        shadowColor: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0xFFE3EAF5)),
        ),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
        filled: true,
        fillColor: AppColors.cardLight,
        contentPadding:
            const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 16),
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: Colors.grey.shade200),
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: Colors.grey.shade200),
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.primary, width: 2),
        ),
        errorBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.error),
        ),
      ),
      bottomNavigationBarTheme: const BottomNavigationBarThemeData(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.cardLight,
        selectedItemColor: AppColors.primary,
        unselectedItemColor: Colors.grey,
        type: BottomNavigationBarType.fixed,
        elevation: 8,
      ),
      navigationBarTheme: NavigationBarThemeData(
        height: 72,
        backgroundColor: AppColors.cardLight,
        indicatorColor: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.12),
        labelTextStyle: WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith(
          (states) => TextStyle(
            color: states.contains(WidgetState.selected)
                ? AppColors.primary
                : const Color(0xFF64748B),
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: states.contains(WidgetState.selected)
                ? FontWeight.w700
                : FontWeight.w500,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  static ThemeData darkTheme() {
    return ThemeData(
      useMaterial3: true,
      brightness: Brightness.dark,
      colorScheme: ColorScheme.fromSeed(
        seedColor: AppColors.primary,
        brightness: Brightness.dark,
        primary: AppColors.primary,
        secondary: AppColors.secondary,
        tertiary: AppColors.accent,
        surface: AppColors.cardDark,
        error: AppColors.error,
      ),
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceDark,
      textTheme: AppTypography.textTheme(Brightness.dark),
      appBarTheme: AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceDark,
        elevation: 0,
        centerTitle: true,
        iconTheme: const IconThemeData(color: Colors.white),
        titleTextStyle:
            AppTypography.textTheme(Brightness.dark).titleLarge?.copyWith(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
      ),
      cardTheme: CardThemeData(
        color: AppColors.cardDark,
        elevation: 0,
        shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.borderDark),
        ),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
        filled: true,
        fillColor: AppColors.cardDarkElevated,
        contentPadding:
            const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 16),
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.borderDark),
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.borderDark),
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.primary, width: 2),
        ),
        errorBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.error),
        ),
      ),
      bottomNavigationBarTheme: BottomNavigationBarThemeData(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.cardDark,
        selectedItemColor: AppColors.primary,
        unselectedItemColor: Colors.grey.shade500,
        type: BottomNavigationBarType.fixed,
        elevation: 8,
      ),
      dividerColor: AppColors.borderDark,
      navigationBarTheme: NavigationBarThemeData(
        height: 72,
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0B1A2E),
        indicatorColor: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.18),
        iconTheme: WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith(
          (states) => IconThemeData(
            color: states.contains(WidgetState.selected)
                ? AppColors.primaryLight
                : AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
          ),
        ),
        labelTextStyle: WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith(
          (states) => TextStyle(
            color: states.contains(WidgetState.selected)
                ? AppColors.primaryLight
                : AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: states.contains(WidgetState.selected)
                ? FontWeight.w700
                : FontWeight.w500,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/core/theme/typography.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:google_fonts/google_fonts.dart';

class AppTypography {
  static TextTheme textTheme(Brightness brightness) {
    final baseTextTheme = brightness == Brightness.light
        ? Typography.material2021().black
        : Typography.material2021().white;

    return TextTheme(
      displayLarge: GoogleFonts.outfit(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.displayLarge,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
      ),
      displayMedium: GoogleFonts.outfit(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.displayMedium,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
      ),
      displaySmall: GoogleFonts.outfit(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.displaySmall,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
      ),
      headlineLarge: GoogleFonts.outfit(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.headlineLarge,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
      ),
      headlineMedium: GoogleFonts.outfit(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.headlineMedium,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
      ),
      headlineSmall: GoogleFonts.outfit(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.headlineSmall,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
      ),
      titleLarge: GoogleFonts.outfit(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.titleLarge,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
      ),
      titleMedium: GoogleFonts.outfit(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.titleMedium,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
      ),
      titleSmall: GoogleFonts.outfit(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.titleSmall,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
      ),
      bodyLarge: GoogleFonts.inter(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.bodyLarge,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.normal,
      ),
      bodyMedium: GoogleFonts.inter(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.bodyMedium,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.normal,
      ),
      bodySmall: GoogleFonts.inter(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.bodySmall,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.normal,
      ),
      labelLarge: GoogleFonts.inter(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.labelLarge,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
      ),
      labelMedium: GoogleFonts.inter(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.labelMedium,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
      ),
      labelSmall: GoogleFonts.inter(
        textStyle: baseTextTheme.labelSmall,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/core/utils/extensions.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

extension BuildContextExtensions on BuildContext {
  ThemeData get theme => Theme.of(this);
  ColorScheme get colorScheme => theme.colorScheme;
  TextTheme get textTheme => theme.textTheme;
  Size get screenSize => MediaQuery.of(this).size;
  bool get isTablet => screenSize.width > 600;
  bool get isDarkMode => theme.brightness == Brightness.dark;
}

extension StringExtensions on String {
  String get capitalize {
    if (isEmpty) return this;
    return '${this[0].toUpperCase()}${substring(1)}';
  }

  bool get isValidEmail {
    return RegExp(r'^[\w-\.]+@([\w-]+\.)+[\w-]{2,4}$').hasMatch(this);
  }
}

extension DateTimeExtensions on DateTime {
  String get timeAgo {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final difference = now.difference(this);

    if (difference.inDays > 365)
      return '${(difference.inDays / 365).floor()} years ago';
    if (difference.inDays > 30)
      return '${(difference.inDays / 30).floor()} months ago';
    if (difference.inDays > 0) return '${difference.inDays} days ago';
    if (difference.inHours > 0) return '${difference.inHours} hours ago';
    if (difference.inMinutes > 0) return '${difference.inMinutes} minutes ago';
    return 'Just now';
  }

  String get formatDate {
    return DateFormat('MMM dd, yyyy').format(this);
  }

  String get formatTime {
    return DateFormat('hh:mm a').format(this);
  }
}

extension DoubleExtensions on double {
  String toCurrency({String symbol = '₹'}) {
    return NumberFormat.currency(symbol: symbol, decimalDigits: 0).format(this);
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/core/utils/validators.dart =====
class Validators {
  static String? validateEmail(String? value) {
    if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
      return 'Email is required';
    }
    final emailRegex = RegExp(r'^[\w-\.]+@([\w-]+\.)+[\w-]{2,4}$');
    if (!emailRegex.hasMatch(value)) {
      return 'Enter a valid email address';
    }
    return null;
  }

  static String? validatePassword(String? value) {
    if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
      return 'Password is required';
    }
    if (value.length < 8) {
      return 'Password must be at least 8 characters';
    }
    if (!value.contains(RegExp(r'[A-Z]'))) {
      return 'Password must contain at least one uppercase letter';
    }
    if (!value.contains(RegExp(r'[0-9]'))) {
      return 'Password must contain at least one number';
    }
    return null;
  }

  static String? validateName(String? value) {
    if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
      return 'Name is required';
    }
    if (value.length < 2) {
      return 'Name must be at least 2 characters';
    }
    return null;
  }

  static String? validatePhone(String? value) {
    if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
      return 'Phone number is required';
    }
    final phoneRegex = RegExp(r'^\+?[\d\s-]{10,}$');
    if (!phoneRegex.hasMatch(value)) {
      return 'Enter a valid phone number';
    }
    return null;
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/core/widgets/animated_counter.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_constants.dart';

class AnimatedCounter extends StatelessWidget {
  final num targetValue;
  final String prefix;
  final String suffix;
  final TextStyle? textStyle;
  final Duration duration;

  const AnimatedCounter({
    super.key,
    required this.targetValue,
    this.prefix = '',
    this.suffix = '',
    this.textStyle,
    this.duration = const Duration(seconds: 1),
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return TweenAnimationBuilder<num>(
      tween: Tween<num>(begin: 0, end: targetValue),
      duration: duration,
      curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
      builder: (context, value, child) {
        String formattedValue = targetValue is int
            ? value.toInt().toString()
            : value.toStringAsFixed(1);
        return Text(
          '$prefix$formattedValue$suffix',
          style: textStyle,
        );
      },
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/core/widgets/glass_card.dart =====
import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';

class GlassCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final double borderRadius;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry padding;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;

  const GlassCard({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.borderRadius = 20.0,
    this.padding = const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
    this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(borderRadius),
      child: BackdropFilter(
        filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 8, sigmaY: 8),
        child: Material(
          color: Colors.transparent,
          child: InkWell(
            onTap: onTap,
            child: Container(
              padding: padding,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(borderRadius),
                color: AppColors.cardDark.withOpacity(0.94),
                border: Border.all(color: AppColors.borderDark, width: 1),
              ),
              child: child,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/core/widgets/gradient_button.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';

class GradientButton extends StatefulWidget {
  final String text;
  final VoidCallback? onPressed;
  final bool isLoading;
  final IconData? icon;

  const GradientButton({
    super.key,
    required this.text,
    this.onPressed,
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.icon,
  });

  @override
  State<GradientButton> createState() => _GradientButtonState();
}

class _GradientButtonState extends State<GradientButton>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _controller;
  late Animation<double> _scaleAnimation;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _controller = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 150),
    );
    _scaleAnimation = Tween<double>(begin: 1.0, end: 0.95).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(parent: _controller, curve: Curves.easeInOut),
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTapDown: (_) => widget.onPressed != null ? _controller.forward() : null,
      onTapUp: (_) {
        if (widget.onPressed != null) {
          _controller.reverse();
          widget.onPressed!();
        }
      },
      onTapCancel: () =>
          widget.onPressed != null ? _controller.reverse() : null,
      child: ScaleTransition(
        scale: _scaleAnimation,
        child: AnimatedContainer(
          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
          width: double.infinity,
          height: 58,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
            gradient: widget.onPressed == null
                ? LinearGradient(
                    colors: [Colors.grey.shade400, Colors.grey.shade500])
                : AppColors.primaryGradient,
            boxShadow: [
              if (widget.onPressed != null)
                BoxShadow(
                  color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.3),
                  blurRadius: 20,
                  offset: const Offset(0, 8),
                ),
            ],
          ),
          child: Center(
            child: widget.isLoading
                ? const SizedBox(
                    height: 24,
                    width: 24,
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                        color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2),
                  )
                : Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      if (widget.icon != null) ...[
                        Icon(widget.icon, color: Colors.white, size: 20),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      ],
                      Text(
                        widget.text,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontSize: 16,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/core/widgets/premium_app_bar.dart =====
import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/utils/extensions.dart';

class PremiumAppBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final List<Widget>? actions;
  final Widget? leading;
  final double expandedHeight;
  final Widget? flexibleSpace;

  const PremiumAppBar({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    this.actions,
    this.leading,
    this.expandedHeight = 0,
    this.flexibleSpace,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = context.isDarkMode;

    return SliverAppBar(
      expandedHeight: expandedHeight,
      pinned: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      elevation: 0,
      leading: leading,
      flexibleSpace: ClipRRect(
        child: BackdropFilter(
          filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 15, sigmaY: 15),
          child: FlexibleSpaceBar(
            titlePadding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 16, bottom: 16),
            centerTitle: flexibleSpace == null,
            title: flexibleSpace == null
                ? ShaderMask(
                    shaderCallback: (bounds) =>
                        AppColors.primaryGradient.createShader(bounds),
                    child: Text(
                      title,
                      style: context.textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: Colors.white,
                      ),
                    ),
                  )
                : null,
            background: flexibleSpace != null
                ? Container(
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      gradient: LinearGradient(
                        begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                        end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                        colors: [
                          isDark
                              ? Colors.black.withOpacity(0.4)
                              : Colors.white.withOpacity(0.8),
                          isDark
                              ? Colors.black.withOpacity(0.8)
                              : Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: flexibleSpace,
                  )
                : Container(
                    color: isDark
                        ? Colors.black.withOpacity(0.7)
                        : Colors.white.withOpacity(0.7),
                  ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
      actions: actions,
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/core/widgets/shimmer_loading.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:shimmer/shimmer.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/utils/extensions.dart';

class ShimmerLoading extends StatelessWidget {
  final double width;
  final double height;
  final double borderRadius;

  const ShimmerLoading({
    super.key,
    required this.width,
    required this.height,
    this.borderRadius = 8.0,
  });

  static Widget card({double height = 150}) {
    return ShimmerLoading(
        width: double.infinity, height: height, borderRadius: 16);
  }

  static Widget listTile() {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8.0),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          const ShimmerLoading(width: 50, height: 50, borderRadius: 25),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: const [
                ShimmerLoading(width: double.infinity, height: 16),
                SizedBox(height: 8),
                ShimmerLoading(width: 150, height: 12),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static Widget grid({int crossAxisCount = 2, double childAspectRatio = 1.0}) {
    return GridView.builder(
      shrinkWrap: true,
      physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
      itemCount: 4,
      gridDelegate: SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
        crossAxisCount: crossAxisCount,
        childAspectRatio: childAspectRatio,
        crossAxisSpacing: 16,
        mainAxisSpacing: 16,
      ),
      itemBuilder: (context, index) => const ShimmerLoading(
          width: double.infinity, height: double.infinity, borderRadius: 12),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = context.isDarkMode;
    final baseColor = isDark ? Colors.grey[800]! : Colors.grey[300]!;
    final highlightColor = isDark ? Colors.grey[700]! : Colors.grey[100]!;

    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) {
        final resolvedWidth = width.isInfinite
            ? (constraints.hasBoundedWidth ? constraints.maxWidth : 280.0)
            : width;
        final resolvedHeight = height.isInfinite
            ? (constraints.hasBoundedHeight ? constraints.maxHeight : 120.0)
            : height;

        return Shimmer.fromColors(
          baseColor: baseColor,
          highlightColor: highlightColor,
          child: SizedBox(
            width: resolvedWidth,
            height: resolvedHeight,
            child: DecoratedBox(
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: baseColor,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(borderRadius),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/data/models/notification_model.dart =====
class NotificationModel {
  final String id;
  final String userId;
  final String title;
  final String body;
  final String type;
  final bool isRead;
  final Map<String, dynamic>? data;
  final DateTime createdAt;

  NotificationModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.userId,
    required this.title,
    required this.body,
    required this.type,
    this.isRead = false,
    this.data,
    required this.createdAt,
  });

  factory NotificationModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return NotificationModel(
      id: json['id'].toString(),
      userId: (json['user_id'] ?? json['userId'] ?? '').toString(),
      title: (json['title'] ?? '').toString(),
      body: (json['body'] ?? json['message'] ?? '').toString(),
      type: (json['type'] ?? 'info').toString(),
      isRead: (json['is_read'] ?? json['isRead'] ?? false) == true,
      data: json['data'] is Map
          ? Map<String, dynamic>.from(json['data'] as Map)
          : null,
      createdAt: DateTime.tryParse(
            (json['created_at'] ?? json['createdAt'] ?? '').toString(),
          ) ??
          DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'user_id': userId,
      'title': title,
      'body': body,
      'type': type,
      'is_read': isRead,
      'data': data,
      'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/data/models/parking_area_model.dart =====
class ParkingAreaModel {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String address;
  final double latitude;
  final double longitude;
  final int totalFloors;
  final int totalSlots;
  final double hourlyRate;
  final String currency;
  final String? imageUrl;
  final bool isActive;
  final String openingTime;
  final String closingTime;
  final int availableSlots;
  final int occupiedSlots;

  ParkingAreaModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.address,
    required this.latitude,
    required this.longitude,
    required this.totalFloors,
    required this.totalSlots,
    required this.hourlyRate,
    this.currency = '₹',
    this.imageUrl,
    this.isActive = true,
    required this.openingTime,
    required this.closingTime,
    this.availableSlots = 0,
    this.occupiedSlots = 0,
  });

  factory ParkingAreaModel.fromJson(
    Map<String, dynamic> json,
  ) {
    final data = _unwrapData(json);

    final currencyValue = data['currency']?.toString();

    return ParkingAreaModel(
      id: data['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      name: data['name']?.toString() ?? '',
      address: data['address']?.toString() ?? '',
      latitude: _toDouble(data['latitude']),
      longitude: _toDouble(data['longitude']),
      totalFloors: _toInt(
        data['total_floors'] ?? data['totalFloors'],
        fallback: 1,
      ),
      totalSlots: _toInt(
        data['total_slots'] ?? data['totalSlots'],
      ),
      hourlyRate: _toDouble(
        data['hourly_rate'] ?? data['hourlyRate'],
      ),
      currency:
          currencyValue == null || currencyValue.isEmpty ? '₹' : currencyValue,
      imageUrl: data['image_url']?.toString() ?? data['imageUrl']?.toString(),
      isActive: _toBool(
        data['is_active'] ?? data['isActive'],
        fallback: true,
      ),
      openingTime:
          (data['opening_time'] ?? data['openingTime'])?.toString() ?? '',
      closingTime:
          (data['closing_time'] ?? data['closingTime'])?.toString() ?? '',
      availableSlots: _toInt(
        data['available_slots'] ?? data['availableSlots'],
      ),
      occupiedSlots: _toInt(
        data['occupied_slots'] ?? data['occupiedSlots'],
      ),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'address': address,
      'latitude': latitude,
      'longitude': longitude,
      'total_floors': totalFloors,
      'total_slots': totalSlots,
      'hourly_rate': hourlyRate,
      'currency': currency,
      'image_url': imageUrl,
      'is_active': isActive,
      'opening_time': openingTime,
      'closing_time': closingTime,
      'available_slots': availableSlots,
      'occupied_slots': occupiedSlots,
    };
  }

  static Map<String, dynamic> _unwrapData(
    Map<String, dynamic> json,
  ) {
    final nestedData = json['data'];

    if (nestedData is Map) {
      return Map<String, dynamic>.from(nestedData);
    }

    return json;
  }

  static int _toInt(
    dynamic value, {
    int fallback = 0,
  }) {
    if (value is int) return value;
    if (value is num) return value.toInt();

    return int.tryParse(value?.toString() ?? '') ?? fallback;
  }

  static double _toDouble(dynamic value) {
    if (value is double) return value;
    if (value is num) return value.toDouble();

    return double.tryParse(value?.toString() ?? '') ?? 0;
  }

  static bool _toBool(
    dynamic value, {
    bool fallback = false,
  }) {
    if (value is bool) return value;
    if (value is num) return value != 0;

    final normalized = value?.toString().toLowerCase();

    if (normalized == 'true' || normalized == '1') {
      return true;
    }

    if (normalized == 'false' || normalized == '0') {
      return false;
    }

    return fallback;
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/data/models/parking_slot_model.dart =====
class ParkingSlotModel {
  final String id;
  final String slotId;
  final String parkingAreaId;
  final int floor;
  final String slotType;
  final String status;
  final int positionX;
  final int positionY;

  ParkingSlotModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.slotId,
    required this.parkingAreaId,
    required this.floor,
    required this.slotType,
    required this.status,
    required this.positionX,
    required this.positionY,
  });

  factory ParkingSlotModel.fromJson(
    Map<String, dynamic> json,
  ) {
    final data = _unwrapData(json);

    return ParkingSlotModel(
      id: data['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      slotId: (data['slot_id'] ?? data['slotId'])?.toString() ?? '',
      parkingAreaId:
          (data['parking_area_id'] ?? data['parkingAreaId'])?.toString() ?? '',
      floor: _toInt(data['floor'], fallback: 1),
      slotType: _normalizeEnum(
        data['slot_type'] ?? data['slotType'],
        fallback: 'STANDARD',
      ),
      status: _normalizeEnum(
        data['status'],
        fallback: 'VACANT',
      ),
      positionX: _toInt(
        data['position_x'] ?? data['positionX'],
      ),
      positionY: _toInt(
        data['position_y'] ?? data['positionY'],
      ),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'slot_id': slotId,
      'parking_area_id': parkingAreaId,
      'floor': floor,
      'slot_type': slotType,
      'status': status,
      'position_x': positionX,
      'position_y': positionY,
    };
  }

  static Map<String, dynamic> _unwrapData(
    Map<String, dynamic> json,
  ) {
    final nestedData = json['data'];

    if (nestedData is Map) {
      return Map<String, dynamic>.from(nestedData);
    }

    return json;
  }

  static int _toInt(
    dynamic value, {
    int fallback = 0,
  }) {
    if (value is int) return value;
    if (value is num) return value.toInt();

    return int.tryParse(value?.toString() ?? '') ?? fallback;
  }

  static String _normalizeEnum(
    dynamic value, {
    required String fallback,
  }) {
    final text = value?.toString().trim();

    if (text == null || text.isEmpty) {
      return fallback;
    }

    return text.split('.').last.toUpperCase();
  }
}

===== user_app/lib/data/models/reservation_model.dart =====
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/parking_slot_model.dart';

class ReservationModel {
  final String id;
  final String userId;
  final String slotId;
  final String parkingAreaId;
  final DateTime startTime;
  final DateTime endTime;
  final DateTime? actualCheckIn;
  final DateTime? actualCheckOut;
  final DateTime? createdAt;
  final String status;
  final String? qrCode;
  final double? totalAmount;
  final ParkingSlotModel? slotDetails;
  final String? areaName;

  ReservationModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.userId,
    required this.slotId,
    required this.parkingAreaId,
    required this.startTime,
    required this.endTime,
    this.actualCheckIn,
    this.actualCheckOut,
    this.createdAt,
    required this.status,
    this.qrCode,
    this.totalAmount,
    this.slotDetails,
    this.areaName,
  });

  factory ReservationModel.fromJson(
    Map<String, dynamic> json,
  ) {
    final data = _unwrapData(json);

    final startTime = _toDateTime(
          data['start_time'] ?? data['startTime'],
        ) ??
        DateTime.now();

    final rawSlot = data['slot'] ?? data['slot_details'];
    ParkingSlotModel? slotDetails;

    if (rawSlot is Map) {
      slotDetails = ParkingSlotModel.fromJson(
        Map<String, dynamic>.from(rawSlot),
      );
    }

    return ReservationModel(
      id: data['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      userId: (data['user_id'] ?? data['userId'])?.toString() ?? '',
      slotId: (data['slot_id'] ?? data['slotId'])?.toString() ?? '',
      parkingAreaId:
          (data['parking_area_id'] ?? data['parkingAreaId'])?.toString() ?? '',
      startTime: startTime,
      endTime: _toDateTime(
            data['end_time'] ?? data['endTime'],
          ) ??
          startTime,
      actualCheckIn: _toDateTime(
        data['actual_check_in'] ?? data['actualCheckIn'],
      ),
      actualCheckOut: _toDateTime(
        data['actual_check_out'] ?? data['actualCheckOut'],
      ),
      createdAt: _toDateTime(data['created_at'] ?? data['createdAt']),
      status: _normalizeStatus(data['status']),
      qrCode: (data['qr_code'] ?? data['qrCode'])?.toString(),
      totalAmount: _toNullableDouble(
        data['total_amount'] ?? data['totalAmount'],
      ),
      slotDetails: slotDetails,
      areaName:
          (data['parking_area_name'] ?? data['area_name'] ?? data['areaName'])
              ?.toString(),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'user_id': userId,
      'slot_id': slotId,
      'parking_area_id': parkingAreaId,
      'start_time': startTime.toIso8601String(),
      'end_time': endTime.toIso8601String(),
      'actual_check_in': actualCheckIn?.toIso8601String(),
      'actual_check_out': actualCheckOut?.toIso8601String(),
      'created_at': createdAt?.toIso8601String(),
      'status': status,
      'qr_code': qrCode,
      'total_amount': totalAmount,
      'slot': slotDetails?.toJson(),
      'parking_area_name': areaName,
    };
  }

  static Map<String, dynamic> _unwrapData(
    Map<String, dynamic> json,
  ) {
    final nestedData = json['data'];

    if (nestedData is Map) {
      return Map<String, dynamic>.from(nestedData);
    }

    return json;
  }

  static DateTime? _toDateTime(dynamic value) {
    if (value == null) return null;
    if (value is DateTime) return value;

    return DateTime.tryParse(value.toString());
  }

  static double? _toNullableDouble(dynamic value) {
    if (value == null) return null;
    if (value is double) return value;
    if (value is num) return value.toDouble();

    return double.tryParse(value.toString());
  }

  static String _normalizeStatus(dynamic value) {
    final status = value?.toString().trim();

    if (status == null || status.isEmpty) {
      return 'PENDING';
    }

    return status.split('.').last.toUpperCase();
  }
}

    required this.fullName,
    this.phone,
    this.avatarUrl,
    required this.role,
    this.isActive = true,
    this.isVerified = false,
    required this.createdAt,
  });

  factory UserModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$UserModelFromJson(json);

}

===== user_app/lib/data/repositories/auth_repository.dart =====
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/user_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/services/api_service.dart';

class AuthRepository {
  AuthRepository(this._apiService);

  final ApiService _apiService;

  Future<UserModel> login(String email, String password) async {
    final response = await _apiService.postForm(
      '/auth/login',
      data: {
        'username': email.trim().toLowerCase(),
        'password': password,
      },
    );
    await _saveTokens(response);
    return getCurrentUser();
  }

  Future<UserModel> register(
    String email,
    String password,
    String fullName,
    String phone,
  ) async {
    final response = await _apiService.post(
      '/auth/register',
      data: {
        'email': email.trim().toLowerCase(),
        'password': password,
        'full_name': fullName.trim(),
        'phone': phone.trim().isEmpty ? null : phone.trim(),
      },
    );
    await _saveTokens(response);
    return getCurrentUser();
  }

  Future<void> _saveTokens(dynamic response) async {
    if (response is! Map || response['access_token'] == null) {
      throw AppException('The backend returned an invalid login response');
    }
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setString('auth_token', response['access_token'].toString());
    if (response['refresh_token'] != null) {
      await prefs.setString(
        'auth_refresh_token',
        response['refresh_token'].toString(),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> logout() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.remove('auth_token');
    await prefs.remove('auth_refresh_token');
  }

  Future<UserModel> getCurrentUser() async {
    final response = await _apiService.get('/auth/me');
    if (response is! Map) {
      throw AppException('The backend returned invalid user data');
    }
    return UserModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(response));
  }

  Future<void> forgotPassword(String email) async {
    await _apiService.post(
      '/auth/forgot-password',
      data: {'email': email.trim().toLowerCase()},
    );
  }

  Future<String?> getToken() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    return prefs.getString('auth_token');
  }

  Future<bool> isLoggedIn() async {
    final token = await getToken();
    return token != null && token.isNotEmpty;
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/data/repositories/notification_repository.dart =====
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/notification_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/services/api_service.dart';

class NotificationRepository {
  final ApiService _apiService;

  NotificationRepository(this._apiService);

  Future<List<NotificationModel>> getNotifications() async {
    final response = await _apiService.get('/notifications/');
    final dynamic payload = response is Map && response['data'] != null
        ? response['data']
        : response;
    if (payload is! List) return const [];

    return payload
        .whereType<Map>()
        .map((json) => NotificationModel.fromJson(
              Map<String, dynamic>.from(json),
            ))
        .toList();
  }

  Future<void> markAsRead(String id) async {
    await _apiService.put('/notifications/$id/read');
  }

  Future<void> markAllAsRead() async {
    await _apiService.put('/notifications/read-all');
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/data/repositories/parking_repository.dart =====
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/parking_area_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/parking_slot_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/services/api_service.dart';

class ParkingRepository {
  final ApiService _apiService;

  ParkingRepository(this._apiService);

  Future<List<ParkingAreaModel>> getAreas() async {
    final response = await _apiService.get('/parking/areas');
    final items = _extractList(response, 'parking areas');

    return items.map((item) {
      if (item is! Map) {
        throw AppException('Invalid parking area received');
      }

      return ParkingAreaModel.fromJson(
        Map<String, dynamic>.from(item),
      );
    }).toList();
  }

  Future<ParkingAreaModel> getAreaById(
    String areaId,
  ) async {
    final response = await _apiService.get(
      '/parking/areas/$areaId',
    );

    return ParkingAreaModel.fromJson(
      _extractMap(response, 'parking area'),
    );
  }

  Future<List<ParkingSlotModel>> getSlotsByArea(
    String areaId, {
    int? floor,
  }) async {
    final query = floor == null
        ? null
        : <String, dynamic>{
            'floor': floor,
          };

    final response = await _apiService.get(
      '/parking/areas/$areaId/slots',
      queryParameters: query,
    );

    final items = _extractList(response, 'parking slots');

    return items.map((item) {
      if (item is! Map) {
        throw AppException('Invalid parking slot received');
      }

      return ParkingSlotModel.fromJson(
        Map<String, dynamic>.from(item),
      );
    }).toList();
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> getAreaSummary(
    String areaId,
  ) async {
    final response = await _apiService.get(
      '/parking/areas/$areaId/summary',
    );

    final floors = _extractList(
      response,
      'parking summary',
    ).map((item) {
      if (item is! Map) {
        throw AppException('Invalid floor summary received');
      }

      return Map<String, dynamic>.from(item);
    }).toList();

    var total = 0;
    var vacant = 0;
    var occupied = 0;
    var reserved = 0;

    for (final floor in floors) {
      total += _toInt(floor['total']);
      vacant += _toInt(floor['vacant']);
      occupied += _toInt(floor['occupied']);
      reserved += _toInt(floor['reserved']);
    }

    return {
      'floors': floors,
      'total': total,
      'vacant': vacant,
      'occupied': occupied,
      'reserved': reserved,
      'total_slots': total,
      'available_slots': vacant,
      'occupied_slots': occupied,
      'reserved_slots': reserved,
    };
  }

  List<dynamic> _extractList(
    dynamic response,
    String label,
  ) {
    if (response is List) {
      return response;
    }

    if (response is Map) {
      final data = response['data'];

      if (data is List) {
        return data;
      }

      final items = response['items'];

      if (items is List) {
        return items;
      }
    }

    throw AppException('Invalid $label response from server');
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> _extractMap(
    dynamic response,
    String label,
  ) {
    if (response is Map) {
      final data = response['data'];

      if (data is Map) {
        return Map<String, dynamic>.from(data);
      }

      return Map<String, dynamic>.from(response);
    }

    throw AppException('Invalid $label response from server');
  }

  int _toInt(dynamic value) {
    if (value is int) return value;
    if (value is num) return value.toInt();

    return int.tryParse(value?.toString() ?? '') ?? 0;
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/data/repositories/payment_repository.dart =====
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/payment_order_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/services/api_service.dart';

class PaymentRepository {
  PaymentRepository(this._apiService);

  final ApiService _apiService;

  Future<PaymentOrderModel> createOrder({
    required String reservationId,
    required int amount,
    String currency = 'INR',
    String purpose = 'ADVANCE',
  }) async {
    final response = await _apiService.post(
      '/payments/create-order',
      data: {
        'reservation_id': reservationId,
        'amount': amount,
        'currency': currency,
        'purpose': purpose,
      },
    );

    return PaymentOrderModel.fromJson(response as Map<String, dynamic>);
  }

  Future<void> verifyPayment({
    required String paymentId,
    required String orderId,
    required String signature,
  }) async {
    await _apiService.post(
      '/payments/verify-payment',
      data: {
        'razorpay_payment_id': paymentId,
        'razorpay_order_id': orderId,
        'razorpay_signature': signature,
      },
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/data/repositories/reservation_repository.dart =====
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/reservation_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/services/api_service.dart';

class ReservationRepository {
  final ApiService _apiService;

  ReservationRepository(this._apiService);

  Future<ReservationModel> createReservation(
    String slotId,
    String areaId,
    DateTime startTime,
    DateTime endTime,
  ) async {
    final response = await _apiService.post(
      '/reservations/',
      data: {
        'slot_id': slotId,
        'parking_area_id': areaId,
        'start_time': startTime.toIso8601String(),
        'end_time': endTime.toIso8601String(),
      },
    );

    return ReservationModel.fromJson(
      _extractMap(response, 'reservation'),
    );
  }

  Future<List<ReservationModel>> getReservations({
    String? status,
  }) async {
    final query = status == null
        ? null
        : <String, dynamic>{
            'status': status,
          };

    final response = await _apiService.get(
      '/reservations/',
      queryParameters: query,
    );

    final items = _extractList(
      response,
      'reservations',
    );

    return items.map((item) {
      if (item is! Map) {
        throw AppException('Invalid reservation received');
      }

      return ReservationModel.fromJson(
        Map<String, dynamic>.from(item),
      );
    }).toList();
  }

  Future<ReservationModel?> getActiveReservation() async {
    final response = await _apiService.get(
      '/reservations/active',
    );

    if (response == null) {
      return null;
    }

    if (response is Map &&
        response.containsKey('data') &&
        response['data'] == null) {
      return null;
    }

    return ReservationModel.fromJson(
      _extractMap(response, 'active reservation'),
    );
  }

  Future<ReservationModel> getReservationById(
    String id,
  ) async {
    final response = await _apiService.get(
      '/reservations/$id',
    );

    return ReservationModel.fromJson(
      _extractMap(response, 'reservation'),
    );
  }

  Future<ReservationModel> cancelReservation(
    String id,
  ) async {
    final response = await _apiService.put(
      '/reservations/$id/cancel',
    );

    return ReservationModel.fromJson(
      _extractMap(response, 'reservation'),
    );
  }

  Future<ReservationModel> checkIn(
    String id,
  ) async {
    final response = await _apiService.put(
      '/reservations/$id/check-in',
    );

    return ReservationModel.fromJson(
      _extractMap(response, 'reservation'),
    );
  }

  Future<ReservationModel> checkOut(
    String id,
  ) async {
    final response = await _apiService.put(
      '/reservations/$id/check-out',
    );

    return ReservationModel.fromJson(
      _extractMap(response, 'reservation'),
    );
  }

  List<dynamic> _extractList(
    dynamic response,
    String label,
  ) {
    if (response is List) {
      return response;
    }

    if (response is Map) {
      final data = response['data'];

      if (data is List) {
        return data;
      }

      final items = response['items'];

      if (items is List) {
        return items;
      }
    }

    throw AppException('Invalid $label response from server');
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> _extractMap(
    dynamic response,
    String label,
  ) {
    if (response is Map) {
      final data = response['data'];

      if (data is Map) {
        return Map<String, dynamic>.from(data);
      }

      return Map<String, dynamic>.from(response);
    }

    throw AppException('Invalid $label response from server');
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/data/services/api_service.dart =====
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_constants.dart';

class AppException implements Exception {
  final String message;

  AppException(this.message);

  @override
  String toString() => message;
}

class ApiService {
  late final Dio _dio;

  ApiService() {
    _dio = Dio(
      BaseOptions(
        baseUrl: AppConstants.apiBaseUrl,
        connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 15),
        receiveTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 15),
        headers: const {'Accept': 'application/json'},
      ),
    );

    _dio.interceptors.add(
      InterceptorsWrapper(
        onRequest: (options, handler) async {
          final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
          final token = prefs.getString('auth_token');

          if (token != null && token.isNotEmpty) {
            options.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $token';
          }

          return handler.next(options);
        },
        onError: (DioException e, handler) async {
          if (e.response?.statusCode == 401 &&
              !e.requestOptions.path.endsWith('/auth/login')) {
            final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
            await prefs.remove('auth_token');
          }

          return handler.next(e);
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<dynamic> get(
    String path, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? queryParameters,
  }) async {
    try {
      final response = await _dio.get(
        path,
        queryParameters: queryParameters,
      );

      return response.data;
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<dynamic> post(
    String path, {
    dynamic data,
  }) async {
    try {
      final response = await _dio.post(
        path,
        data: data,
      );

      return response.data;
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  // FastAPI OAuth2 login requires application/x-www-form-urlencoded
  Future<dynamic> postForm(
    String path, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? data,
  }) async {
    try {
      final response = await _dio.post(
        path,
        data: data,
        options: Options(
          contentType: Headers.formUrlEncodedContentType,
        ),
      );

      return response.data;
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<dynamic> put(
    String path, {
    dynamic data,
  }) async {
    try {
      final response = await _dio.put(
        path,
        data: data,
      );

      return response.data;
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<dynamic> delete(String path) async {
    try {
      final response = await _dio.delete(path);

      return response.data;
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  AppException _handleError(DioException e) {
    if (e.type == DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout ||
        e.type == DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout ||
        e.type == DioExceptionType.connectionError) {
      return AppException(
        'Cannot reach ${AppConstants.apiBaseUrl}. Start the backend on port 8000.',
      );
    }

    if (e.response != null) {
      final data = e.response?.data;

      if (data is Map && data['detail'] != null) {
        return AppException(data['detail'].toString());
      }

      if (data is Map && data['message'] != null) {
        return AppException(data['message'].toString());
      }

      return AppException(
        'Request failed (${e.response?.statusCode})',
      );
    }

    return AppException('Network error');
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/data/services/websocket_service.dart =====
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_constants.dart';
import 'package:web_socket_channel/web_socket_channel.dart';

class WebSocketService {
  final String areaId;

  WebSocketChannel? _channel;
  StreamSubscription<dynamic>? _subscription;

  final StreamController<Map<String, dynamic>> _controller =
      StreamController<Map<String, dynamic>>.broadcast();

  int _reconnectAttempts = 0;

  Timer? _reconnectTimer;
  Timer? _heartbeatTimer;

  bool _isDisposed = false;

  WebSocketService({
    required this.areaId,
  }) {
    _connect();
  }

  Stream<Map<String, dynamic>> get stream {
    return _controller.stream;
  }

  bool get isConnected {
    return _channel != null && !_isDisposed;
  }

  void _connect() {
    if (_isDisposed || _channel != null) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      final uri = Uri.parse(
        '${AppConstants.wsBaseUrl}/parking/$areaId',
      );

      debugPrint('Connecting WebSocket: $uri');

      final channel = WebSocketChannel.connect(uri);
      _channel = channel;

      _subscription = channel.stream.listen(
        (message) {
          _reconnectAttempts = 0;

          final rawMessage = message.toString();

          // FastAPI heartbeat response is plain text.
          if (rawMessage == 'pong') {
            debugPrint('WebSocket heartbeat received');
            return;
          }

          try {
            final decoded = jsonDecode(rawMessage);

            if (decoded is Map) {
              if (!_controller.isClosed) {
                _controller.add(
                  Map<String, dynamic>.from(decoded),
                );
              }
            } else {
              debugPrint(
                'WebSocket ignored non-map message: $decoded',
              );
            }
          } catch (error) {
            debugPrint(
              'WebSocket message parse error: $error',
            );
          }
        },
        onDone: _handleDisconnect,
        onError: (Object error) {
          debugPrint('WebSocket error: $error');
          _handleDisconnect();
        },
        cancelOnError: false,
      );

      _startHeartbeat();
    } catch (error) {
      debugPrint('WebSocket connection error: $error');
      _handleDisconnect();
    }
  }

  void _handleDisconnect() {
    _subscription?.cancel();
    _subscription = null;

    _channel?.sink.close();
    _channel = null;

    _heartbeatTimer?.cancel();
    _heartbeatTimer = null;

    if (!_isDisposed) {
      _scheduleReconnect();
    }
  }

  void _scheduleReconnect() {
    if (_isDisposed || (_reconnectTimer?.isActive ?? false)) {
      return;
    }

    const backoffSeconds = [1, 2, 4, 8, 30];

    final index = _reconnectAttempts < backoffSeconds.length
        ? _reconnectAttempts
        : backoffSeconds.length - 1;

    final delay = backoffSeconds[index];
    _reconnectAttempts++;

    debugPrint(
      'Reconnecting WebSocket in $delay seconds',
    );

    _reconnectTimer = Timer(
      Duration(seconds: delay),
      () {
        _reconnectTimer = null;
        _connect();
      },
    );
  }

  void _startHeartbeat() {
    _heartbeatTimer?.cancel();

    _heartbeatTimer = Timer.periodic(
      const Duration(seconds: 30),
      (timer) {
        if (!isConnected) {
          return;
        }

        try {
          // FastAPI expects plain-text "ping".
          _channel?.sink.add('ping');
        } catch (error) {
          debugPrint(
            'WebSocket heartbeat error: $error',
          );

          _handleDisconnect();
        }
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> disconnect() async {
    if (_isDisposed) {
      return;
    }

    _isDisposed = true;

    _reconnectTimer?.cancel();
    _reconnectTimer = null;

    _heartbeatTimer?.cancel();
    _heartbeatTimer = null;

    await _subscription?.cancel();
    _subscription = null;

    await _channel?.sink.close();
    _channel = null;

    if (!_controller.isClosed) {
      await _controller.close();
    }
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/domain/models/parking_slot.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';

enum DomainSlotStatus {
  vacant('VACANT'),
  occupied('OCCUPIED'),
  reserved('RESERVED'),
  maintenance('MAINTENANCE');

  final String value;
  const DomainSlotStatus(this.value);

  Color get color {
    switch (this) {
      case DomainSlotStatus.vacant:
        return AppColors.slotVacant;
      case DomainSlotStatus.occupied:
        return AppColors.slotOccupied;
      case DomainSlotStatus.reserved:
        return AppColors.slotReserved;
      case DomainSlotStatus.maintenance:
        return AppColors.slotMaintenance;
    }
  }

  String get label {
    switch (this) {
      case DomainSlotStatus.vacant:
        return 'Available';
      case DomainSlotStatus.occupied:
        return 'Occupied';
      case DomainSlotStatus.reserved:
        return 'Reserved';
      case DomainSlotStatus.maintenance:
        return 'Maintenance';
    }
  }

  static DomainSlotStatus fromString(String status) {
    return DomainSlotStatus.values.firstWhere(
      (e) => e.value == status,
      orElse: () => DomainSlotStatus.maintenance,
    );
  }
}

enum DomainSlotType {
  standard('STANDARD'),
  vip('VIP'),
  ev('EV'),
  disabled('DISABLED');

  final String value;
  const DomainSlotType(this.value);

  IconData get icon {
    switch (this) {
      case DomainSlotType.standard:
        return Icons.directions_car;
      case DomainSlotType.vip:
        return Icons.star;
      case DomainSlotType.ev:
        return Icons.electric_car;
      case DomainSlotType.disabled:
        return Icons.accessible;
    }
  }

  String get label {
    switch (this) {
      case DomainSlotType.standard:
        return 'Standard';
      case DomainSlotType.vip:
        return 'VIP';
      case DomainSlotType.ev:
        return 'EV Charging';
      case DomainSlotType.disabled:
        return 'Accessible';
    }
  }

  static DomainSlotType fromString(String type) {
    return DomainSlotType.values.firstWhere(
      (e) => e.value == type,
      orElse: () => DomainSlotType.standard,
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/domain/providers/auth_provider.dart =====
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/user_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/repositories/auth_repository.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/services/api_service.dart';

final apiServiceProvider = Provider<ApiService>((ref) => ApiService());

final authRepositoryProvider = Provider<AuthRepository>((ref) {
  return AuthRepository(ref.watch(apiServiceProvider));
});

sealed class AuthState {
  const AuthState();
}

class AuthInitial extends AuthState {
  const AuthInitial();
}

class AuthLoading extends AuthState {
  const AuthLoading();
}

class AuthAuthenticated extends AuthState {
  const AuthAuthenticated(this.user);

  final UserModel user;
}

class AuthUnauthenticated extends AuthState {
  const AuthUnauthenticated();
}

class AuthError extends AuthState {
  const AuthError(this.message);

  final String message;
}

class AuthNotifier extends StateNotifier<AuthState> {
  AuthNotifier(this._repository) : super(const AuthInitial()) {
    checkAuthStatus();
  }

  final AuthRepository _repository;

  Future<void> checkAuthStatus() async {
    try {
      if (!await _repository.isLoggedIn()) {
        state = const AuthUnauthenticated();
        return;
      }
      final user = await _repository.getCurrentUser();
      state = AuthAuthenticated(user);
    } catch (_) {
      await _repository.logout();
      state = const AuthUnauthenticated();
    }
  }

  Future<bool> login(String email, String password) async {
    state = const AuthLoading();
    try {
      final user = await _repository.login(email, password);
      state = AuthAuthenticated(user);
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      state = AuthError(_cleanMessage(error));
      return false;
    }
  }

  Future<bool> register(
    String email,
    String password,
    String fullName,
    String phone,
  ) async {
    state = const AuthLoading();
    try {
      final user = await _repository.register(
        email,
        password,
        fullName,
        phone,
      );
      state = AuthAuthenticated(user);
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      state = AuthError(_cleanMessage(error));
      return false;
    }
  }

  Future<void> logout() async {
    await _repository.logout();
    state = const AuthUnauthenticated();
  }

  String _cleanMessage(Object error) {
    return error
        .toString()
        .replaceFirst('Exception: ', '')
        .replaceFirst('Bad state: ', '');
  }
}

final authStateProvider = StateNotifierProvider<AuthNotifier, AuthState>((ref) {
  return AuthNotifier(ref.watch(authRepositoryProvider));
});

final currentUserProvider = Provider<UserModel?>((ref) {
  final state = ref.watch(authStateProvider);
  return state is AuthAuthenticated ? state.user : null;
});

final isAuthenticatedProvider = Provider<bool>((ref) {
  return ref.watch(authStateProvider) is AuthAuthenticated;
});


===== user_app/lib/domain/providers/notification_provider.dart =====
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/notification_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/repositories/notification_repository.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/auth_provider.dart';

final notificationRepositoryProvider = Provider<NotificationRepository>((ref) {
  return NotificationRepository(ref.watch(apiServiceProvider));
});

final notificationsProvider = FutureProvider<List<NotificationModel>>((ref) {
  return ref.watch(notificationRepositoryProvider).getNotifications();
});

final hasUnreadNotificationsProvider = Provider<bool>((ref) {
  return ref.watch(notificationsProvider).maybeWhen(
        data: (notifications) => notifications.any((item) => !item.isRead),
        orElse: () => false,
      );
});

===== user_app/lib/domain/providers/reservation_provider.dart =====
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/reservation_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/repositories/reservation_repository.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/auth_provider.dart';

final reservationRepositoryProvider = Provider<ReservationRepository>((ref) {
  return ReservationRepository(ref.watch(apiServiceProvider));
});

final activeReservationProvider =
    FutureProvider<ReservationModel?>((ref) async {
  final repository = ref.watch(reservationRepositoryProvider);
  return repository.getActiveReservation();
});

final reservationsListProvider =
    FutureProvider<List<ReservationModel>>((ref) async {
  final repository = ref.watch(reservationRepositoryProvider);
  return repository.getReservations();
});


===== user_app/lib/domain/providers/theme_provider.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

class ThemeNotifier extends StateNotifier<ThemeMode> {
  ThemeNotifier() : super(ThemeMode.dark) {
    _loadTheme();
  }

  Future<void> _loadTheme() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final isDark = prefs.getBool('isDarkMode');
    if (isDark != null) {
      state = isDark ? ThemeMode.dark : ThemeMode.light;
    }
  }

  Future<void> toggle() async {
    state = state == ThemeMode.light ? ThemeMode.dark : ThemeMode.light;
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setBool('isDarkMode', state == ThemeMode.dark);
  }

  Future<void> setDark() async {
    state = ThemeMode.dark;
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setBool('isDarkMode', true);
  }

  Future<void> setLight() async {
    state = ThemeMode.light;
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setBool('isDarkMode', false);
  }
}

final themeProvider = StateNotifierProvider<ThemeNotifier, ThemeMode>((ref) {
  return ThemeNotifier();
});


===== user_app/lib/domain/providers/websocket_provider.dart =====
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/services/websocket_service.dart';

final webSocketProvider =
    StreamProvider.family<Map<String, dynamic>, String>((ref, areaId) {
  final wsService = WebSocketService(areaId: areaId);

  ref.onDispose(() {
    wsService.disconnect();
  });

  return wsService.stream;
});
