===== user_app/lib/ui/auth/forgot_password_screen.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_animate/flutter_animate.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/utils/validators.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/glass_card.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/gradient_button.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/auth/widgets/auth_text_field.dart';

class ForgotPasswordScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const ForgotPasswordScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<ForgotPasswordScreen> createState() =>
      _ForgotPasswordScreenState();
}

class _ForgotPasswordScreenState extends ConsumerState<ForgotPasswordScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool _isSuccess = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _emailController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _sendResetLink() async {
    if (_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      setState(() => _isLoading = true);
      try {
        await ref
            .read(authRepositoryProvider)
            .forgotPassword(_emailController.text.trim());
        setState(() {
          _isLoading = false;
          _isSuccess = true;
        });
      } catch (e) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
        if (!mounted) return;
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
              content: Text(e.toString()), backgroundColor: AppColors.error),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: Container(
        decoration: const BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            begin: Alignment.topLeft,
            end: Alignment.bottomRight,
            colors: [Color(0xFF0F172A), Color(0xFF1E293B)],
          ),
        ),
        child: SafeArea(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Align(
                  alignment: Alignment.topLeft,
                  child: IconButton(
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: Colors.white),
                    onPressed: () => context.pop(),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 40),
                GlassCard(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
                  child: _isSuccess ? _buildSuccess() : _buildForm(),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildForm() {
    return Form(
      key: _formKey,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text(
            'Reset Password',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 28,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              fontFamily: 'Outfit',
              color: Colors.white,
            ),
          ).animate().slideX(begin: -0.2, end: 0, duration: 400.ms).fadeIn(),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            'Enter your email address and we will send you a link to reset your password.',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 16,
              color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.7),
            ),
          )
              .animate()
              .slideX(begin: -0.2, end: 0, delay: 100.ms, duration: 400.ms)
              .fadeIn(),
          const SizedBox(height: 32),
          AuthTextField(
            label: 'Email',
            prefixIcon: Icons.email_outlined,
            controller: _emailController,
            keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
            validator: Validators.validateEmail,
          ).animate().slideY(begin: 0.2, end: 0, delay: 200.ms).fadeIn(),
          const SizedBox(height: 32),
          GradientButton(
            text: 'Send Reset Link',
            isLoading: _isLoading,
            onPressed: _sendResetLink,
          ).animate().slideY(begin: 0.2, end: 0, delay: 300.ms).fadeIn(),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSuccess() {
    return Column(
      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
      children: [
        const SizedBox(height: 24),
        Container(
          width: 80,
          height: 80,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: AppColors.success.withOpacity(0.2),
            shape: BoxShape.circle,
          ),
          child: const Center(
            child: Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: AppColors.success, size: 48),
          ),
        ).animate().scale(curve: Curves.elasticOut, duration: 800.ms),
        const SizedBox(height: 24),
        const Text(
          'Email Sent!',
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 24,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            fontFamily: 'Outfit',
            color: Colors.white,
          ),
        ).animate().fadeIn(delay: 300.ms),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        Text(
          'We have sent a password reset link to ${_emailController.text}',
          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 16,
            color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.7),
          ),
        ).animate().fadeIn(delay: 500.ms),
        const SizedBox(height: 32),
        GradientButton(
          text: 'Back to Login',
          onPressed: () => context.pop(),
        ).animate().fadeIn(delay: 700.ms),
      ],
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/auth/login_screen.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_animate/flutter_animate.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/utils/validators.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/glass_card.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/gradient_button.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/auth/widgets/auth_text_field.dart';

class LoginScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const LoginScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<LoginScreen> createState() => _LoginScreenState();
}

class _LoginScreenState extends ConsumerState<LoginScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _emailController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _login() async {
    final isValid = _formKey.currentState?.validate() ?? false;

    if (!isValid) return;

    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();

    // Read the notifier before the asynchronous operation.
    final authNotifier = ref.read(authStateProvider.notifier);

    try {
      final success = await authNotifier.login(
        _emailController.text.trim(),
        _passwordController.text,
      );

      // The router may have already removed this screen.
      if (!mounted) return;

      if (success) {
        context.go('/dashboard');
        return;
      }

      // It is safe to use ref because mounted was checked above.
      final authState = ref.read(authStateProvider);

      if (authState is AuthError) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text(authState.message),
            backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
          ),
        );
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (!mounted) return;

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Unable to sign in. Please try again.'),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final authState = ref.watch(authStateProvider);
    final isLoading = authState is AuthLoading;

    return Scaffold(
      body: Container(
        decoration: const BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            begin: Alignment.topLeft,
            end: Alignment.bottomRight,
            colors: [
              Color(0xFF0F172A),
              Color(0xFF1E293B),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        child: SafeArea(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                const SizedBox(height: 40),

                // Logo
                Center(
                  child: Container(
                    width: 80,
                    height: 80,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.primary,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                    ),
                    child: const Center(
                      child: Text(
                        'P',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 48,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Colors.white,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ).animate().scale(
                      duration: 500.ms,
                      curve: Curves.easeOutBack,
                    ),

                const SizedBox(height: 40),

                GlassCard(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                  child: Form(
                    key: _formKey,
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        const Text(
                          'Welcome Back',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 28,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            fontFamily: 'Outfit',
                            color: Colors.white,
                          ),
                        )
                            .animate()
                            .slideX(
                              begin: -0.2,
                              end: 0,
                              duration: 400.ms,
                            )
                            .fadeIn(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        Text(
                          'Sign in to continue',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 16,
                            color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.7),
                          ),
                        )
                            .animate()
                            .slideX(
                              begin: -0.2,
                              end: 0,
                              delay: 100.ms,
                              duration: 400.ms,
                            )
                            .fadeIn(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 32),
                        AuthTextField(
                          label: 'Email',
                          prefixIcon: Icons.email_outlined,
                          controller: _emailController,
                          keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                          validator: Validators.validateEmail,
                        )
                            .animate()
                            .slideY(
                              begin: 0.2,
                              end: 0,
                              delay: 200.ms,
                            )
                            .fadeIn(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        AuthTextField(
                          label: 'Password',
                          prefixIcon: Icons.lock_outline,
                          isPassword: true,
                          controller: _passwordController,
                          validator: Validators.validatePassword,
                        )
                            .animate()
                            .slideY(
                              begin: 0.2,
                              end: 0,
                              delay: 300.ms,
                            )
                            .fadeIn(),
                        Align(
                          alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                          child: TextButton(
                            onPressed: isLoading
                                ? null
                                : () => context.push('/forgot-password'),
                            child: const Text(
                              'Forgot Password?',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: AppColors.secondary,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ).animate().fadeIn(delay: 400.ms),
                        const SizedBox(height: 24),
                        GradientButton(
                          text: 'Sign In',
                          isLoading: isLoading,
                          onPressed: isLoading ? null : _login,
                        )
                            .animate()
                            .slideY(
                              begin: 0.2,
                              end: 0,
                              delay: 500.ms,
                            )
                            .fadeIn(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),

                const SizedBox(height: 32),

                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      "Don't have an account? ",
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.7),
                      ),
                    ),
                    GestureDetector(
                      onTap: isLoading ? null : () => context.push('/register'),
                      child: const Text(
                        'Sign Up',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: AppColors.primary,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ).animate().fadeIn(delay: 800.ms),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/auth/register_screen.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_animate/flutter_animate.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/utils/validators.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/glass_card.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/gradient_button.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/auth/widgets/auth_text_field.dart';

class RegisterScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const RegisterScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<RegisterScreen> createState() => _RegisterScreenState();
}

class _RegisterScreenState extends ConsumerState<RegisterScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _phoneController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  final _confirmPasswordController = TextEditingController();

  bool _agreedToTerms = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _emailController.dispose();
    _phoneController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    _confirmPasswordController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _showMessage(
    String message, {
    Color backgroundColor = AppColors.error,
  }) {
    if (!mounted) return;

    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
      ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
      ..showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(message),
          backgroundColor: backgroundColor,
        ),
      );
  }

  Future<void> _register() async {
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();

    final isFormValid = _formKey.currentState?.validate() ?? false;

    if (!isFormValid) {
      return;
    }

    if (!_agreedToTerms) {
      _showMessage(
        'Please agree to Terms & Conditions',
        backgroundColor: AppColors.warning,
      );
      return;
    }

    if (_passwordController.text != _confirmPasswordController.text) {
      _showMessage('Passwords do not match');
      return;
    }

    final authNotifier = ref.read(authStateProvider.notifier);

    final success = await authNotifier.register(
      _emailController.text.trim(),
      _passwordController.text,
      _nameController.text.trim(),
      _phoneController.text.trim(),
    );

    // The screen might have been removed while registration was running.
    if (!mounted) return;

    final authState = ref.read(authStateProvider);

    if (authState is AuthError) {
      _showMessage(authState.message);
      return;
    }

    if (success) {
      context.go('/dashboard');
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // watch() automatically rebuilds the button when loading changes.
    final authState = ref.watch(authStateProvider);
    final isLoading = authState is AuthLoading;

    return Scaffold(
      body: Container(
        decoration: const BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            begin: Alignment.topLeft,
            end: Alignment.bottomRight,
            colors: [
              Color(0xFF0F172A),
              Color(0xFF1E293B),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        child: SafeArea(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Align(
                  alignment: Alignment.topLeft,
                  child: IconButton(
                    icon: const Icon(
                      Icons.arrow_back,
                      color: Colors.white,
                    ),
                    onPressed: isLoading
                        ? null
                        : () {
                            if (context.canPop()) {
                              context.pop();
                            } else {
                              context.go('/login');
                            }
                          },
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                GlassCard(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                  child: Form(
                    key: _formKey,
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        const Text(
                          'Create Account',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 28,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            fontFamily: 'Outfit',
                            color: Colors.white,
                          ),
                        )
                            .animate()
                            .slideX(
                              begin: -0.2,
                              end: 0,
                              duration: 400.ms,
                            )
                            .fadeIn(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 32),
                        AuthTextField(
                          label: 'Full Name',
                          prefixIcon: Icons.person_outline,
                          controller: _nameController,
                          validator: Validators.validateName,
                        )
                            .animate()
                            .slideY(
                              begin: 0.2,
                              end: 0,
                              delay: 100.ms,
                            )
                            .fadeIn(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        AuthTextField(
                          label: 'Email',
                          prefixIcon: Icons.email_outlined,
                          controller: _emailController,
                          keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                          validator: Validators.validateEmail,
                        )
                            .animate()
                            .slideY(
                              begin: 0.2,
                              end: 0,
                              delay: 200.ms,
                            )
                            .fadeIn(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        AuthTextField(
                          label: 'Phone Number',
                          prefixIcon: Icons.phone_outlined,
                          controller: _phoneController,
                          keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
                          validator: Validators.validatePhone,
                        )
                            .animate()
                            .slideY(
                              begin: 0.2,
                              end: 0,
                              delay: 300.ms,
                            )
                            .fadeIn(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        AuthTextField(
                          label: 'Password',
                          prefixIcon: Icons.lock_outline,
                          controller: _passwordController,
                          isPassword: true,
                          validator: Validators.validatePassword,
                        )
                            .animate()
                            .slideY(
                              begin: 0.2,
                              end: 0,
                              delay: 400.ms,
                            )
                            .fadeIn(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        AuthTextField(
                          label: 'Confirm Password',
                          prefixIcon: Icons.lock_outline,
                          controller: _confirmPasswordController,
                          isPassword: true,
                          validator: Validators.validatePassword,
                        )
                            .animate()
                            .slideY(
                              begin: 0.2,
                              end: 0,
                              delay: 500.ms,
                            )
                            .fadeIn(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        Row(
                          children: [
                            Checkbox(
                              value: _agreedToTerms,
                              onChanged: isLoading
                                  ? null
                                  : (value) {
                                      setState(() {
                                        _agreedToTerms = value ?? false;
                                      });
                                    },
                              fillColor:
                                  WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith<Color?>(
                                (states) {
                                  if (states.contains(WidgetState.selected)) {
                                    return AppColors.primary;
                                  }

                                  return Colors.transparent;
                                },
                              ),
                              side: const BorderSide(
                                color: Colors.white,
                              ),
                            ),
                            Expanded(
                              child: Text(
                                'I agree to the Terms & Conditions and '
                                'Privacy Policy',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.7),
                                  fontSize: 12,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ).animate().fadeIn(delay: 600.ms),
                        const SizedBox(height: 24),
                        GradientButton(
                          text: 'Create Account',
                          isLoading: isLoading,
                          onPressed: isLoading ? null : _register,
                        )
                            .animate()
                            .slideY(
                              begin: 0.2,
                              end: 0,
                              delay: 700.ms,
                            )
                            .fadeIn(),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/auth/widgets/auth_text_field.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/utils/extensions.dart';

class AuthTextField extends StatefulWidget {
  final String label;
  final IconData prefixIcon;
  final bool isPassword;
  final TextEditingController? controller;
  final String? Function(String?)? validator;
  final TextInputType keyboardType;

  const AuthTextField({
    super.key,
    required this.label,
    required this.prefixIcon,
    this.isPassword = false,
    this.controller,
    this.validator,
    this.keyboardType = TextInputType.text,
  });

  @override
  State<AuthTextField> createState() => _AuthTextFieldState();
}

class _AuthTextFieldState extends State<AuthTextField> {
  bool _obscureText = true;
  final FocusNode _focusNode = FocusNode();
  bool _isFocused = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _focusNode.addListener(() {
      setState(() {
        _isFocused = _focusNode.hasFocus;
      });
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _focusNode.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return TextFormField(
      controller: widget.controller,
      focusNode: _focusNode,
      obscureText: widget.isPassword && _obscureText,
      validator: widget.validator,
      keyboardType: widget.keyboardType,
      style: context.textTheme.bodyLarge,
      decoration: InputDecoration(
        labelText: widget.label,
        labelStyle: TextStyle(
          color: _isFocused ? AppColors.primary : Colors.grey,
        ),
        prefixIcon: Icon(
          widget.prefixIcon,
          color: _isFocused ? AppColors.primary : Colors.grey,
        ),
        suffixIcon: widget.isPassword
            ? IconButton(
                icon: Icon(
                  _obscureText ? Icons.visibility_off : Icons.visibility,
                  color: Colors.grey,
                ),
                onPressed: () {
                  setState(() {
                    _obscureText = !_obscureText;
                  });
                },
              )
            : null,
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/dashboard/dashboard_screen.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:flutter_animate/flutter_animate.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/utils/extensions.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/shimmer_loading.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/notification_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/parking_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/reservation_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/dashboard/widgets/active_reservation_card.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/dashboard/widgets/availability_card.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/dashboard/widgets/nearby_parking_card.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/dashboard/widgets/quick_actions.dart';

class DashboardScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const DashboardScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<DashboardScreen> createState() => _DashboardScreenState();
}

class _DashboardScreenState extends ConsumerState<DashboardScreen> {
  Future<void> _onRefresh() async {
    ref.invalidate(parkingAreasProvider);
    ref.invalidate(activeReservationProvider);
    ref.invalidate(notificationsProvider);
  }

  String _getGreeting() {
    final hour = DateTime.now().hour;
    if (hour < 12) return 'Good Morning';
    if (hour < 17) return 'Good Afternoon';
    return 'Good Evening';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final user = ref.watch(currentUserProvider);
    final activeReservationAsync = ref.watch(activeReservationProvider);
    final areasAsync = ref.watch(parkingAreasProvider);
    final hasUnreadNotifications = ref.watch(hasUnreadNotificationsProvider);

    return Scaffold(
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: _onRefresh,
        child: CustomScrollView(
          physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          slivers: [
            SliverAppBar(
              expandedHeight: 92,
              floating: true,
              pinned: true,
              backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
              flexibleSpace: FlexibleSpaceBar(
                titlePadding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20, 14),
                title: Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            _getGreeting(),
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 13,
                              color: context.isDarkMode
                                  ? Colors.grey[400]
                                  : Colors.grey[600],
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.normal,
                            ),
                          ),
                          Text(
                            user?.fullName ?? 'User',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 20,
                              color: context.textTheme.titleLarge?.color,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              actions: [
                Stack(
                  alignment: Alignment.center,
                  children: [
                    IconButton(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.notifications_outlined),
                      onPressed: () => context.push('/notifications'),
                    ),
                    if (hasUnreadNotifications)
                      Positioned(
                        right: 12,
                        top: 12,
                        child: Container(
                          width: 8,
                          height: 8,
                          decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                            color: AppColors.error,
                            shape: BoxShape.circle,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
              ],
            ),
            SliverToBoxAdapter(
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20.0),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    const SizedBox(height: 14),

                    // Active Reservation
                    activeReservationAsync.when(
                      data: (reservation) {
                        if (reservation != null) {
                          return Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 24),
                            child: ActiveReservationCard(
                              reservation: reservation,
                              onTap: () => context.go('/reservations'),
                            )
                                .animate()
                                .fadeIn(duration: 400.ms)
                                .slideY(begin: 0.1, end: 0),
                          );
                        }
                        return const SizedBox.shrink();
                      },
                      loading: () => Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 24),
                        child: ShimmerLoading.card(height: 100),
                      ),
                      error: (_, __) => const SizedBox.shrink(),
                    ),

                    InkWell(
                      onTap: () => context.go('/search'),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                      child: Container(
                        height: 52,
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                          border: Border.all(
                            color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor,
                          ),
                        ),
                        child: const Row(
                          children: [
                            Icon(Icons.search_rounded,
                                color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
                            SizedBox(width: 12),
                            Expanded(
                              child: Text(
                                'Search parking nearby',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
                              ),
                            ),
                            Icon(Icons.tune_rounded,
                                color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 18),

                    QuickActions(
                      onFindParking: () => context.go('/search'),
                      onMyBookings: () => context.go('/reservations'),
                    )
                        .animate()
                        .fadeIn(delay: 100.ms, duration: 400.ms)
                        .slideY(begin: 0.1, end: 0),

                    const SizedBox(height: 30),

                    // Live availability from the parking-area API.
                    Text(
                      'Live Availability',
                      style: context.textTheme.titleLarge
                          ?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                    ).animate().fadeIn(delay: 200.ms),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),

                    areasAsync.when(
                      data: (areas) {
                        int total = 0;
                        int available = 0;
                        for (var area in areas) {
                          total += area.totalSlots;
                          available += area.availableSlots;
                        }
                        return AvailabilityCard(
                          totalSlots: total,
                          availableSlots: available,
                          occupiedSlots: total - available,
                        )
                            .animate()
                            .fadeIn(delay: 300.ms)
                            .slideX(begin: 0.1, end: 0);
                      },
                      loading: () => ShimmerLoading.card(height: 120),
                      error: (_, __) => const Text(
                        'Availability is temporarily unavailable.',
                        style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
                      ),
                    ),

                    const SizedBox(height: 32),

                    // Nearby Parking
                    Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          'Nearby Parking',
                          style: context.textTheme.titleLarge
                              ?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                        ),
                        TextButton(
                          onPressed: () => context.go('/search'),
                          child: const Text('See All'),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ).animate().fadeIn(delay: 400.ms),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),

                    SizedBox(
                      height: 272,
                      child: areasAsync.when(
                        data: (areas) {
                          if (areas.isEmpty) {
                            return const Center(
                              child: Text('No parking areas found'),
                            );
                          }
                          return ListView.builder(
                            scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                            itemCount: areas.length,
                            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                              return NearbyParkingCard(
                                area: areas[index],
                                onTap: () => context
                                    .go('/parking-map/${areas[index].id}'),
                              )
                                  .animate()
                                  .fadeIn(
                                      delay: Duration(
                                          milliseconds: 500 + (index * 100)))
                                  .slideX(begin: 0.2, end: 0);
                            },
                          );
                        },
                        loading: () => ListView.builder(
                          scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                          itemCount: 3,
                          itemBuilder: (context, index) => Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 16.0),
                            child: ShimmerLoading.card(height: 240),
                          ),
                        ),
                        error: (_, __) => const Center(
                          child: Text('Unable to load parking areas.'),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),

                    const SizedBox(height: 40),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/dashboard/widgets/active_reservation_card.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/reservation_model.dart';

class ActiveReservationCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final ReservationModel reservation;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const ActiveReservationCard({
    super.key,
    required this.reservation,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final slotLabel = reservation.slotDetails?.slotId ?? reservation.slotId;
    final actionLabel = switch (reservation.status) {
      'PAYMENT_PENDING' => 'Pay Advance',
      'PENDING' => 'Navigate',
      'ACTIVE' => 'View Timer',
      'CHECKOUT_PAYMENT_PENDING' => 'Complete Payment',
      _ => 'View Booking',
    };

    return Material(
      color: Colors.transparent,
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        child: Container(
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            border: Border.all(color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.45)),
          ),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.15),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                ),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    const Icon(
                      Icons.local_parking_rounded,
                      color: AppColors.primaryLight,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(
                      slotLabel,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: AppColors.primaryLight,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        fontSize: 18,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      reservation.areaName ?? 'Parking Area',
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        fontSize: 16,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(
                      actionLabel,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: AppColors.success,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        fontSize: 13,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Container(
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.14),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                ),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                child: const Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      Icons.arrow_forward_rounded,
                      size: 18,
                      color: AppColors.primaryLight,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/dashboard/widgets/availability_card.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:percent_indicator/percent_indicator.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/animated_counter.dart';

class AvailabilityCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final int totalSlots;
  final int availableSlots;
  final int occupiedSlots;

  const AvailabilityCard({
    super.key,
    required this.totalSlots,
    required this.availableSlots,
    required this.occupiedSlots,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final percentage = totalSlots <= 0
        ? 0.0
        : (availableSlots / totalSlots).clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
        children: [
          CircularPercentIndicator(
            radius: 48.0,
            lineWidth: 10.0,
            animation: true,
            percent: percentage,
            center: AnimatedCounter(
              targetValue: percentage * 100,
              suffix: '%',
              textStyle:
                  const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16),
            ),
            circularStrokeCap: CircularStrokeCap.round,
            progressColor: AppColors.success,
            backgroundColor: AppColors.error.withOpacity(0.9),
          ),
          Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              _buildStatRow(
                  context, 'Available', availableSlots, AppColors.success),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              _buildStatRow(
                  context, 'Occupied', occupiedSlots, AppColors.error),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatRow(
    BuildContext context,
    String label,
    int count,
    Color color,
  ) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Container(
          width: 12,
          height: 12,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: color,
            shape: BoxShape.circle,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        SizedBox(
          width: 78,
          child: Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 14,
              color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        AnimatedCounter(
          targetValue: count,
          textStyle: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/dashboard/widgets/nearby_parking_card.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/parking_area_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/utils/extensions.dart';

class NearbyParkingCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final ParkingAreaModel area;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const NearbyParkingCard({
    super.key,
    required this.area,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final imageUrl = area.imageUrl?.trim();
    final hasImage = imageUrl != null && imageUrl.isNotEmpty;

    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) {
        final cardWidth =
            constraints.hasBoundedWidth ? constraints.maxWidth : 310.0;
        return Container(
          width: cardWidth,
          height: 272,
          margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 14),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
          ),
          clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Container(
                height: 112,
                width: double.infinity,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.grey.shade300,
                  image: hasImage
                      ? DecorationImage(
                          image: NetworkImage(imageUrl!),
                          fit: BoxFit.cover,
                        )
                      : null,
                ),
                child: !hasImage
                    ? const Center(
                        child: Icon(Icons.local_parking,
                            size: 48, color: Colors.grey))
                    : null,
              ),
              Expanded(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 12, 14, 10),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        area.name,
                        style: context.textTheme.titleMedium
                            ?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                        maxLines: 1,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(
                        area.address,
                        style: context.textTheme.bodySmall
                            ?.copyWith(color: Colors.grey),
                        maxLines: 1,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      ),
                      const Spacer(),
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                        children: [
                          Row(
                            children: [
                              Container(
                                width: 8,
                                height: 8,
                                decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                                  color: AppColors.success,
                                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(width: 4),
                              Text(
                                '${area.availableSlots} available',
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                          const Text(
                            '₹20/hr',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 14,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              color: AppColors.primaryLight,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 10),
                      SizedBox(
                        width: double.infinity,
                        height: 42,
                        child: FilledButton(
                          onPressed: onTap,
                          style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                            ),
                          ),
                          child: const Text('View Slots'),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/dashboard/widgets/occupancy_chart.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:fl_chart/fl_chart.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';

class OccupancyChart extends StatefulWidget {
  final int vacant;
  final int occupied;
  final int reserved;

  const OccupancyChart({
    super.key,
    required this.vacant,
    required this.occupied,
    required this.reserved,
  });

  @override
  State<OccupancyChart> createState() => _OccupancyChartState();
}

class _OccupancyChartState extends State<OccupancyChart> {
  int touchedIndex = -1;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    int total = widget.vacant + widget.occupied + widget.reserved;
    if (total == 0) total = 1; // Prevent division by zero

    return AspectRatio(
      aspectRatio: 1,
      child: PieChart(
        PieChartData(
          pieTouchData: PieTouchData(
            touchCallback: (FlTouchEvent event, pieTouchResponse) {
              setState(() {
                if (!event.isInterestedForInteractions ||
                    pieTouchResponse == null ||
                    pieTouchResponse.touchedSection == null) {
                  touchedIndex = -1;
                  return;
                }
                touchedIndex =
                    pieTouchResponse.touchedSection!.touchedSectionIndex;
              });
            },
          ),
          borderData: FlBorderData(show: false),
          sectionsSpace: 2,
          centerSpaceRadius: 30,
          sections: [
            PieChartSectionData(
              color: AppColors.slotVacant,
              value: widget.vacant.toDouble(),
              title: '${((widget.vacant / total) * 100).toStringAsFixed(0)}%',
              radius: touchedIndex == 0 ? 40.0 : 30.0,
              titleStyle: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: Colors.white),
            ),
            PieChartSectionData(
              color: AppColors.slotOccupied,
              value: widget.occupied.toDouble(),
              title: '${((widget.occupied / total) * 100).toStringAsFixed(0)}%',
              radius: touchedIndex == 1 ? 40.0 : 30.0,
              titleStyle: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: Colors.white),
            ),
            PieChartSectionData(
              color: AppColors.slotReserved,
              value: widget.reserved.toDouble(),
              title: '${((widget.reserved / total) * 100).toStringAsFixed(0)}%',
              radius: touchedIndex == 2 ? 40.0 : 30.0,
              titleStyle: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: Colors.white),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/dashboard/widgets/quick_actions.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';

class QuickActions extends StatelessWidget {
  final VoidCallback onFindParking;
  final VoidCallback onMyBookings;

  const QuickActions({
    super.key,
    required this.onFindParking,
    required this.onMyBookings,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) {
        final itemWidth = (constraints.maxWidth - 12) / 2;
        return Row(
          children: [
            _buildActionItem(
              context,
              'Find Parking',
              Icons.location_on_rounded,
              onFindParking,
              itemWidth,
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            _buildActionItem(
              context,
              'My Bookings',
              Icons.calendar_month_rounded,
              onMyBookings,
              itemWidth,
            ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActionItem(
    BuildContext context,
    String title,
    IconData icon,
    VoidCallback onTap,
    double width,
  ) {
    return Material(
      color: Colors.transparent,
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        child: Container(
          width: width,
          height: 102,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 14),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(
                color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.22),
                blurRadius: 18,
                offset: const Offset(0, 8),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              Icon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 30),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              Text(
                title,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  color: Colors.white,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  fontSize: 14,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/navigation/indoor_navigation_screen.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/gradient_button.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/reservation_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/reservation_provider.dart';

class IndoorNavigationScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const IndoorNavigationScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.reservation,
  });

  final ReservationModel reservation;

  @override
  ConsumerState<IndoorNavigationScreen> createState() =>
      _IndoorNavigationScreenState();
}

class _IndoorNavigationScreenState
    extends ConsumerState<IndoorNavigationScreen> {
  String get _slotLabel =>
      widget.reservation.slotDetails?.slotId ?? widget.reservation.slotId;

  int get _floor => widget.reservation.slotDetails?.floor ?? 1;

  void _viewBookingStatus() {
    ref.invalidate(activeReservationProvider);
    ref.invalidate(reservationsListProvider);
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      const SnackBar(
        content: Text(
          'The timer starts automatically after the camera confirms your vehicle in the allotted slot.',
        ),
        backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
      ),
    );
    context.go('/reservations');
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text('Navigate to $_slotLabel'),
      ),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 8, 16, 24),
          children: [
            Center(
              child: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: 16,
                  vertical: 9,
                ),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.14),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  border: Border.all(
                    color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.35),
                  ),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  'Floor $_floor',
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: AppColors.primaryLight,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            _ParkingFloorPlan(targetSlot: _slotLabel),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
              ),
              child: const Column(
                children: [
                  _DirectionRow(
                    icon: Icons.arrow_upward_rounded,
                    title: 'Continue straight from Gate 1',
                    subtitle: 'Follow the highlighted driving lane',
                  ),
                  Divider(height: 26),
                  _DirectionRow(
                    icon: Icons.turn_left_rounded,
                    title: 'Turn left at the marked zone',
                    subtitle: 'Your allocated slot is highlighted in green',
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            GradientButton(
              text: 'Check Camera Confirmation',
              icon: Icons.videocam_outlined,
              onPressed: _viewBookingStatus,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                Icon(
                  Icons.info_outline_rounded,
                  size: 16,
                  color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                ),
                SizedBox(width: 7),
                Flexible(
                  child: Text(
                    'Use this route from Gate 1. Billing begins only after the parking camera confirms your vehicle in $_slotLabel.',
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                      fontSize: 12,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ParkingFloorPlan extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ParkingFloorPlan({required this.targetSlot});

  final String targetSlot;

  List<String> get _slots {
    final match = RegExp(r'^[A-Za-z]+').firstMatch(targetSlot);
    final prefix = match?.group(0)?.toUpperCase() ?? 'A';
    final result = List.generate(
      8,
      (index) => '$prefix${(index + 1).toString().padLeft(2, '0')}',
    );
    if (!result.contains(targetSlot)) result[0] = targetSlot;
    return result;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final slots = _slots;
    return Container(
      height: 380,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFF0C1C31),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.borderDark),
      ),
      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
      child: LayoutBuilder(
        builder: (context, constraints) {
          final slotWidth = (constraints.maxWidth - 128) / 2;
          return Stack(
            children: [
              Positioned.fill(
                child: CustomPaint(
                  painter: _RoutePainter(),
                ),
              ),
              Positioned(
                top: 18,
                left: constraints.maxWidth / 2 - 34,
                child: _zoneLabel(),
              ),
              for (var index = 0; index < 4; index++)
                Positioned(
                  left: 14,
                  top: 18 + (index * 72),
                  child: _slotCard(
                    slots[index],
                    slotWidth,
                    slots[index] == targetSlot,
                  ),
                ),
              for (var index = 0; index < 4; index++)
                Positioned(
                  right: 14,
                  top: 18 + (index * 72),
                  child: _slotCard(
                    slots[index + 4],
                    slotWidth,
                    slots[index + 4] == targetSlot,
                  ),
                ),
              Positioned(
                bottom: 18,
                left: constraints.maxWidth / 2 - 42,
                child: Container(
                  width: 84,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: AppColors.cardDarkElevated,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                    border: Border.all(color: AppColors.borderDark),
                  ),
                  child: const Column(
                    children: [
                      Icon(
                        Icons.my_location_rounded,
                        color: AppColors.primaryLight,
                        size: 20,
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: 3),
                      Text(
                        'Gate 1',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _zoneLabel() {
    return Container(
      width: 68,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.2),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
      ),
      child: const Text(
        'ZONE',
        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
        style: TextStyle(
          color: AppColors.primaryLight,
          fontSize: 11,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _slotCard(String label, double width, bool selected) {
    final color = selected ? AppColors.success : AppColors.borderDark;
    return Container(
      width: width,
      height: 58,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: selected
            ? AppColors.success.withOpacity(0.18)
            : AppColors.cardDark.withOpacity(0.8),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: color, width: selected ? 2 : 1),
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          Icon(
            Icons.directions_car_filled_rounded,
            size: 20,
            color: selected ? AppColors.success : AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 11,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
              color: selected ? Colors.white : AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _RoutePainter extends CustomPainter {
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final route = Paint()
      ..color = AppColors.primaryLight
      ..strokeWidth = 5
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round
      ..strokeJoin = StrokeJoin.round
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke;
    final glow = Paint()
      ..color = AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.35)
      ..strokeWidth = 12
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round
      ..strokeJoin = StrokeJoin.round
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke;

    final path = Path()
      ..moveTo(size.width / 2, size.height - 72)
      ..lineTo(size.width / 2, 76)
      ..lineTo(78, 76);
    canvas.drawPath(path, glow);
    canvas.drawPath(path, route);

    final arrow = Path()
      ..moveTo(size.width / 2 - 8, size.height / 2 + 8)
      ..lineTo(size.width / 2, size.height / 2)
      ..lineTo(size.width / 2 + 8, size.height / 2 + 8);
    canvas.drawPath(arrow, route);
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant CustomPainter oldDelegate) => false;
}

class _DirectionRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DirectionRow({
    required this.icon,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
  });

  final IconData icon;
  final String title;
  final String subtitle;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Container(
          width: 42,
          height: 42,
          decoration: const BoxDecoration(
            color: AppColors.primary,
            shape: BoxShape.circle,
          ),
          child: Icon(icon, color: Colors.white),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                title,
                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 3),
              Text(
                subtitle,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                  fontSize: 12,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/notifications/notifications_screen.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_animate/flutter_animate.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/utils/extensions.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/notification_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/notification_provider.dart';

class NotificationsScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
  const NotificationsScreen({super.key});

  Future<void> _refresh(WidgetRef ref) async {
    ref.invalidate(notificationsProvider);
    await ref.read(notificationsProvider.future);
  }

  Future<void> _markAllRead(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) async {
    try {
      await ref.read(notificationRepositoryProvider).markAllAsRead();
      ref.invalidate(notificationsProvider);
    } catch (_) {
      if (!context.mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Unable to update notifications. Please try again.'),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _markRead(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref,
    NotificationModel notification,
  ) async {
    if (notification.isRead) return;
    try {
      await ref
          .read(notificationRepositoryProvider)
          .markAsRead(notification.id);
      ref.invalidate(notificationsProvider);
    } catch (_) {
      if (!context.mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Unable to update this notification.'),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final notifications = ref.watch(notificationsProvider);
    final hasUnread = ref.watch(hasUnreadNotificationsProvider);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Notifications'),
        centerTitle: true,
        actions: [
          if (hasUnread)
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => _markAllRead(context, ref),
              child: const Text('Mark all read'),
            ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
        ],
      ),
      body: notifications.when(
        data: (items) {
          if (items.isEmpty) return const _EmptyNotifications();
          return RefreshIndicator(
            onRefresh: () => _refresh(ref),
            child: ListView.separated(
              physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 12, 20, 32),
              itemCount: items.length,
              separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(height: 12),
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                final notification = items[index];
                return _NotificationTile(
                  notification: notification,
                  onTap: () => _markRead(context, ref, notification),
                )
                    .animate()
                    .fadeIn(delay: Duration(milliseconds: 70 * index))
                    .slideY(begin: 0.06, end: 0);
              },
            ),
          );
        },
        loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
        error: (_, __) => _NotificationsError(onRetry: () => _refresh(ref)),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _NotificationTile extends StatelessWidget {
  const _NotificationTile({
    required this.notification,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  final NotificationModel notification;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final (icon, color) = switch (notification.type.toLowerCase()) {
      'success' || 'reservation' => (
          Icons.check_circle_rounded,
          AppColors.success
        ),
      'warning' || 'expiry' => (Icons.warning_rounded, AppColors.warning),
      'payment' => (Icons.payment_rounded, AppColors.primary),
      _ => (Icons.info_rounded, AppColors.accent),
    };

    return Material(
      color: notification.isRead
          ? Theme.of(context).cardColor
          : AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.12),
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 46,
                height: 46,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
                child: Icon(icon, color: color),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 14),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Expanded(
                          child: Text(
                            notification.title,
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 16,
                              fontWeight: notification.isRead
                                  ? FontWeight.w600
                                  : FontWeight.w800,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        if (!notification.isRead)
                          const Padding(
                            padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: 8),
                            child: CircleAvatar(
                              radius: 4,
                              backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                            ),
                          ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 6),
                    Text(
                      notification.body,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                        height: 1.4,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Text(
                      notification.createdAt.timeAgo,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                        fontSize: 12,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _EmptyNotifications extends StatelessWidget {
  const _EmptyNotifications();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      child: Padding(

        child: Column(

            Icon(
              Icons.notifications_none_rounded,
              size: 68,
              color: Colors.grey.shade500,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 18),
            const Text(
              'You are all caught up',
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            const Text(
              'Booking and payment updates will appear here.',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _NotificationsError extends StatelessWidget {
  const _NotificationsError({required this.onRetry});

  final Future<void> Function() onRetry;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const Icon(
              Icons.cloud_off_rounded,
              size: 62,
              color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            const Text(
              'Notifications are unavailable right now.',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            OutlinedButton.icon(
              onPressed: onRetry,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded),
              label: const Text('Try Again'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/parking_map/parking_map_screen.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/shimmer_loading.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/parking_area_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/parking_slot_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/models/parking_slot.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/parking_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/reservation_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/websocket_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/parking_map/widgets/floor_selector.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/parking_map/widgets/legend_bar.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/parking_map/widgets/parking_slot_widget.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/parking_map/widgets/slot_detail_sheet.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/payment/payment_screen.dart';

class ParkingMapScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  final String? areaId;

  const ParkingMapScreen({
    super.key,
    this.areaId,
  });

  @override
  ConsumerState<ParkingMapScreen> createState() => _ParkingMapScreenState();
}

class _ParkingMapScreenState extends ConsumerState<ParkingMapScreen> {
  int _selectedFloor = 1;
  bool _isReserving = false;

  String _normalizeStatus(String status) {
    return status.split('.').last.toUpperCase();
  }

  String _friendlyError(Object error) {
    final message = error.toString().toLowerCase();
    if (message.contains('not authenticated') ||
        message.contains('unauthorized')) {
      return 'Your session has expired. Please sign in again.';
    }
    if (message.contains('cannot reach') ||
        message.contains('connection') ||
        message.contains('timeout')) {
