      return 'Unable to connect to parking services. Please try again.';
    }
    return 'Something went wrong. Please try again.';
  }

  ParkingAreaModel? _findSelectedArea(
    List<ParkingAreaModel> areas,
  ) {
    if (areas.isEmpty) {
      return null;
    }

    // When opened from the bottom navigation, use the first area.
    if (widget.areaId == null) {
      return areas.first;
    }

    for (final area in areas) {
      if (area.id == widget.areaId) {
        return area;
      }
    }

    return null;
  }

  void _showMessage(
    String message, {
    Color backgroundColor = AppColors.error,
  }) {
    if (!mounted) return;

    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
      ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
      ..showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(message),
          backgroundColor: backgroundColor,
        ),
      );
  }

  void _showSlotDetails(
    ParkingSlotModel slot,
    ParkingAreaModel area,
  ) {
    showModalBottomSheet<void>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (bottomSheetContext) {
        return StatefulBuilder(
          builder: (context, setModalState) {
            return SlotDetailSheet(
              slot: slot,
              areaName: area.name,
              hourlyRate: area.hourlyRate,
              currency: area.currency,
              isReserving: _isReserving,
              onReserve: () async {
                if (_isReserving) {
                  return;
                }

                if (_normalizeStatus(slot.status) != 'VACANT') {
                  _showMessage(
                    'This parking slot is no longer available.',
                  );
                  return;
                }

                if (mounted) {
                  setState(() {
                    _isReserving = true;
                  });
                }

                if (bottomSheetContext.mounted) {
                  setModalState(() {});
                }

                try {
                  final repository = ref.read(
                    reservationRepositoryProvider,
                  );

                  final startTime = DateTime.now();
                  final endTime = startTime.add(
                    const Duration(hours: 1),
                  );

                  final reservation = await repository.createReservation(
                    slot.id,
                    area.id,
                    startTime,
                    endTime,
                  );

                  if (!mounted) {
                    return;
                  }

                  // Refresh all data affected by the reservation.
                  ref.invalidate(activeReservationProvider);
                  ref.invalidate(reservationsListProvider);
                  ref.invalidate(
                    parkingSlotsProvider(area.id),
                  );
                  ref.invalidate(parkingAreasProvider);

                  if (bottomSheetContext.mounted) {
                    Navigator.of(bottomSheetContext).pop();
                  }

                  if (!mounted) {
                    return;
                  }

                  _showMessage(
                    'Slot held. Pay the ₹10 advance within 30 minutes.',
                    backgroundColor: AppColors.success,
                  );

                  await context.push(
                    '/payment',
                    extra: PaymentScreenArgs(
                      reservationId: reservation.id,
                      amountPaise:
                          ((reservation.totalAmount ?? 10) * 100).round(),
                      currency: 'INR',
                    ),
                  );
                } catch (error) {
                  if (!mounted) {
                    return;
                  }

                  _showMessage(_friendlyError(error));
                } finally {
                  if (mounted) {
                    setState(() {
                      _isReserving = false;
                    });
                  }

                  if (bottomSheetContext.mounted) {
                    setModalState(() {});
                  }
                }
              },
            );
          },
        );
      },
    ).whenComplete(() {
      if (mounted && _isReserving) {
        setState(() {
          _isReserving = false;
        });
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final areasAsync = ref.watch(parkingAreasProvider);

    return areasAsync.when(
      data: (areas) {
        final selectedArea = _findSelectedArea(areas);

        if (selectedArea == null) {
          return Scaffold(
            appBar: _buildAppBar(),
            body: Center(
              child: Text(
                areas.isEmpty
                    ? 'No parking areas available'
                    : 'Parking area not found',
              ),
            ),
          );
        }

        return _buildMap(selectedArea);
      },
      loading: () {
        return Scaffold(
          appBar: _buildAppBar(),
          body: const Center(
            child: CircularProgressIndicator(),
          ),
        );
      },
      error: (error, stackTrace) {
        return Scaffold(
          appBar: _buildAppBar(),
          body: Center(
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  const Text(
                    'Unable to load parking areas.',
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      color: AppColors.error,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  ElevatedButton.icon(
                    onPressed: () {
                      ref.invalidate(parkingAreasProvider);
                    },
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
                    label: const Text('Try Again'),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  PreferredSizeWidget _buildAppBar() {
    return AppBar(
      title: const Text('Choose a Parking Slot'),
      leading: widget.areaId != null
          ? IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),
              onPressed: () {
                if (context.canPop()) {
                  context.pop();
                } else {
                  context.go('/dashboard');
                }
              },
            )
          : null,
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMap(ParkingAreaModel area) {
    final slotsAsync = ref.watch(
      parkingSlotsProvider(area.id),
    );

    final webSocketAsync = ref.watch(
      webSocketProvider(area.id),
    );

    final totalFloors = area.totalFloors > 0 ? area.totalFloors : 1;

    final currentFloor = _selectedFloor.clamp(1, totalFloors).toInt();

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: _buildAppBar(),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 4, 16, 0),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        area.name,
                        maxLines: 1,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        style:
                            Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text(
                        '${area.availableSlots} slots available',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: AppColors.success,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                    horizontal: 12,
                    vertical: 8,
                  ),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.14),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  ),
                  child: const Text(
                    '₹10 advance',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: AppColors.primaryLight,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          FloorSelector(
            totalFloors: totalFloors,
            selectedFloor: currentFloor,
            onFloorSelected: (floor) {
              setState(() {
                _selectedFloor = floor;
              });
            },
          ),
          const LegendBar(),
          const SizedBox(height: 6),
          Expanded(
            child: slotsAsync.when(
              data: (slots) {
                final floorSlots = slots
                    .where(
                      (slot) => slot.floor == currentFloor,
                    )
                    .toList();

                if (floorSlots.isEmpty) {
                  return Center(
                    child: Text(
                      'No parking slots available on '
                      'floor $currentFloor',
                    ),
                  );
                }

                return GridView.builder(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 10, 16, 18),
                  gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
                    crossAxisCount: 4,
                    childAspectRatio: 0.78,
                    crossAxisSpacing: 10,
                    mainAxisSpacing: 10,
                  ),
                  itemCount: floorSlots.length,
                  itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                    final slot = floorSlots[index];

                    var currentStatus = _normalizeStatus(slot.status);

                    webSocketAsync.whenData((data) {
                      final eventSlotId = data['slot_id']?.toString();

                      final isSameSlot =
                          eventSlotId == slot.id || eventSlotId == slot.slotId;

                      if (isSameSlot && data['status'] != null) {
                        currentStatus = _normalizeStatus(
                          data['status'].toString(),
                        );
                      }
                    });

                    return ParkingSlotWidget(
                      slotId: slot.slotId,
                      status: DomainSlotStatus.fromString(
                        currentStatus,
                      ),
                      type: DomainSlotType.fromString(
                        slot.slotType,
                      ),
                      onTap: () {
                        if (currentStatus != 'VACANT') {
                          _showMessage(
                            'Only vacant parking slots '
                            'can be booked.',
                          );
                          return;
                        }

                        _showSlotDetails(slot, area);
                      },
                    );
                  },
                );
              },
              loading: () {
                return ShimmerLoading.grid(
                  crossAxisCount: 4,
                  childAspectRatio: 0.7,
                );
              },
              error: (error, stackTrace) {
                return Center(
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        const Text(
                          'Unable to load parking slots.',
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            color: AppColors.error,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        ElevatedButton.icon(
                          onPressed: () {
                            ref.invalidate(
                              parkingSlotsProvider(area.id),
                            );
                          },
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
                          label: const Text('Try Again'),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/parking_map/widgets/floor_selector.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';

class FloorSelector extends StatelessWidget {
  final int totalFloors;
  final int selectedFloor;
  final Function(int) onFloorSelected;

  const FloorSelector({
    super.key,
    required this.totalFloors,
    required this.selectedFloor,
    required this.onFloorSelected,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      height: 46,
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10),
      child: ListView.builder(
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        itemCount: totalFloors,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
          final floor = index + 1;
          final isSelected = floor == selectedFloor;

          return GestureDetector(
            onTap: () => onFloorSelected(floor),
            child: AnimatedContainer(
              duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 12),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: isSelected
                    ? AppColors.primary
                    : Theme.of(context).cardColor,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                border: Border.all(
                  color: isSelected
                      ? AppColors.primary
                      : Colors.grey.withOpacity(0.3),
                ),
              ),
              alignment: Alignment.center,
              child: Text(
                'Floor $floor',
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: isSelected
                      ? Colors.white
                      : Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.color,
                  fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/parking_map/widgets/legend_bar.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/models/parking_slot.dart';

class LegendBar extends StatelessWidget {
  const LegendBar({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    const visibleStatuses = [
      DomainSlotStatus.vacant,
      DomainSlotStatus.occupied,
      DomainSlotStatus.reserved,
    ];

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: visibleStatuses.map((status) {
          return Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 12,
                height: 12,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: status.color,
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Text(
                status.label,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          );
        }).toList(),
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/parking_map/widgets/parking_slot_widget.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/models/parking_slot.dart';

class ParkingSlotWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  final String slotId;
  final DomainSlotStatus status;
  final DomainSlotType type;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final bool isSelected;

  const ParkingSlotWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.slotId,
    required this.status,
    required this.type,
    required this.onTap,
    this.isSelected = false,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Material(
      color: Colors.transparent,
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        child: AnimatedContainer(
          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: isSelected
                ? status.color.withOpacity(0.28)
                : status.color.withOpacity(0.14),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
            border: Border.all(
              color: status.color,
              width: isSelected ? 2.5 : 1.5,
            ),
            boxShadow: isSelected
                ? [
                    BoxShadow(
                        color: status.color.withOpacity(0.5),
                        blurRadius: 8,
                        spreadRadius: 2)
                  ]
                : [],
          ),
          child: Stack(
            children: [
              Center(
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      type.icon,
                      color: status.color,
                      size: 28,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Text(
                      slotId,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.color,
                        fontSize: 14,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              if (isSelected)
                const Positioned(
                  right: 7,
                  top: 7,
                  child: Icon(
                    Icons.check_circle_rounded,
                    color: Colors.white,
                    size: 20,
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/parking_map/widgets/slot_detail_sheet.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/parking_slot_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/models/parking_slot.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/gradient_button.dart';

class SlotDetailSheet extends StatelessWidget {
  final ParkingSlotModel slot;
  final String areaName;
  final double hourlyRate;
  final String currency;
  final VoidCallback onReserve;
  final bool isReserving;

  const SlotDetailSheet({
    super.key,
    required this.slot,
    required this.areaName,
    required this.hourlyRate,
    required this.currency,
    required this.onReserve,
    this.isReserving = false,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final domainStatus = DomainSlotStatus.fromString(slot.status);
    final domainType = DomainSlotType.fromString(slot.slotType);

    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
        borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(28)),
        border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
      ),
      padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
        20,
        14,
        20,
        20 + MediaQuery.paddingOf(context).bottom,
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Center(
            child: Container(
              width: 40,
              height: 4,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.grey.withOpacity(0.3),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 18),
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      'Slot ${slot.slotId}',
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 28,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        fontFamily: 'Outfit',
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(
                      areaName,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 16,
                        color: Colors.grey,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Container(
                padding:
                    const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: domainStatus.color.withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  border: Border.all(color: domainStatus.color),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  domainStatus.label,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: domainStatus.color,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: _buildInfoItem(
                  context,
                  'Type',
                  domainType.label,
                  domainType.icon,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: _buildInfoItem(
                  context,
                  'Rate',
                  '₹20 / first hr',
                  Icons.payments_outlined,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: _buildInfoItem(
                  context,
                  'Floor',
                  '${slot.floor}',
                  Icons.layers_outlined,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          if (domainStatus == DomainSlotStatus.vacant)
            GradientButton(
              text: 'Reserve ${slot.slotId} · Pay ₹10',
              isLoading: isReserving,
              onPressed: onReserve,
            )
          else
            Container(
              width: double.infinity,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.grey.withOpacity(0.1),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              ),
              child: const Text(
                'This slot is currently unavailable.',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Colors.grey,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildInfoItem(
      BuildContext context, String label, String value, IconData icon) {
    return Container(
      constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 78),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.08),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.18)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Icon(icon, size: 15, color: AppColors.primaryLight),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Flexible(
                child: Text(
                  label,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.grey, fontSize: 11),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 7),
          Text(
            value,
            maxLines: 1,
            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              fontSize: 13,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/payment/payment_screen.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:razorpay_flutter/razorpay_flutter.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/gradient_button.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/payment_order_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/payment_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/reservation_provider.dart';

class PaymentScreenArgs {
  const PaymentScreenArgs({
    required this.reservationId,
    required this.amountPaise,
    this.currency = 'INR',
    this.purpose = 'ADVANCE',
  });

  final String reservationId;
  final int amountPaise;
  final String currency;
  final String purpose;

  bool get isSettlement => purpose == 'SETTLEMENT';
}

class PaymentScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const PaymentScreen({super.key, required this.args});

  final PaymentScreenArgs args;

  @override
  ConsumerState<PaymentScreen> createState() => _PaymentScreenState();
}

class _PaymentScreenState extends ConsumerState<PaymentScreen> {
  late final Razorpay _razorpay;
  PaymentOrderModel? _activeOrder;
  bool _isLoading = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _razorpay = Razorpay();
    _razorpay.on(Razorpay.EVENT_PAYMENT_SUCCESS, _handlePaymentSuccess);
    _razorpay.on(Razorpay.EVENT_PAYMENT_ERROR, _handlePaymentError);
    _razorpay.on(Razorpay.EVENT_EXTERNAL_WALLET, _handleExternalWallet);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _razorpay.clear();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _startCheckout() async {
    if (widget.args.amountPaise < 100) {
      _showMessage('Payment amount must be at least ₹1.', isError: true);
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    try {
      final order = await ref.read(paymentRepositoryProvider).createOrder(
            reservationId: widget.args.reservationId,
            amount: widget.args.amountPaise,
            currency: widget.args.currency,
            purpose: widget.args.purpose,
          );
      if (!mounted) return;

      _activeOrder = order;
      final user = ref.read(currentUserProvider);
      final options = <String, dynamic>{
        'key': order.keyId,
        'order_id': order.orderId,
        'amount': order.amount,
        'currency': order.currency,
        'name': 'Smart Parking',
        'description': widget.args.isSettlement
            ? 'Final parking balance'
            : '₹10 reservation advance',
        'prefill': {
          'email': user?.email ?? '',
          'contact': user?.phone ?? '',
        },
        'retry': {'enabled': true, 'max_count': 3},
        'theme': {'color': '#2563EB'},
      };

      setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      _razorpay.open(options);
    } catch (error) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      _showMessage(_friendlyPaymentError(error), isError: true);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handlePaymentSuccess(PaymentSuccessResponse response) async {
    final paymentId = response.paymentId;
    final orderId = response.orderId ?? _activeOrder?.orderId;
    final signature = response.signature;
    if (paymentId == null || orderId == null || signature == null) {
      _showMessage(
        'Razorpay returned incomplete payment details.',
        isError: true,
      );
      return;
    }

    if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    try {
      await ref.read(paymentRepositoryProvider).verifyPayment(
            paymentId: paymentId,
            orderId: orderId,
            signature: signature,
          );
      ref.invalidate(activeReservationProvider);
      ref.invalidate(reservationsListProvider);
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      _showMessage(
        widget.args.isSettlement
            ? 'Final payment verified. Checkout is complete.'
            : '₹10 advance verified. Your booking is confirmed.',
      );
      context.go('/reservations');
    } catch (_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      _showMessage(
        'Payment was received, but confirmation is still pending. Please refresh your booking before trying again.',
        isError: true,
      );
    }
  }

  void _handlePaymentError(PaymentFailureResponse response) {
    if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    final cancelled = response.code == Razorpay.PAYMENT_CANCELLED;
    final cancellationMessage = widget.args.isSettlement
        ? 'Payment cancelled. Checkout remains pending and the slot stays occupied.'
        : 'Payment cancelled. The slot remains held until the 30-minute timer expires.';
    _showMessage(
      cancelled
          ? cancellationMessage
          : 'Payment failed: ${response.message ?? 'Please try again.'}',
      isError: !cancelled,
    );
  }

  void _handleExternalWallet(ExternalWalletResponse response) {
    _showMessage(
      'External wallet selected: ${response.walletName ?? 'wallet'}',
    );
  }

  void _showMessage(String message, {bool isError = false}) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text(message),
        backgroundColor: isError ? AppColors.error : AppColors.success,
      ),
    );
  }

  String _friendlyPaymentError(Object error) {
    final message = error.toString().toLowerCase();
    if (message.contains('not authenticated') ||
        message.contains('unauthorized')) {
      return 'Your session has expired. Please sign in again.';
    }
    if (message.contains('razorpay') || message.contains('payment')) {
      return 'The payment service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again shortly.';
    }
    if (message.contains('connection') || message.contains('timeout')) {
      return 'Unable to connect to the payment service. Check your connection and try again.';
    }
    return 'Unable to start payment. Please try again.';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final amount = widget.args.amountPaise / 100;
    final isSettlement = widget.args.isSettlement;

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(isSettlement ? 'Final Payment' : 'Reservation Advance'),
      ),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 12, 20, 28),
          children: [
            Center(
              child: Container(
                width: 92,
                height: 92,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.12),
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  border: Border.all(
                    color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.4),
                    width: 2,
                  ),
                ),
                child: Icon(
                  isSettlement
                      ? Icons.receipt_long_outlined
                      : Icons.shield_outlined,
                  size: 48,
                  color: AppColors.primaryLight,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 22),
            Text(
              isSettlement
                  ? 'Complete your checkout'
                  : 'Reserve your parking slot',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall?.copyWith(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                  ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(
              isSettlement
                  ? 'The ₹10 advance has already been deducted. The slot becomes vacant only after this payment is verified.'
                  : 'Pay a non-refundable ₹10 advance. You then have 30 minutes to enter and start parking.',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: const TextStyle(
                color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                height: 1.45,
              ),
            ),
            if (!isSettlement) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 24),
              const _PaymentProgress(),
            ],
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
              ),
              child: Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  Text(isSettlement ? 'Balance to pay' : 'Advance to pay'),
                  Text(
                    '₹${amount.toStringAsFixed(2)}',
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 22,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: AppColors.primaryLight,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            GradientButton(
              text: isSettlement
                  ? 'Pay Final Balance Securely'
                  : 'Pay Securely with Razorpay',
              icon: Icons.payment,
              isLoading: _isLoading,
              onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _startCheckout,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 18),
            const Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                Icon(
                  Icons.verified_user_outlined,
                  color: AppColors.success,
                  size: 17,
                ),
                SizedBox(width: 7),
                Text(
                  'Secure payment · Verified by backend',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                    fontSize: 12,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            if (isSettlement) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              TextButton(
                onPressed: _isLoading ? null : () => context.pop(),
                child: const Text('Return to booking'),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _PaymentProgress extends StatelessWidget {
  const _PaymentProgress();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        _step('1', 'Slot', true),
        _line(true),
        _step('2', 'Payment', true),
        _line(false),
        _step('3', 'Confirmed', false),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _line(bool active) {
    return Expanded(
      child: Container(
        height: 2,
        color: active ? AppColors.primary : AppColors.borderDark,
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _step(String number, String label, bool active) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: 68,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 32,
            height: 32,
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: active ? AppColors.primary : Colors.transparent,
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
              border: Border.all(
                color: active ? AppColors.primary : AppColors.borderDark,
              ),
            ),
            child: Text(
              number,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: active ? Colors.white : AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 6),
          Text(
            label,
            style: const TextStyle(
              color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
              fontSize: 11,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/profile/history_screen.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_animate/flutter_animate.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/reservation_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/reservation/widgets/reservation_card.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/shimmer_loading.dart';

class HistoryScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const HistoryScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<HistoryScreen> createState() => _HistoryScreenState();
}

class _HistoryScreenState extends ConsumerState<HistoryScreen>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late TabController _tabController;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tabController = TabController(length: 3, vsync: this);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tabController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final historyAsync = ref.watch(reservationsListProvider);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Booking History'),
        centerTitle: true,
        bottom: TabBar(
          controller: _tabController,
          indicatorColor: AppColors.primary,
          labelColor: AppColors.primary,
          unselectedLabelColor: Colors.grey,
          tabs: const [
            Tab(text: 'All'),
            Tab(text: 'Completed'),
            Tab(text: 'Cancelled'),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: historyAsync.when(
        data: (reservations) {
          final completed = reservations
              .where((r) => r.status.toUpperCase() == 'COMPLETED')
              .toList();
          final cancelled = reservations
              .where((r) => r.status.toUpperCase() == 'CANCELLED')
              .toList();

          return TabBarView(
            controller: _tabController,
            children: [
              _buildList(reservations),
              _buildList(completed),
              _buildList(cancelled),
            ],
          );
        },
        loading: () => ListView.builder(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
          itemCount: 5,
          itemBuilder: (context, index) => const Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16.0),
            child: ShimmerLoading(width: double.infinity, height: 120),
          ),
        ),
        error: (e, _) => Center(child: Text('Error: $e')),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildList(List reservations) {
    if (reservations.isEmpty) {
      return Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.history, size: 80, color: Colors.grey.withOpacity(0.3)),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            const Text(
              'No history found',
              style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 18,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: Colors.grey),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ).animate().fadeIn();
    }

    return ListView.builder(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      itemCount: reservations.length,
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        return ReservationCard(reservation: reservations[index])
            .animate()
            .fadeIn(delay: Duration(milliseconds: 50 * index))
            .slideX(begin: 0.1, end: 0);
      },
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/profile/profile_screen.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:flutter_animate/flutter_animate.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/auth_provider.dart';

class ProfileScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
  const ProfileScreen({super.key});

  void _logout(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) async {
    final confirm = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Logout'),
        content: const Text('Are you sure you want to log out?'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child:
                const Text('Logout', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.error)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    if (confirm == true) {
      await ref.read(authStateProvider.notifier).logout();
      if (context.mounted) {
        context.go('/login');
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final user = ref.watch(currentUserProvider);
    final displayName = user?.fullName.trim();
    final name =
        displayName == null || displayName.isEmpty ? 'User' : displayName;
    final email = user?.email.trim();
    final avatarUrl = user?.avatarUrl?.trim();
    final hasAvatar = avatarUrl != null && avatarUrl.isNotEmpty;
    final initial = name.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase();

    return Scaffold(
      body: CustomScrollView(
        slivers: [
          SliverAppBar(
            expandedHeight: 250,
            pinned: true,
            flexibleSpace: FlexibleSpaceBar(
              background: Container(
                decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                  gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
                ),
                child: SafeArea(
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          shape: BoxShape.circle,
                          border: Border.all(color: Colors.white, width: 2),
                        ),
                        child: CircleAvatar(
                          radius: 40,
                          backgroundColor: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                          backgroundImage:
                              hasAvatar ? NetworkImage(avatarUrl!) : null,
                          child: !hasAvatar
                              ? Text(
                                  initial,
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                      fontSize: 32, color: Colors.white),
                                )
                              : null,
                        ),
                      )
                          .animate()
                          .scale(duration: 400.ms, curve: Curves.easeOutBack),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Text(
                        name,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 24,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontFamily: 'Outfit',
                        ),
                      ).animate().fadeIn(delay: 100.ms),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(
                        email == null || email.isEmpty
                            ? 'Email unavailable'
                            : email,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 14,
                          color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.8),
                        ),
                      ).animate().fadeIn(delay: 200.ms),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          SliverToBoxAdapter(
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20.0),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  // Menu Items
                  _buildMenuSection(context, [
                    _buildMenuItem(
                      context,
                      title: 'Booking History',
                      icon: Icons.history,
                      onTap: () => context.push('/profile/history'),
                    ),
                    _buildMenuItem(
                      context,
                      title: 'Notifications',
                      icon: Icons.notifications_outlined,
                      onTap: () => context.push('/notifications'),
                    ),
                    _buildMenuItem(
                      context,
                      title: 'Settings',
                      icon: Icons.settings_outlined,
                      onTap: () => context.push('/profile/settings'),
                    ),
                  ])
                      .animate()
                      .slideY(begin: 0.1, end: 0, delay: 400.ms)
                      .fadeIn(),

                  const SizedBox(height: 24),

                  _buildMenuSection(context, [
                    _buildMenuItem(
                      context,
                      title: 'Logout',
                      icon: Icons.logout,
                      textColor: AppColors.error,
                      iconColor: AppColors.error,
                      onTap: () => _logout(context, ref),
                    ),
                  ])
                      .animate()
                      .slideY(begin: 0.1, end: 0, delay: 600.ms)
                      .fadeIn(),

                  const SizedBox(height: 40),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMenuSection(BuildContext context, List<Widget> children) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05),
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: children,
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMenuItem(
    BuildContext context, {
    required String title,
    required IconData icon,
    required VoidCallback onTap,
    Color? textColor,
    Color? iconColor,
  }) {
    return ListTile(
      onTap: onTap,
      leading: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: (iconColor ?? AppColors.primary).withOpacity(0.1),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
        ),
        child: Icon(icon, color: iconColor ?? AppColors.primary, size: 20),
      ),
      title: Text(
        title,
        style: TextStyle(
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
          color: textColor ?? Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge?.color,
        ),
      ),
      trailing:
          const Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios, size: 16, color: Colors.grey),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/profile/settings_screen.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/theme_provider.dart';

class SettingsScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
  const SettingsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final themeMode = ref.watch(themeProvider);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Settings'),
        centerTitle: true,
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
        children: [
          _buildSectionHeader('Appearance'),
          _buildSettingTile(
            context: context,
            title: 'Dark Mode',
            icon: Icons.dark_mode_outlined,
            trailing: Switch(
              value: themeMode == ThemeMode.dark,
              onChanged: (val) {
                ref.read(themeProvider.notifier).toggle();
              },
              activeColor: AppColors.primary,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 28),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(18),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
              border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
            ),
            child: const Row(
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.info_outline_rounded, color: AppColors.primary),
                SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    'Only settings that are fully available are shown here.',
                    style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 40),
          const Center(
            child: Text(
              'App Version 1.0.0',
              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey, fontSize: 12),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSectionHeader(String title) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16, left: 8),
      child: Text(
        title.toUpperCase(),
        style: const TextStyle(
          fontSize: 12,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
          color: Colors.grey,
          letterSpacing: 1.2,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSettingTile({
    required BuildContext context,
    required String title,
    required IconData icon,
    Widget? trailing,
  }) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.02),
            blurRadius: 5,
            offset: const Offset(0, 2),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: ListTile(
        leading: Icon(icon, color: AppColors.primary),
        title: Text(
          title,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
            color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge?.color,
          ),
        ),
        trailing: trailing,
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/reservation/reservation_screen.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_animate/flutter_animate.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_constants.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/utils/extensions.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/gradient_button.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/widgets/shimmer_loading.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/data/models/reservation_model.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/domain/providers/reservation_provider.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/payment/payment_screen.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/reservation/widgets/countdown_timer.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/reservation/widgets/parking_elapsed_timer.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/ui/reservation/widgets/reservation_card.dart';

class ReservationScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const ReservationScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<ReservationScreen> createState() => _ReservationScreenState();
}

class _ReservationScreenState extends ConsumerState<ReservationScreen> {
  bool _isActionLoading = false;

  Future<void> _onRefresh() async {
    ref.invalidate(activeReservationProvider);
    ref.invalidate(reservationsListProvider);
  }

  Future<void> _cancelReservation(String id) async {
    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Cancel Reservation'),
        content: const Text(
          'Are you sure? Any ₹10 advance already paid is non-refundable.',
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text('No'),
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child: const Text(
              'Yes, Cancel',
              style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.error),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    if (confirmed != true) return;

    try {
      await ref.read(reservationRepositoryProvider).cancelReservation(id);
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Reservation cancelled')),
      );
      await _onRefresh();
    } catch (_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: const Text(
              'Unable to cancel this reservation. Please try again.'),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _beginCheckout(ReservationModel reservation) async {
    if (_isActionLoading) return;
    setState(() => _isActionLoading = true);

    try {
      final checkout = await ref
          .read(reservationRepositoryProvider)
          .checkOut(reservation.id);
      final totalAmount = checkout.totalAmount ?? 20;
      final amountDuePaise = ((totalAmount - 10) * 100).round();

      ref.invalidate(activeReservationProvider);
      ref.invalidate(reservationsListProvider);
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isActionLoading = false);

      await context.push(
        '/payment',
        extra: PaymentScreenArgs(
          reservationId: checkout.id,
          amountPaise: amountDuePaise,
          purpose: 'SETTLEMENT',
        ),
      );
      if (!mounted) return;
      await _onRefresh();
    } catch (_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isActionLoading = false);
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: const Text('Unable to complete checkout. Please try again.'),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final activeReservation = ref.watch(activeReservationProvider);
    final reservationHistory = ref.watch(reservationsListProvider);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('My Bookings'),
        centerTitle: true,
      ),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: _onRefresh,
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
          children: [
            activeReservation.when(
              data: (reservation) {
                if (reservation == null) {
                  return _buildEmptyState().animate().fadeIn();
                }

                switch (reservation.status) {
                  case 'PAYMENT_PENDING':
                    return _buildAdvancePayment(reservation).animate().fadeIn();
                  case 'PENDING':
                    return _buildWaitingForEntry(reservation)
                        .animate()
                        .fadeIn();
                  case 'ACTIVE':
                    return _buildActiveParking(reservation).animate().fadeIn();
                  case 'CHECKOUT_PAYMENT_PENDING':
                    return _buildCheckoutPayment(reservation)
                        .animate()
                        .fadeIn();
                  default:
                    return _buildEmptyState().animate().fadeIn();
                }
              },
              loading: () => Column(
                children: [
                  const SizedBox(height: 20),
                  ShimmerLoading.card(height: 200),
                  const SizedBox(height: 20),
                  ShimmerLoading.card(height: 300),
                ],
              ),
              error: (error, _) => _buildLoadError(
                'Unable to load your active booking.',
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 40),
            Text(
              'Past Bookings',
              style: context.textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            reservationHistory.when(
              data: (history) {
                final past = history.where((reservation) {
                  return reservation.status != 'ACTIVE' &&
                      reservation.status != 'PENDING' &&
                      reservation.status != 'PAYMENT_PENDING' &&
                      reservation.status != 'CHECKOUT_PAYMENT_PENDING';
                }).toList();

                if (past.isEmpty) {
                  return const Padding(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.all(32),
                    child: Center(
                      child: Text(
                        'No past bookings',
                        style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey),
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                }

                return ListView.builder(
                  shrinkWrap: true,
                  physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                  itemCount: past.length,
                  itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                    return ReservationCard(reservation: past[index])
                        .animate()
                        .fadeIn(delay: Duration(milliseconds: 100 * index))
                        .slideX(begin: 0.1, end: 0);
                  },
                );
              },
              loading: () => ListView.builder(
                shrinkWrap: true,
                physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                itemCount: 3,
                itemBuilder: (context, index) => const Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
                  child: ShimmerLoading(width: double.infinity, height: 120),
                ),
              ),
              error: (error, _) => _buildLoadError(
                'Unable to load past bookings.',
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  DateTime _entryDeadline(ReservationModel reservation) {
    final reservedAt = reservation.createdAt ?? reservation.startTime;
    return reservedAt.add(
      const Duration(minutes: AppConstants.reservationTimeoutMinutes),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAdvancePayment(ReservationModel reservation) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        _statusIcon(
          icon: Icons.lock_outline_rounded,
          color: AppColors.primary,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        Text(
          'Complete Your Reservation',
          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          style: context.textTheme.headlineSmall?.copyWith(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        const Text(
          'Pay the ₹10 advance to confirm the selected slot.',
          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 22),
        _bookingSummaryCard(reservation),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        CountdownTimer(
          targetTime: _entryDeadline(reservation),
          onExpire: _onRefresh,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 22),
        GradientButton(
          text: 'Pay ₹10 Advance',
          icon: Icons.payment,
          onPressed: () => context.push(
            '/payment',
            extra: PaymentScreenArgs(
              reservationId: reservation.id,
              amountPaise: 1000,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => _cancelReservation(reservation.id),
          child: const Text(
            'Cancel Reservation',
            style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.error),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildWaitingForEntry(ReservationModel reservation) {
    final slotLabel = _slotLabel(reservation);
    return Column(
      children: [
        _statusIcon(
          icon: Icons.check_rounded,
          color: AppColors.success,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        Text(
          'Booking Confirmed',
          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          style: context.textTheme.headlineSmall?.copyWith(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        const Text(
          'Your ₹10 advance is paid. Enter before the hold expires.',
          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 22),
        _bookingSummaryCard(reservation),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        CountdownTimer(
          targetTime: _entryDeadline(reservation),
          onExpire: _onRefresh,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 22),
        GradientButton(
          text: 'Navigate to Slot $slotLabel',
          icon: Icons.navigation_rounded,
          onPressed: () => context.push(
            '/reservations/navigate',
            extra: reservation,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => _cancelReservation(reservation.id),
          child: const Text(
            'Cancel (₹10 advance is non-refundable)',
            style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.error),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActiveParking(ReservationModel reservation) {
    final checkInTime = reservation.actualCheckIn;
    if (checkInTime == null) {
      return const Center(child: Text('Waiting for check-in time...'));
    }

    return Column(
      children: [
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: AppColors.success.withOpacity(0.12),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            border: Border.all(color: AppColors.success.withOpacity(0.4)),
          ),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              const Icon(
                Icons.check_circle_rounded,
                color: AppColors.success,
                size: 18,
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 7),
              Text(
                'Vehicle checked in · ${_slotLabel(reservation)}',
                style: const TextStyle(
                  color: AppColors.success,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        _bookingSummaryCard(reservation),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        ParkingElapsedTimer(checkInTime: checkInTime),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        GradientButton(
          text: 'End Parking & Pay',
          icon: Icons.exit_to_app,
          isLoading: _isActionLoading,
          onPressed:
              _isActionLoading ? null : () => _beginCheckout(reservation),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCheckoutPayment(ReservationModel reservation) {
    final total = reservation.totalAmount ?? 20;
    final balancePaise = ((total - 10) * 100).round();

    return Column(
      children: [
        _statusIcon(
          icon: Icons.payments_outlined,
          color: AppColors.warning,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        Text(
          'Complete Your Checkout',
          style: context.textTheme.headlineSmall?.copyWith(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        const Text(
          'The parking timer has stopped.',
          style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        Card(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            child: Column(
              children: [
                _AmountRow(label: 'Total parking fee', amount: total),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                const _AmountRow(label: 'Advance paid', amount: -10),
                const Divider(height: 28),
                _AmountRow(
                  label: 'Balance to pay',
                  amount: total - 10,
                  emphasize: true,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const Text(
          'The slot remains occupied until the final payment is verified.',
          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 24),
        GradientButton(
          text: 'Pay Final Balance',
          icon: Icons.payment,
          onPressed: () => context.push(
            '/payment',
            extra: PaymentScreenArgs(
              reservationId: reservation.id,
              amountPaise: balancePaise,
              purpose: 'SETTLEMENT',
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildEmptyState() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 40),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          Icon(
            Icons.directions_car,
            size: 80,
            color: Colors.grey.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          const Text(
            'No Active Reservations',
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          const Text(
            'You don\'t have any active parking reservations at the moment.',
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 32),
          GradientButton(
            text: 'Find Parking',
            icon: Icons.search,
            onPressed: () => context.go('/search'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _bookingSummaryCard(ReservationModel reservation) {
    final floor = reservation.slotDetails?.floor ?? 1;
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 54,
            height: 54,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.14),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
            ),
            child: const Icon(
              Icons.local_parking_rounded,
              color: AppColors.primaryLight,
              size: 30,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 14),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  reservation.areaName ?? 'Smart Parking',
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    fontSize: 16,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 5),
                Text(
                  'Slot ${_slotLabel(reservation)} · Floor $floor',
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _statusIcon({required IconData icon, required Color color}) {
    return Container(
      width: 82,
      height: 82,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.45), width: 2),
      ),
      child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 44),
    );
  }

  String _slotLabel(ReservationModel reservation) {
    return reservation.slotDetails?.slotId ?? reservation.slotId;
  }

  Widget _buildLoadError(String message) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          const Icon(Icons.cloud_off_rounded, color: AppColors.warning),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          Text(message, textAlign: TextAlign.center),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          OutlinedButton.icon(
            onPressed: _onRefresh,
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded),
            label: const Text('Try Again'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _AmountRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const _AmountRow({
    required this.label,
    required this.amount,
    this.emphasize = false,
  });

  final String label;
  final double amount;
  final bool emphasize;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final prefix = amount < 0 ? '-' : '';
    final style = TextStyle(
      fontWeight: emphasize ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
      color: emphasize ? AppColors.primary : null,
      fontSize: emphasize ? 18 : null,
    );

    return Row(
      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
      children: [
        Text(label, style: style),
        Text(
          '$prefix₹${amount.abs().toStringAsFixed(2)}',
          style: style,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/reservation/widgets/countdown_timer.dart =====
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:percent_indicator/circular_percent_indicator.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_constants.dart';

class CountdownTimer extends StatefulWidget {
  final DateTime targetTime;
  final VoidCallback? onExpire;

  const CountdownTimer({
    super.key,
    required this.targetTime,
    this.onExpire,
  });

  @override
  State<CountdownTimer> createState() => _CountdownTimerState();
}

class _CountdownTimerState extends State<CountdownTimer> {
  Timer? _timer;
  Duration _remaining = Duration.zero;
  final Duration _totalDuration = const Duration(
    minutes: AppConstants.reservationTimeoutMinutes,
  );

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _updateTime();
    if (_remaining > Duration.zero) {
      _timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (_) => _updateTime());
    }
  }

  void _updateTime() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    setState(() {
      _remaining = widget.targetTime.difference(now);
      if (_remaining.isNegative) {
        _remaining = Duration.zero;
        _timer?.cancel();
        widget.onExpire?.call();
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final percentage =
        (_remaining.inSeconds / _totalDuration.inSeconds).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    final color = _remaining.inMinutes > 5
        ? AppColors.success
        : (_remaining.inMinutes > 2 ? AppColors.warning : AppColors.error);

    final minutes = _remaining.inMinutes.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
    final seconds = (_remaining.inSeconds % 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0');

    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 18),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          CircularPercentIndicator(
            radius: 42.0,
            lineWidth: 8.0,
            percent: percentage,
            center: Icon(Icons.timer_outlined, color: color, size: 28),
            progressColor: color,
            backgroundColor: color.withOpacity(0.2),
            circularStrokeCap: CircularStrokeCap.round,
            animation: true,
            animateFromLastPercent: true,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 18),
          Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                '$minutes:$seconds',
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 30,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                  color: color,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 3),
              const Text(
                'Entry time remaining',
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 12,
                  color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


===== user_app/lib/ui/reservation/widgets/parking_elapsed_timer.dart =====
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:smart_parking_user/core/constants/app_colors.dart';

class ParkingElapsedTimer extends StatefulWidget {
  const ParkingElapsedTimer({
    super.key,
    required this.checkInTime,
  });

  final DateTime checkInTime;

  @override
  State<ParkingElapsedTimer> createState() => _ParkingElapsedTimerState();
}

class _ParkingElapsedTimerState extends State<ParkingElapsedTimer> {
  Timer? _timer;
  int _elapsedSeconds = 1;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _updateElapsedTime();
    _timer = Timer.periodic(
      const Duration(seconds: 1),
      (_) => _updateElapsedTime(),
    );
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant ParkingElapsedTimer oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (oldWidget.checkInTime != widget.checkInTime) {
      _updateElapsedTime();
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _updateElapsedTime() {
    final seconds = DateTime.now().difference(widget.checkInTime).inSeconds;
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() => _elapsedSeconds = seconds < 1 ? 1 : seconds);
  }

  int get _totalFee {
    if (_elapsedSeconds <= 3600) return 20;
    final extraStartedHours = (_elapsedSeconds - 3600 + 3599) ~/ 3600;
    return 20 + (extraStartedHours * 5);
  }

  String get _formattedElapsed {
    final hours = _elapsedSeconds ~/ 3600;
    final minutes = (_elapsedSeconds % 3600) ~/ 60;
    final seconds = _elapsedSeconds % 60;
    return '${hours.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:'
        '${minutes.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:'
        '${seconds.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final checkoutBalance = _totalFee - 10;

    return Column(
      children: [
        Container(
          width: double.infinity,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 26),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: AppColors.success.withOpacity(0.08),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            border: Border.all(color: AppColors.success.withOpacity(0.45)),
          ),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              Text(
                _formattedElapsed,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 42,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                  letterSpacing: 2,
                  color: AppColors.success,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              const Text(
                'Parking time',
                style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 14),
        Container(
          width: double.infinity,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(18),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
            border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
          ),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              const _FeeRow(label: 'First hour', amount: 20),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              const _FeeRow(
                  label: 'Each started extra hour', amount: 5, prefix: '+'),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              _FeeRow(label: 'Current parking fee', amount: _totalFee),
              const Divider(height: 26),
              const _FeeRow(label: 'Advance paid', amount: -10),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.success.withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                ),
                child: _FeeRow(
                  label: 'Current balance',
                  amount: checkoutBalance,
                  emphasize: true,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              const Text(
                '₹20 covers the first hour. Each started extra hour adds ₹5.',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey, fontSize: 12),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _FeeRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const _FeeRow({
    required this.label,
    required this.amount,
    this.emphasize = false,
    this.prefix = '',
  });

  final String label;
